### [1] [SPORT: Spherical-PSNR-Optimized tRuncaTion for Power-Efficient 360-Degree Video Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24916)
*Md. Sajjad Hossain,Hasibur Rahman Hemel,Kyle Mooney,Yiwen Xu,William Oswald,Mario Renteria-Pinon,Hritom Das,Zhenlin Pei,Jinhui Wang,Na Gong*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出SPORT框架，通过位截断技术减少VR头显中显示路径的内存功耗，在保证视觉质量的前提下实现了约50%的内存功耗和带宽节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在独立VR头显中，内存带宽占总功耗的30-40%，但现有系统通常以统一分辨率存储整个360度帧，而忽略了用户注视点位置，造成功耗浪费。

Method: 开发了SPORT位截断框架，仅对用户视野(FoV)外的像素存储最高有效位；采用加权球面均匀PSNR(WS-PSNR)直接作为优化约束，消除了传统PSNR用于WS-PSNR质量目标时的度量不一致性；设计了注视预测性瓦片分类来补偿9.33ms的端到端流水线延迟。

Result: SPORT-B变体（保持FoV无损）实现了47.9%的内存功耗节省和47.9%的带宽减少，在注视区域保持SSIM=1.000；自适应变体SPORT-A达到51.6%功耗节省，比基于PSNR的优化器高3.1个百分点；注视预测瓦片分类将边界误分类减少5.2个百分点；在SkyWater 130nm CMOS工艺的ASIC上验证，软硬件结果字节级精确匹配。

Conclusion: SPORT框架成功实现了显著的内存功耗和带宽节省，同时满足所有WS-PSNR阈值要求并保持注视区域的完美图像质量，总运动到光子延迟9.33ms满足VR舒适预算要求并有53.3%的安全余量。

Abstract: Memory bandwidth accounts for 30-40% of total power consumption in standalone virtual reality (VR) headsets, yet existing systems typically store the entire 360-degree frame at a uniform resolution regardless of viewer gaze. This paper presents SPORT (Spherical-PSNR Optimized tRuncaTion), a bit-truncation framework that reduces display-path memory power by storing only the most significant bits of pixels outside the user's field of view (FoV). Specifically, a new bit-truncation framework is developed to use weighted-to-spherically-uniform PSNR (WS-PSNR) directly in the optimization constraint, eliminating the metric inconsistency that arises when standard PSNR is used for a WS-PSNR quality target. Also, gaze-predictive tile classification compensates for the 9.33 ms end-to-end pipeline latency, reducing boundary misclassifications by 5.2 percentage points at a cost of only 0.01 ms. In addition, the developed SPORT-B variant, which keeps the FoV lossless, achieves 47.9% memory power saving and 47.9% bandwidth reduction across different 4K video sequences while satisfying all three per-region WS-PSNR thresholds and maintaining SSIM = 1.000 in the attended region. The full adaptive variant SPORT-A reaches 51.6% power saving, 3.1percentage points more than a PSNR-based optimizer at equal measured quality. SPORT is validated on the TrunMEM360 flexible SRAM Application-Specific Integrated Circuit (ASIC) fabricated in SkyWater 130 nm CMOS, confirming byte-exact silicon-software agreement, with WS-PSNR and SSIM matching within 0.1 dB and 0.001. CACTI-based analysis confirms 48.72% DRAM leakage reduction and 36.4%/36.7% read/write energy reduction. The total motion-to-photon latency of 9.33 ms satisfies the 20 ms VR comfort budget with a 53.3% safety margin.

</details>


### [2] [SafeGen: LLM-Driven Assertion Generation and Fault Criticality Evaluation for Functional Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25296)
*Xuanyi Tan,Arjun Chaudhuri,Rubin Parekhji,Krishnendu Chakrabarty*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了SafeGen框架，利用LLM和文档级超知识图谱(HyperKG)进行功能安全导向的故障关键性评估，结合形式化验证，相比传统方法生成更高质量的断言并提供更好的语义可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自动驾驶和电动汽车技术的发展，功能安全已成为汽车芯片设计的关键要求。传统的基于仿真的故障分析在模块级别往往过于保守，无法准确反映故障的关键性，因此需要更精确的评估方法。

Method: SafeGen框架利用大语言模型(LLM)和结合FMEDA指导原则的文档级超知识图谱(HyperKG)从设计和安全文档中提取可验证的规范。HyperKG结合RTL信息指导生成语义上基于规范且设计感知的功能安全断言(FSA)。采用支持stuck-at和桥接故障的门级到RTL故障映射机制，结合形式化属性验证(FPV)，基于规范关联的断言违规进行语义级别的故障关键性分级。

Result: 在面向场向控制(FOC)系统的数字-物理协同仿真平台上验证了SafeGen。实验结果表明，与现有基于LLM的断言生成框架相比，SafeGen生成更高质量的断言，并且与传统仿真方法相比在故障关键性评估中提供更好的语义可解释性。

Conclusion: SafeGen成功实现了功能安全导向的故障关键性评估，通过LLM和形式化验证相结合的方法，克服了传统仿真方法的保守性问题，为汽车芯片设计提供了更准确和可解释的安全评估解决方案。

Abstract: With advances in autonomous driving and electric vehicle technologies, functional safety has become a critical requirement in automotive chip design. Traditional simulation-based fault analysis is often overly conservative at the module level and fails to accurately reflect fault criticality. This paper presents SafeGen, an LLM-driven, formal-verification-assisted framework for functional-safety-oriented fault criticality assessment. SafeGen leverages large language models (LLMs) and a document-level Hyper Knowledge Graph (HyperKG) that incorporates Failure Modes, Effects, and Diagnostic Analysis (FMEDA) guidelines to extract verifiable specifications from design and safety documents and evaluate their relevance to overall system safety. The HyperKG is further enriched with register-transfer-level (RTL) information to guide the generation of Functional Safety Assertions (FSAs) that are both semantically grounded and design-aware. Each assertion is linked to its corresponding specification, enabling traceable reasoning throughout the assessment process. A gate-to-RTL fault-mapping mechanism supporting both stuck-at and bridging faults, combined with formal property verification (FPV), enables semantic-level fault criticality grading based on specification-linked assertion violations. A digital-physical co-simulation platform for a field-oriented control (FOC) system is developed to validate SafeGen. Experimental results demonstrate that SafeGen generates higher-quality assertions than existing LLM-based assertion generation frameworks while providing greater semantic interpretability in fault criticality assessment compared with traditional simulation-based approaches.

</details>


### [3] [Cache-Resident LLM Inference in GB-Scale Last-Level Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25353)
*Wanning Zhang,Tongzhou Gu,Marco Canini,Ceyu Xu,Jian Weng*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向分层内存集群系统的缓存驻留执行模型，通过将权重相关算子与注意力机制/KV缓存管理解耦到专用资源域，并放宽同步粒度至子算子依赖，实现了在GB级末级缓存CPU上的高效LLM推理，相比llama.cpp获得最高11.51倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型推理的主要瓶颈已转变为内存层级间的数据移动。现代服务器CPU中的3D堆叠缓存技术提供了GB级末级缓存，使得可复用的模型权重能够保持在片上。然而实现这一目标面临挑战：更深的流水线会增加在途请求和KV缓存占用，而缓存驻留算子使算子边界同步成为可见的瓶颈。

Method: 提出缓存驻留执行模型，核心方法包括：(1)将权重中心算子与注意力/KV缓存管理分离到独立的资源域，保持可复用权重缓存驻留的同时独立扩展KV容量；(2)将同步粒度从算子边界放宽到真正的子算子依赖，减少缓存驻留场景下的协调开销；(3)在多插槽CPU集群上实现权重-注意力解耦架构、局部性感知放置和专用静态运行时。

Result: 在Llama-3.2-3B和Llama-2-7B配置下，相比同等配置的llama.cpp实现了2.04x-11.51x的TPOT加速；在验证的分析模型下，跨不同模型大小、上下文长度和批处理规模可获得高达13.9x的TPOT加速。

Conclusion: 研究表明，当执行围绕缓存驻留、解耦状态管理和依赖感知协调进行组织时，配备GB级末级缓存的商用CPU能够支持高效的大语言模型推理，为解决LLM推理中的数据移动瓶颈提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) inference is increasingly dominated by data movement across the memory hierarchy. Recent 3D-stacked cache technologies have enabled GB-scale last-level caches in modern server CPUs, making it possible to keep reusable model weights on chip and exploit cache bandwidth and latency. Achieving this regime is not straightforward: deeper pipelining for weight residency increases in-flight requests and KV-cache footprint, while cache-resident operators make operator-boundary synchronization a visible bottleneck.
  We present a cache-resident execution model for inference on hierarchical-memory clustered systems. The model separates weight-centric operators from attention and KV-cache management into dedicated resource domains, keeping reusable weights cache-resident while scaling KV capacity independently of pipeline depth. It also relaxes synchronization from operator boundaries to true sub-operator dependencies, reducing coordination overhead in the cache-resident regime.
  We instantiate this model on a multi-socket CPU cluster with a weight-attention decoupled architecture, locality-aware placement, and a specialized static runtime. The prototype substantially outperforms equally provisioned llama.cpp. On deployed Llama-3.2-3B and Llama-2-7B configurations, it achieves 2.04x-11.51x speedup on time-per-output-token (TPOT). Under a validated analytical model, it further reaches up to 13.9x TPOT speedup across model sizes, context lengths, and batch sizes. These results show that commodity CPUs with GB-scale last-level caches can support efficient LLM inference when execution is organized around cache residency, decoupled state management, and dependency-aware coordination.

</details>


### [4] [Energy-Efficient CNN Acceleration with MSDF Digit-Serial Arithmetic on FPGA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25562)
*Muhammad Usman,Yousef Sadegheih,Dorit Merhof*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出一种用于U-Net图像分割的FPGA加速器，通过融合乘加操作的MMA架构解决了数字串行算术的延迟累积问题，实现高达15.14 GOPS/W的能效，相比CPU提升约8倍，相比传统MSDF实现降低9倍能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字串行算术（特别是MSDF技术）虽然具有紧凑的硬件面积优势，但在级联操作中存在初始延迟累积问题——乘法后的加法操作各自引入启动开销，导致整体延迟增加，限制了在资源受限边缘设备上的应用效率。

Method: 提出合并乘加（MMA）架构，将乘法和加法操作融合为统一流水线，使每次迭代仅产生单一精简延迟而非传统级联单元的组合延迟。MMA单元设计为并行处理空间输入深度，从而提升吞吐量和硬件效率。

Result: 在U-Net上的评估表明，尽管运行频率低于CPU，该FPGA加速器实现了15.14 GOPS/W的能效（CPU仅为1.93 GOPS/W），能效提升近一个数量级；与基于MSDF的FPGA实现相比，能耗降低约9倍。

Conclusion: 融合算术方法有效解决了数字串行算术的延迟累积问题，在资源受限、延迟敏感的医疗成像和计算机视觉边缘应用中展现出显著的能效优势，为FPGA加速神经网络推理提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents an energy-efficient hardware acceleration of the convolutional layers in the U-Net architecture for image segmentation, implemented on FPGA. While digit-serial arithmetic, particularly most-significant-digit-first (MSDF) techniques, offers a compact hardware footprint, it suffers from initial latency before producing the first output digit. This delay accumulates in cascaded operations like multiplication followed by addition, where each unit introduces its own startup overhead. To overcome this, we propose a merged multiply-add (MMA) architecture that fuses these operations into a unified pipeline. Instead of incurring separate delays, the MMA introduces a single streamlined latency per iteration, shorter than the combined latency of conventional cascaded units, resulting in enhanced throughput and efficiency. The MMA units are designed to process spatial input depths in parallel, achieving significantly higher performance than both standalone MSDF-based and conventional designs. We evaluate the proposed design using U-Net as a target application. Despite operating at a lower frequency than a CPU, the FPGA-based accelerator achieves up to an order of magnitude higher energy efficiency, delivering up to $15.14$ GOPS/W compared to $1.93$ GOPS/W for CPU-based inference. The design also shows approximately $9\times$ reduction in energy consumption compared to MSDF-based FPGA implementations. These results highlight the efficacy of the merged arithmetic approach for resource-constrained, latency-sensitive edge applications in medical imaging and computer vision.

</details>


### [5] [Croc: Training the Next Generation Chip Designers on Domain-Specific End-to-End Open Source Silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25673)
*Enrico Zelioli,Philippe Sauter,Thomas Benz,Hannah Pochert,Luisa Wüthrich,Beat Muheim,Frank K. Gürkaynak,Luca Benini*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了基于开源RISC-V平台Croc的端到端SoC设计制造流程，在65名学生参与的课程中成功制造了5款芯片，验证了开源工具链和PDK的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI、机器人及汽车系统对领域专用SoC需求的增长，亟需培养具备从架构规范到流片实现全流程VLSI设计能力的工程师。然而现有VLSI课程依赖限制性许可的EDA工具、PDK及闭源硬件设计，严重制约了教学与实践。

Method: 构建了围绕Croc可定制RISC-V平台的端到端开源领域专用SoC设计与制造流程。该平台采用开源SystemVerilog IP模块，集成130nm开源PDK的端到端开源设计流程，支持指令集扩展、加速协处理器、外设等多种领域定制选项。

Result: 在首次开源课程实践中，65名学生完成33个项目，30个产出可制造版图，18个设计入选流片候选，最终5个设计成功制造。首个基线芯片已在硅上成功表征，展示了与闭源工具链和PDK实现的同等功能复杂度产品相当的微控制器级功能与实现指标。

Conclusion: 开源工具链、PDK与硬件设计能够支撑完整的领域专用SoC教学与实践，产出的芯片功能与性能指标可达工业级水平，为VLSI教育提供了可行的开源解决方案。

Abstract: The demand for domain-specific systems-on-chip (SoCs) in artificial intelligence, robotics, and automotive systems is increasing the need for engineers with hands-on expertise on very-large-scale integration (VLSI) design from architecture specification to fabricated silicon. Yet, most VLSI courses rely on restrictively licensed electronic design automation tools and process design kits (PDKs), as well as closed-source hardware designs. We present an end-to-end open-source domain-specific SoC design and fabrication flow built around Croc, a highly customizable RISC-V platform. Built from open-source SystemVerilog intellectual property blocks and integrated with an end-to-end open-source design flow in a 130nm open PDK, Croc enables tapeout projects supporting multiple domain customization options: instruction-set extensions, accelerator co-processors, and peripherals. In our first open-source course experience using Croc, 65 students completed 33 projects, 30 of which produced manufacturable layouts. 18 designs were selected as tapeout candidates, and five were fabricated. A first baseline chip has already been successfully characterized in silicon, demonstrating microcontroller-class functionality and implementation metrics comparable to those of products with similar functional complexity completed with closed-source toolchains and PDKs.

</details>

### [6] [Lightweight PCGAE-Net: Parallel CrossGate Attention and Bottleneck AutoEncoder for Efficient 5G Channel Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25401)
*Uma Kishore Godavarti,K. Giridhar,Vanani Prince Dharmendrabhai,Anchit Panday,Madhan Raj Kanagarathinam*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出Lightweight PCGAE-Net，通过修正CS3T-UNet的两个架构缺陷（时序注意力顺序偏差和未压缩瓶颈），在参数减少58%的情况下实现CSI预测性能提升高达6.0 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度Transformer类CSI预测模型（如CS3T-UNet）在5G大规模MIMO系统基站硬件部署面临挑战，主要原因是模型参数量庞大（超过30M），亟需轻量化解决方案。

Method: 提出两个核心改进：（1）通过路由两个注意力模块至同一层归一化输入并使用学习的CrossGate融合独立输出，消除时序注意力的顺序依赖偏差；（2）引入瓶颈自编码器（BAE），使用1×1卷积将最深层通道数减半并添加辅助重构损失防止信息崩塌；整体采用更浅的编码器-解码器结构与频域降维（Nf=32, C=48）。

Result: 模型参数仅8.54M，比CS3T-UNet基线减少58%；在QuaDriGa数据集单步预测任务上，5 km/h速度下性能提升最高3.26 dB，9 km/h速度下提升最高6.0 dB。

Conclusion: 通过修正架构设计缺陷而非后压缩方法，成功实现了高精度CSI预测模型的轻量化，证明了针对性架构优化在模型效率与性能权衡中的有效性，为基站硬件部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Accurate channel state information (CSI) prediction is essential for proactive beamforming and resource management in 5G massive MIMO systems, yet the deployment of high-accuracy transformer-based predictors on base-station hardware remains challenging because the most capable models carry upwards of 30\,M parameters. This paper introduces Lightweight PCGAE-Net, which addresses the efficiency problem not by post-hoc compression but by correcting two architectural flaws in the current state of the art. The first is a sequential attention ordering bias: in CS3T-UNet, group-wise temporal attention (GTA) always operates on features that have already been transformed by cross-shaped spatial attention (CSA), distorting what temporal information GTA can capture. We remove this dependency by routing both attention modules to the same layer-normalized input and combining their independent outputs through a learned per-channel sigmoid CrossGate. The second flaw is an uncompressed bottleneck: applying full self-attention at the deepest encoder stage, where channel depth reaches $4C$, is quadratically expensive and carries redundant features. A Bottleneck AutoEncoder (BAE) with $1\times1$ convolutions halves this depth and uses an auxiliary reconstruction loss to prevent information collapse. Wrapping these components inside a shallower encoder-decoder with frequency-domain dimensionality reduction ($N_f\!=\!32$, $C\!=\!48$) produces a model with just 8.54\,M parameters -- 58\% fewer than the CS3T-UNet baseline -- that outperforms it by up to 3.26\,dB at 5\,km/h and 6.0\,dB at 9\,km/h in single-step prediction on QuaDriGa dataset.

</details>


### [7] [Kom8ndor: An IEEE 802.11bn-Oriented Simulator for Wi-Fi 8 and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25435)
*Francesc Wilhelmi,Sergio Barrachina-Muñoz,Boris Bellalta*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Kom8ndor，一个支持IEEE 802.11bn（Wi-Fi 8）标准的离散事件网络模拟器，扩展了开源Komondor平台，实现了多接入点协调、非主信道访问和动态子带操作等关键功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 即将到来的IEEE 802.11bn修正案标志着Wi-Fi在超高可靠性（UHR）范式下的重大变革，为理解这一新技术的内涵并支持Wi-Fi 8的早期研究和协议设计，需要一个能够模拟802.11bn特性的仿真工具。

Method: 在已通过ns-3和其他分析工具验证的开源Komondor平台基础上进行扩展，实现了多接入点协调（包括协调时分多址Co-TDMA、协调空间复用Co-SR和协调波束成形Co-BF）、非主信道访问（NPCA）和动态子带操作（DSO）等802.11bn核心功能，并引入机器学习封装器以支持基于AI的协议开发，采用模块化设计促进未来Wi-Fi技术的原型设计和研究。

Result: 成功开发了Kom8ndor模拟器，实现了IEEE 802.11bn的关键特性，包括多接入点协调机制、NPCA和DSO功能，同时提供了机器学习接口和模块化架构，为Wi-Fi 8及未来Wi-Fi技术的研究提供了有效的仿真平台。

Conclusion: Kom8ndor作为开源（GNU GPLv3）工具公开发布，为学术界和工业界提供了研究Wi-Fi 8技术的早期平台，其模块化设计和AI支持能力将加速未来Wi-Fi技术的创新和标准化进程。

Abstract: The upcoming IEEE 802.11bn amendment marks a paradigm shift in Wi-Fi, which will pose ambitious performance targets under the paradigm of Ultra-High Reliability (UHR). To understand the implications of such a new technology and to support early research and protocol design for Wi-Fi~8, we present \texttt{Kom8ndor}. This discrete-event network simulator extends the open-source Komondor platform (a simulator validated against ns-3 and other analytical tools) with 802.11bn features. Among the newly added functionalities, we highlight Multi-Access Point Coordination (MAPC) -- including Coordinated Time-Division Multiple Access (Co-TDMA), Coordinated Spatial Reuse (Co-SR), and Coordinated Beamforming (Co-BF) -- , Non-Primary Channel Access (NPCA), and Dynamic Subband Operation (DSO). Beyond Wi-Fi~8 implementations, \texttt{Kom8ndor} introduces novel functionalities (e.g., a machine learning wrapper for building AI-based protocols) and a modular design to boost the prototyping and research of future Wi-Fi technologies. \texttt{Kom8ndor} is open-source (GNU GPLv3) and available at https://github.com/wn-upf/Komondor.

</details>


### [8] [RQ-SAFE: Coupled Request-Resource Scheduling for Online Edge SFC-DAGs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25467)
*Shengdong Gu,Hongyuan Wan,Taixin Li*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: RQ-SAFE框架通过请求-资源耦合调度，利用意图驱动边缘服务中SFC-DAG局部重排序的自由度，在保证QoS的同时显著改善资源负载均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 意图驱动的边缘服务允许SFC-DAG中的多个VNF分段在保持服务语义的前提下进行局部重排序，这为编排提供了更大的自由度。然而现有编排方法主要针对预定服务顺序优化VNF放置、路由或队列感知调度，未能充分利用这种重排序自由度，也未将其与运行时资源调度有效耦合。

Method: 提出RQ-SAFE请求-资源耦合调度框架，通过预览可行局部顺序在当前边缘基础设施上的资源侧影响来评估每个候选顺序；在整个决策过程中利用队列状态评估局部顺序、对每个VNF候选实例排序并进行最终QoS验证；采用配置感知的学习辅助重排序器，通过优化保留的top-K候选实例来平衡请求侧QoS目标和资源侧负载目标。

Result: 在匹配的边缘SFC-DAG工作负载上，RQ-SAFE实现了与图感知基线相当的QoS合规服务结果，同时改善了资源平衡。相对于GNN-DAG-Score基线，CPU不平衡度降低6.1%，峰值CPU降低2.3%，且控制平面决策时间增加有限。消融实验显示，启用局部顺序灵活性和队列感知相比禁用两者时QoS提升4.53个百分点，两因素间存在3.83个百分点的正向交互作用。

Conclusion: RQ-SAFE为利用意图感知边缘SFC-DAG服务中的编排自由度提供了一种实用的请求-资源耦合范式，证明了局部重排序与队列感知协同作用的有效性，为边缘服务编排提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Intent-driven edge services allow multiple virtual network function (VNF) segments in a service function chain directed acyclic graph (SFC-DAG) to be locally reordered without changing service semantics, creating richer request-side orchestration freedom. Existing orchestration methods mainly optimize VNF placement, routing, or queue-aware scheduling for a predetermined service order; they do not fully exploit this freedom or couple it with runtime resource scheduling. This paper presents RQ-SAFE, a request-resource coupled scheduling framework for online edge SFC-DAG orchestration with checked commitment. RQ-SAFE evaluates each feasible local order by previewing its resource-side consequences on the current edge infrastructure, and uses the retained order to guide VNF instance selection and path construction. Queue state is used throughout the decision process to evaluate local orders, rank per-VNF candidates, and perform final queue-aware quality-of-service (QoS) validation. A profile-aware learning-assisted re-ranker balances request-side QoS objectives and resource-side load objectives by refining retained top-K candidates. On matched edge SFC-DAG workloads, RQ-SAFE achieves comparable QoS-compliant service outcomes to graph-aware baselines while improving resource balance. Relative to the graph neural network (GNN)-based GNN-DAG-Score baseline on public-mixed workloads, it reduces central processing unit (CPU) imbalance by 6.1% and peak CPU by 2.3%, with limited additional control-plane decision time. Ablation results show that enabling local-order flexibility and queue awareness together improves QoS by 4.53 percentage points over disabling both factors, with a 3.83 percentage-point positive interaction between the two factors. Overall, RQ-SAFE offers a practical request-resource coupling paradigm for exploiting orchestration freedom in intent-aware edge SFC-DAG services.

</details>


### [9] [Dependency-Aware Dominant Resource Fairness for Multi-Tenant Multi-Resource Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25540)
*Braik Zeidan,Francesca Fossati,Sahar Hoteit,Stefano Secci*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出DDRF策略，解决多资源分配中的资源依赖问题，提升用户满意度并减少资源浪费。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在需求超过可用容量的多租户网络拥塞系统中，传统的多资源分配方法（如DRF）假设资源之间存在线性比例依赖关系，这可能导致低效分配和资源浪费。实际场景中资源间存在相互依赖关系，无法简单确定各资源分配量。

Method: 提出依赖感知主导资源公平（DDRF）策略，这是DRF的集中式泛化方法。DDRF考虑资源间依赖关系，通过均衡拥塞资源的活跃主导份额来分配资源，同时保持DRF的优良特性并避免对低需求租户的低效分配。

Result: 通过Amazon EC2追踪数据和虚拟化无线接入网（vRAN）用例进行评估，DDRF相比依赖无关的基线方法，将有效用户满意度提升高达80%，资源浪费减少高达60%，相比功利主义策略将Jain公平性指数提升超过15%。

Conclusion: DDRF策略成功解决了多资源分配中的资源依赖问题，证明了其能够确保至少一个拥塞资源饱和，实现帕累托效率并消除资源浪费，在真实场景中显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Multi-resource allocation in network-congested, multi-tenant systems in which demand exceeds available capacity is challenging, as there is no straightforward way to determine how much of each resource to assign, especially when resources are interdependent. Classical approaches such as Dominant Resource Fairness (DRF), which generalizes Max-Min Fairness (MMF) to multiple resources, assume linear proportional dependencies across resources, requiring allocations to follow fixed proportions implied by tenants demands. However, this assumption may lead to inefficient allocations and resource waste, with allocated resources that go unused in practice. In this paper, we consider a multi-resource orchestrator and propose the Dependency-aware Dominant Resource Fairness (DDRF) policy, a centralized generalization of DRF that considers inter-resource dependencies: it equalizes active dominant shares of congested resources, preserving DRFs desirable properties, while avoiding its inefficiency with low-demand tenants. We prove that DDRF always saturates at least one congested resource, ensuring Pareto efficiency and eliminating resource waste. We evaluate DDRF using Amazon EC2 traces and a virtualized radio access network (vRAN) use case while considering real resource dependencies. The results show that DDRF improves effective user satisfaction by up to 80% and reduces resource waste by up to 60% compared to dependency-agnostic baselines, while improving Jain's fairness index by more than 15% compared to the utilitarian policy.

</details>


### [10] [A Backward-Compatible Protocol Upgrade for HotNets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25786)
*Paolo Costa,Michael Schapira*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文概述了ACM HotNets 2026在范围、审稿流程和会议结构等方面的变革，旨在强化其作为议程设置研究和社区讨论场所的角色，并探索生成式AI在审稿中的负责任应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HotNets希望继续成为雄心勃勃、非传统和发人深省思想的家园，同时定位为大型会议（如SIGCOMM或NSDI）可能后续采纳的新方法和格式的实验场所。当前需要明确和扩展HotNets在网络社区中的角色。

Method: 提出了系统性改革方案：1) 扩大范围纳入观点性和面向社区的贡献；2) 为技术论文和观点论文引入差异化评价标准；3) 采用更具协作性和讨论导向的审稿流程；4) 重新思考入选工作的展示和讨论方式；5) 探索生成式AI在审稿和研究传播中的负责任应用。通过在活动期间和之后收集社区反馈，并准备总结社区反应和经验教训的后续报告。

Result: 形成了一套连贯的变革方案，这些改革不是孤立的调整，而是明确了HotNets作为议程设置研究、社区讨论以及评估、传播和讨论研究方式实验场所的角色。

Conclusion: 这些变革将帮助HotNets继续服务网络社区，为其提供创新思想交流平台，同时为大型会议提供可借鉴的新方法和格式经验。通过社区反馈机制确保持续改进。

Abstract: This document outlines the changes adopted for ACM HotNets 2026, spanning its scope, review process, and program structure. Rather than isolated adjustments, these changes form a coherent effort to clarify and extend HotNets' role as a venue for agenda-setting research, community discussion, and experimentation with how the networking community evaluates, disseminates, and discusses research. In particular, HotNets 2026 broadens its scope to include perspective and community-facing contributions, introduces distinct evaluation criteria for technical and perspective papers, adopts a more collaborative and discussion-oriented review process, rethinks how accepted work is presented and discussed at the workshop, and explores responsible uses of generative AI (GenAI) in reviewing and research dissemination.
  We believe these changes will help HotNets continue to serve as a home for ambitious, unconventional, and thought-provoking ideas, while also positioning it as a venue for experimenting with new approaches and formats that larger conferences, e.g., SIGCOMM or NSDI, might later adopt. We use this document to solicit feedback from the community, both on these changes and on how HotNets can best serve the networking community in the future. We plan to collect feedback during and after the event and to prepare a follow-up report summarizing the community's reactions and lessons learned.

</details>


### [11] [Can Machine Learning Break Wi-Fi Privacy? A Study on MAC Address Randomization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25788)
*Marta Puig,Costas Michaelides,Lucia Pintor,Boris Bellalta,Francesc Wilhelmi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了MAC地址随机化隐私保护机制的漏洞，证明通过机器学习指纹识别仍可追踪设备，最高准确率达89.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管MAC地址随机化已被广泛采用作为防止被动跟踪的对策，但其在IEEE 802.11网络发现阶段的隐私保护有效性仍存在疑问。本文旨在研究被动攻击者在不同窃听场景下的潜在追踪能力，以评估现有隐私协议的安全性。

Method: 从探测帧(Probe Frames)中提取未加密的硬件规格信息，结合帧间到达时间(IFAT)和模拟接收信号强度指示(SRSSI)信号。关键创新点是对高吞吐量(HT)能力信息字段进行按位分解，以改进设备识别准确性。采用三种无监督聚类算法(K-Means、DBSCAN和OPTICS)在来自六个制造商的22台设备数据集上进行评估。

Result: DBSCAN算法在使用分解的HT能力信息和三个SRSSI测量值时，实现了高达89.6%的全局准确率，证明了即使启用MAC地址随机化，设备仍然可以通过硬件特征指纹识别被追踪。

Conclusion: 现有的MAC地址随机化解决方案不足以保护用户隐私，需要在Wi-Fi标准制定过程中增强隐私保护机制，以应对基于机器学习的指纹识别攻击。

Abstract: Medium Access Control (MAC) address randomization has been widely adopted during the IEEE 802.11 network discovery phase as a countermeasure against passive tracking. This paper exposes vulnerabilities in these privacy protocols by demonstrating that devices remain identifiable using Machine Learning (ML)-based fingerprinting. To study the potential tracking capabilities of a passive attacker, we evaluate different eavesdropping scenarios and configurations. To this end, we extract unencrypted hardware specifications from Probe Frames, which we combine with the Inter-Probe Frame Arrival Time (IFAT) and Simulated Received Signal Strength Indication (SRSSI) signals. A core contribution of this paper is the bitwise decomposition of the High Throughput (HT) capabilities information field, which improves device identification accuracy. We evaluate this de-randomization approach using three unsupervised clustering algorithms (K-Means, DBSCAN, and OPTICS) across a dataset of 22 devices from six manufacturers. Our results show that DBSCAN, when using decomposed HT capabilities information and three SRSSI measurements, achieves a global accuracy up to 89.6%. This suggests that the existing MAC randomization solutions are insufficient and underscores the need for enhancing privacy within Wi-Fi standardization.

</details>


### [12] [Lyapunov Optimization based Queue-aware Traffic Shaping for 5G-TSN in Industrial Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25823)
*Kouros Zanbouri,Md Noor-A-Rahim,Cormac J Sreenan,Dirk Pesch*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Lyapunov Drift-plus-Penalty理论的跨层速率控制算法，用于解决工业5G网络中AGV管理面临的LOS遮挡导致的深衰落问题，有效消除了缓冲区膨胀，保障了低延迟通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制造企业日益依赖私有5G网络管理自动导引车(AGV)，但工业环境中密集的金属结构经常导致视距(LOS)遮挡事件，引起接收信号电平的深衰落，使信道容量近乎归零。标准传输协议和速率自适应机制无法对这些深衰落做出足够快的反应，导致缓冲区膨胀和延迟峰值，违反安全裕度要求。

Method: 提出一种基于Lyapunov Drift-plus-Penalty理论的跨层速率控制算法。该控制器基于瞬时缓冲区状态动态优化服务效用与队列稳定性之间的权衡，无需预测性信道模型。使用基于轨迹的仿真框架进行验证，该框架利用符合3GPP标准的容量数据复现5G阻塞的随机动态特性。

Result: 数值结果表明，基准调度方案会遭受灾难性的队列累积，导致重连时出现过度的延迟；而所提出的Lyapunov控制器能有效消除缓冲区膨胀。通过防止拥塞引起的积压，系统确保在信道恢复后立即进行低延迟操作，维持近乎确定性的行为。

Conclusion: 该跨层速率控制算法成功解决了工业5G环境中LOS遮挡引起的深衰落问题，无需预测性信道模型即可实现有效的队列稳定性控制，为AGV等工业应用提供了可靠的低延迟通信保障，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Manufacturing companies look increasingly at Private 5G networks to manage Automated Guided Vehicles (AGVs). While 5G promises Ultra-Reliable Low Latency Communication (URLLC), its service quality is challenged by industrial environments characterized by dense metallic structures, which frequently cause line-of-sight (LOS) blockage events, causing deep fades in received signal levels that can degrade channel capacity to near-zero. Standard transport protocols and rate adaptation mechanisms fail to react sufficiently fast to these deep fades, resulting in bufferbloat and latency spikes that violate safety margins. In this paper, we propose a cross-layer rate control algorithm based on Lyapunov Drift-plus-Penalty theory. The proposed controller dynamically optimizes the trade-off between service utility and queue stability based on instantaneous buffer states, without requiring predictive channel models. We validate the approach using a trace-driven simulation framework that replicates the stochastic dynamics of 5G blockage using 3GPP-compliant capacity data. Numerical results demonstrate that while baseline scheduling schemes suffer from catastrophic queue accumulation, leading to excessive delays upon reconnection, the proposed Lyapunov controller effectively eliminates bufferbloat. By preventing congestion-induced backlog, the system ensures immediate low-latency operation as soon as the channel recovers, maintaining near-deterministic behavior.

</details>

### [13] [ActPlane: Programmable OS-Level Policy Enforcement for Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25189)
*Yusheng Zheng,Tianyuan Wu,Quanzhi Fu,Tong Yu,Wenan Mao,Wei Wang,Dan Williams,Andi Quinn*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 提出了ActPlane，一个在操作系统内核层面执行AI代理策略的引擎，解决了现有工具层拦截无法覆盖所有执行路径的问题，同时提供语义反馈和隔离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理在生产环境中运行需要策略执行（如安全性和有效性策略），但存在语义鸿沟：策略意图用自然语言表达，而执行需要作用于具体系统动作。现有方法有局限：工具调用护栏会遗漏绕过工具层的系统动作，而OS沙箱只控制资源访问而非动作，返回的错误信息让代理困惑。

Method: 识别关键洞察：策略上下文存在于最接近任务的代理中，而执行必须在OS层面覆盖所有执行路径。ActPlane允许代理声明策略并在OS内核中执行，提供语义反馈和隔离。使用简单的信息流控制(IFC)领域特定语言(DSL)支持跨事件策略，通过eBPF实现。

Result: 在实证研究策略、编码任务基准和安全基准上评估，ActPlane提高了策略合规性，包括工具调用拦截无法观察到的间接执行路径，开销仅为1.9%-8.4%。

Conclusion: ActPlane成功弥合了策略声明和执行之间的鸿沟，在保证性能的同时实现了更全面的策略执行覆盖，为AI代理的安全运行提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly run in production through harnesses, the software around the LLM, including an engine that enforces safety and effectiveness policies, e.g., 'run tests before committing.' Enforcing these policies requires bridging a semantic gap: policy intent is expressed in underspecified natural language, while enforcement must act on concrete system actions, e.g., which test to run. Many policies also define event ordering or data flow actions. Yet existing approaches fall short. Tool-call guardrails miss system actions that bypass the tool layer, while OS sandboxes control resource access instead of actions, returning opaque errors that confuse the agent. Our key insight is that policy context lives within the agent closest to the task, while enforcement must happen at the OS to cover all execution paths. We introduce ActPlane, a policy engine that lets agents declare policies and enforces them in the OS kernel with semantic feedback and isolation. ActPlane uses a simple information-flow control (IFC) DSL to support cross-event policies. We implement ActPlane with eBPF and evaluate it on policies from the empirical study, coding-task benchmarks, and safety benchmarks. ActPlane improves policy compliance, including on indirect execution paths that tool-call interception cannot observe, with 1.9%-8.4% overhead. ActPlane is at https://github.com/eunomia-bpf/ActPlane

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [14] [Bridging the Post-discharge Gap: A Traceable Multi-agent Framework for Safe and Continuous Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25334)
*Runwei Guan,Yi Zhou,Heyi Lin,Jinjing Zhu,Mingyuan Hou,Yang Yang,Fang Yuan,Xiaohong Lin,Shaofeng Liang,Xuming Hu,Tao Li,Tianbin Zhao,Yutao Yue,Zhiyuan Wang,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了Healink，一个记忆增强的多智能体框架，用于AI辅助的出院后随访，通过生成基于处方可追溯的回复，在临床安全性和权威性方面优于人类医生基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 出院后临床随访对维持护理连续性和降低长期健康风险至关重要，但传统随访模式面临医疗人力短缺、患者病史碎片化和跨科室信息孤岛等问题。虽然大语言模型在医学问答中展现出潜力，但其在持续护理中的应用受到幻觉风险和无法基于纵向、患者特异性约束进行推理的限制。

Method: Healink架构集成了分诊路由机制、利用关系数据库实现最优延迟的统一记忆增强模块，以及严格的基于约束的检索增强生成引擎。通过向量化历史临床记录并在多样化的表型和干预维度上采用加权相似度函数，框架确保了精确的患者间和患者内病例匹配，同时主动防止跨科室药物冲突。

Result: 在包含400个连续病例和85个高复杂度真实世界随访病例的数据集以及webMedQA基准上进行了评估。在临床专家进行的严格单盲评估中，该框架在权威性和临床安全性方面均优于人类医生基线。

Conclusion: 通过生成可追溯的白盒证据链，Healink为智能患者管理提供了一个可扩展、安全且高效的范式，最终提升社会医疗健康结果。

Abstract: Post-discharge clinical follow-up is critical for maintaining continuity of care and mitigating long-term health risks. However, traditional follow-up paradigms suffer from shortage of health workforce, fragmented patient histories, and information silos across clinical departments. While large language models have demonstrated potential in medical question-answering, their deployment in continuous care is hindered by hallucination risks and a fundamental inability to reason over longitudinal, patient-specific constraints. Here we present Healink, a memory-enhanced multi-agent framework to support AI-assisted post-discharge follow-up by generating prescription-grounded, traceable responses that improved completeness and perceived clinical utility in retrospective and physician-blinded evaluations. The architecture seamlessly integrates a triage routing mechanism, a unified memory enhancement module utilizing a robust relational database for optimal latency, and a strict constraint-based retrieval-augmented generation engine. By vectorizing historical clinical records and employing weighted similarity functions across diverse phenotypic and intervention dimensions, Healink ensures precise inter-patient and intra-patient case matching while actively preventing cross-departmental drug conflicts. We evaluated Healink on a dataset comprising 400 continuous and 85 highly complex real-world follow-up cases, alongside the webMedQA benchmark. In a rigorous single-blind evaluation conducted by clinical experts, the framework outperformed human physician baselines in both authoritativeness and clinical safety. By generating a traceable, white-box evidence chain, Healink provides a scalable, safe, and highly effective paradigm for intelligent patient management, ultimately enhancing societal healthcare outcomes.

</details>


### [15] [Rate-Aware Quantum-Inspired Trajectory Learning for Interference-Limited Multi-UAV Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25480)
*Khaoula Khaled,Muhammad Afaq,Ali Arshad Nasir,Zeeshan Kaleem*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出RA-QAGC方案，通过速率感知图抽象与去中心化强化学习解决无人机轨迹优化的维度灾难问题，显著提升网络吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机(UAV)在灾难和常规场景下可提供按需、高容量连接，但在轨迹优化中面临维度灾难挑战。干扰受限环境和巨大搜索空间使得实时协调计算代价高昂。

Method: 提出速率感知量子退火图凝聚(RA-QAGC)方案，该方法结合速率感知图抽象与去中心化强化学习，实现可扩展的干扰感知无人机协调。通过识别高吞吐量位置并引导无人机轨迹向吞吐量最优区域自适应调整。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提方案总吞吐量达到59.4 Mbps，优先用户吞吐量达23.9 Mbps，相比基线方案分别获得约15%和34%的性能增益，优于现有方案。

Conclusion: RA-QAGC方案有效平衡了网络容量并满足服务质量(QoS)要求，成功解决了无人机轨迹优化中的计算复杂性难题，为干扰受限环境下的无人机实时协调提供了可行解决方案。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) can provide on-demand, high-capacity connectivity in disaster and normal situation. However, it faces a challenge of curse of dimensionality in trajectory optimization, where interference-limited environments and vast search spaces make real-time coordination computationally expensive. To overcome this challenge, we propose the Rate-Aware Quantum-Annealed Graph Condensation (RA-QAGC) scheme, which combines rate-aware graph abstraction with decentralized reinforcement learning to enable scalable, interference-aware UAV coordination. By identifying high throughput locations and guiding UAV trajectory adaptation toward throughput-optimal regions, RA-QAGC effectively balances network capacity by maintaining quality-of-service (QoS) requirements. Simulation results demonstrate the proposal outperformed over existing schemes by achieving 59.4 Mbps total throughput and 23.9 Mbps priority-user throughput, representing gains of approximately 15% and 34%, respectively, over the baseline schemes.

</details>

*Adeeb Zaman,Erik Nordby,Fred Heiding*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文通过六个维度评估了六种前沿语言模型的操纵行为，发现不同环境下的操纵倾向互不相关，操纵驱动因素因任务类型而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试通常仅在单一环境中改变单一评估维度，这种评估方法不足以全面衡量语言模型的操纵倾向。作者认为需要更严格的多维度评估方法来理解模型在不同场景下的操纵行为。

Method: 研究者在六个不同的环境（从谈判任务到智能体工作流）中评估了六种前沿语言模型的操纵行为，共产生13,590个独立场景。评估沿着三个轴进行测量：框架设置（要求诚实或允许操纵）、激励结构（从无激励到实质性激励）、以及任务难度。研究还使用Spearman等级相关性分析不同环境间操纵倾向的关系。

Result: 研究发现不同环境间模型操纵率的Spearman等级相关性平均仅为ρ=0.055，表明一个任务中的操纵倾向不能预测另一个任务中的操纵倾向。更重要的是，驱动操纵的因素在不同环境中存在差异：在激励模型歪曲未来行为的环境中，指令框架和结构性约束激励是主要驱动因素；而在激励模型歪曲基本事实的环境中，任务难度占主导地位。这一规律在五个环境中得到识别，并在第六个保留环境中得到验证。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调了在测量语言模型操纵倾向时进行严格多维度评估的重要性。由于操纵行为在不同环境中表现不一致且驱动因素各异，单一维度的评估方法不足以全面理解模型的操纵倾向，需要建立更全面的评估框架。

Abstract: We evaluate manipulative behavior in six frontier language models across six environments, ranging from negotiation tasks to agentic workflows, resulting in 13{,}590 individual scenarios. Manipulation rates are measured across three axes: framing (mandate honesty or permit manipulation), incentive structure (from no incentives to substantial ones), and task difficulty. Existing benchmarks typically vary a single axis within a single environment, an approach our results show is insufficient. We rank models by manipulation rate and find Spearman rank correlations across environments average $ρ= 0.055$, indicating manipulative tendencies in one task do not necessarily predict those in another. Additionally, we find the axis that drives manipulation varies across different environments. In environments where models are incentivized to misrepresent future actions, instructional framing and structurally binding incentives are the primary drivers; in environments where models are incentivized to misrepresent a ground truth, task difficulty dominates. This split was identified in five environments and validated against a sixth held-out environment. Together, these findings illustrate the importance of rigorous multi-dimensional evaluations when measuring manipulative propensities.

</details>


### [17] [Multi-Agent Goal Recognition with Team- and Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning and Factorized Branch-and-Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25978)
*Thiago Thomas,Gabriel de Oliveira Ramos,Felipe Meneguzzi*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: MAGR-BB通过分支定界搜索高效解决多智能体目标识别问题，在保持识别准确性的同时大幅降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体目标识别需要对智能体团队划分和各团队目标进行联合推断，假设空间随团队分区数量和每队目标数量呈组合爆炸式增长。实际应用（如无人机监控和协作机器人）仅暴露智能体轨迹，迫使观察者仅从行为对团队-目标假设进行排序。

Method: 提出Multi-Agent Goal Recognition with Branch-and-Bound (MAGR-BB)方法，使用共享的团队和目标条件策略作为评分模型，在因式分解的分支定界搜索中进行假设空间搜索。

Result: 在受控的多智能体Blocksworld基准测试中，MAGR-BB在整个轨迹过程中返回与穷举搜索相同的顶级假设，同时将假设具体化减少数个数量级，并显著降低累积识别运行时间。

Conclusion: MAGR-BB成功解决了多智能体目标识别中的组合爆炸问题，在保持识别准确性的同时实现了显著的计算效率提升，为实际应用提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent goal recognition asks an observer to jointly infer which agents act together and what each team is trying to achieve, so the hypothesis space grows combinatorially with the number of team partitions and goals per team. Real applications such as drone surveillance and collaborative robotics expose only the agents' trajectory, which forces the observer to rank team-goal hypotheses from behavior alone. Multi-Agent Goal Recognition with Branch-and-Bound (MAGR-BB) addresses this setting with a shared team- and goal-conditioned policy used as the scoring model inside a factorized branch-and-bound search. On a controlled multi-agent Blocksworld benchmark, MAGR-BB returns the same top-ranked hypothesis as exhaustive search throughout the trajectory while cutting hypothesis materialization by orders of magnitude and reducing cumulative recognition runtime substantially.

</details>

### [18] [The Hitchhiker's Guide to Agentic AI: From Foundations to Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24937)
*Haggai Roitman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本书是构建自主AI系统（Agentic AI）的综合性实践指南，涵盖从底层原理到生产部署的完整技术栈，包括LLM基础设施、对齐与推理层、以及智能体的核心架构与协调机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认为构建优秀的智能体系统需要理解整个技术栈的每一层，而不仅仅是单一层面。现有研究往往聚焦于特定技术，缺乏对智能体系统全貌的系统性梳理，因此需要一本从基础原理到生产部署的综合性参考书。

Method: 全书采用分层架构组织：（1）LLM基础层——Transformer架构、GPU系统、训练与微调方法（SFT、LoRA、MoE）、模型压缩及推理优化；（2）对齐与推理层——RLHF、PPO、DPO及其变体、GRPO、奖励建模、思维链与测试时扩展；（3）智能体层——轨迹强化学习、RAG与Agentic RAG、记忆系统（上下文、外部、情景、语义）、智能体工具设计与上下文管理、设计模式分类；（4）多智能体协调——MCP协议、工具使用、A2A通信协议、集中/分散/分层拓扑架构。每章结合理论、代码示例与原始文献。

Result: 构建了一个覆盖智能体系统全技术栈的完整框架，系统阐述了从模型基础设施、对齐推理到智能体协调的设计方法，提供了丰富的设计模式分类法与多智能体架构方案，并配套实现指南、代码示例与评估方法论。

Conclusion: 成功的智能体系统开发需要打通底层基础设施、对齐技术、智能体架构与多智能体协调等多个层面，理解各层之间的交互关系。本书为从业者提供了从理论到实践的系统性指导，填补了该领域综合性参考资料空白。

Abstract: The Hitchhiker's Guide to Agentic AI is a comprehensive practitioner's reference for building autonomous AI systems. The book covers the full stack from first principles to production deployment, organized around a central thesis: building great agentic systems requires understanding every layer of the pipeline, not just one. The book opens with the LLM substrate -- transformer architecture, GPU systems, training and fine-tuning (SFT,LoRA, MoE), model compression, and inference optimization -- treated as essential foundations rather than the primary focus. It then develops the alignment and reasoning layer: reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF), PPO, DPO and its variants, GRPO, reward modeling, and RL for large reasoning models including chain-of-thought and test-time scaling. The second half is devoted to agentic AI proper. Topics include agentic training and trajectory-based RL, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG and Agentic RAG), memory systems (in-context, external, episodic, and semantic), agent harness design and context management, and a taxonomy of agent design patterns. Inter-agent coordination is covered in depth: the Model Context Protocol (MCP), agent skills and tool use, the Agent-to-Agent (A2A) communication protocol, and multi-agent architectures spanning centralized, decentralized, and hierarchical topologies. The book concludes with agent development frameworks, agentic UI design, evaluation methodology for agentic tasks, and production deployment. Each chapter pairs rigorous theoretical foundations with implementation guidance, code examples, and references to the primary literature.

</details>


### [19] [Project Auto-World: Towards Automated Benchmarking of Neural Relational Reasoners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24965)
*Anirban Das,Joanne Boisson,Irtaza Khalid,Sumita Garai,Steven Schockaert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出利用大语言模型（LLM）自动化生成具有挑战性的关系推理基准测试数据，通过进化搜索和智能体搜索发现困难样本，并利用这些数据提升Edge Transformer的泛化能力，实现了神经关系推理研究的自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经模型在关系结构推理上仍面临重大挑战，特别是在需要系统性地将学习到的知识应用到比训练时更难的问题实例时。评估这种泛化能力的难点在于，人们很难预先确定什么使得一个实例变得困难，这阻碍了该领域的研究进展。

Method: 提出了一种端到端的自动化基准生成方法：在由Datalog规则参数化的世界中，使用Edge Transformer作为推理评估器，采用基于FunSearch的LLM驱动进化搜索和自主智能体搜索来发现能够产生困难问题实例的采样函数。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法能够成功发现并生成具有挑战性的问题实例；同时，利用这些生成的数据可以改进Edge Transformer模型，使其在面对进一步的数据扰动时表现出更好的泛化能力；此外，该框架还能应用于LLM提出的新世界，实现了神经关系推理的自主研究。

Conclusion: 本研究成功展示了利用LLM自动化生成困难基准测试数据的可行性，这不仅为评估神经模型的泛化能力提供了新途径，还通过生成的数据提升了模型性能，为神经关系推理领域的自主研究开辟了新的可能性。

Abstract: Reasoning about relational structures remains a significant challenge for neural models, particularly when they must systematically apply learned knowledge to problem instances that are harder than those seen in training. Progress is hampered by the difficulty of evaluating such generalization, since a priori, it is rarely clear what makes an instance hard. We study how this issue can be addressed by using large language models (LLMs) to automate benchmark generation, learning to produce increasingly challenging instances in an end-to-end manner. Concretely, given a world parametrized by Datalog rules, and an Edge Transformer as the reasoning evaluator, we use LLM-driven evolutionary search (based on FunSearch) and autonomous agentic search to discover sampling functions that yield hard problem instances. We also show that the Edge Transformer can be improved using this data such that it generalizes well to further data perturbations. Finally, we show that the same machinery can be applied to novel worlds proposed by LLMs, opening the door to autonomous research on neural relational reasoning.

</details>


### [20] [Diagnosing and Mitigating Compounding Failures in Agentic Persuasion via Taxonomic Strategy Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24976)
*Pradyumna Narayana,Sana Ayromlou,Purvi Sehgal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TS-RAG框架，通过分类瓶颈消除语义泄漏，显著提升开放环境下的辩论说服能力，使轻量级智能体能击败参数更强的对手。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步开放环境中的基础模型智能体常因早期错误累积而失败。标准RAG存在语义泄漏问题（优先词汇重叠而非逻辑必要性），导致多智能体辩论在主观任务中出现问题漂移和迎合性从众现象。

Method: 提出分类策略RAG（TS-RAG），通过离散分类瓶颈路由策略，将论证结构与主题内容解耦；引入逐轮辩论状态表示（DSR）提供细粒度诊断。

Result: 零样本跨域评估显示TS-RAG显著提升抽象逻辑迁移能力；在非对称部署中，轻量级说服者的胜率从70.5%提升至78.5%，能稳定击败参数更优的对手；加速论证效率。

Conclusion: TS-RAG作为"能力桥梁"有效缓解了语义泄漏问题，严格约束对于防止智能体因默认迎合性导致评估崩溃至关重要。

Abstract: Foundation-model agents in multi-step, open-ended environments frequently suffer from compounding errors, where early mistakes contaminate long-horizon trajectories. While Multi-Agent Debate (MAD) succeeds in deterministic domains, agents in subjective tasks like persuasion experience severe problem drift and sycophantic conformity. We identify semantic leakage in standard Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) as a reproducible trigger for these failures, as standard RAG prioritizes vocabulary overlap over logical necessity.
  To eliminate this leakage, we introduce Taxonomic Strategy RAG (TS-RAG), a systems intervention that routes strategies through a discrete categorical bottleneck to decouple argumentative structure from topical content. Zero-shot, cross-domain evaluations demonstrate that TS-RAG significantly improves the transfer of abstract logic where standard semantic retrieval collapses. Crucially, TS-RAG acts as a "capability bridge" in asymmetric deployments, empowering lightweight persuaders to consistently defeat parametrically superior opponents (improving win rates from 70.5 to 78.5) and accelerating argumentative efficiency. Finally, we introduce trace-level diagnostics via a turn-by-turn Debate State Representation (DSR), demonstrating the necessity of strict constraints to prevent evaluation collapse via default agentic sycophancy.

</details>


### [21] [Do vision-language models search like humans? Reasoning tokens as a reaction-time analog in classic visual-search paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25066)
*Farahnaz Wick*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将经典视觉搜索范式应用于视觉语言模型(VLMs)，以推理token数量类比反应时，发现模型能复现人类的部分搜索特征(如特征搜索与联合搜索的差异)，但在目标呈现效应、计数准确性等方面表现出与人类不同的认知模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉搜索是研究视觉注意力的经典范式，通过反应时随项目数量变化的关系可区分并行(pop-out)搜索与串行(注意需求)搜索。研究者希望探究视觉语言模型是否表现出与人类相同的行为特征，从而理解机器视觉认知的本质。

Method: 采用四种经典视觉搜索范式：(1)特征vs联合搜索、(2)空间配置(T-vs-L)搜索、(3)枚举任务、(4)倾斜/垂直搜索不对称性。使用模型推理("思考")token数量作为搜索努力的替代指标，与大型人类基准数据集(Wolfe et al., 2010)进行比较，测试前沿模型和中端模型。

Result: 模型复现了部分人类特征：特征搜索努力平坦，联合搜索努力随集合大小增长；前沿模型保持准确性而中端模型降至随机水平；分辨率控制表明联合搜索成本源于真实搜索而非形状解析困难。但也存在差异：目标呈现的努力斜率超过目标缺失斜率(与人类顺序相反)；枚举任务保持准确而人类会丢失计数；自适应推理模型在检测任务上拒绝深思熟虑。

Conclusion: 心理物理学范式应用于行为测试是探测机器视觉认知的敏锐且低成本的方法，模型与人类的差异点和一致性同样具有信息价值，为理解机器视觉认知提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Visual search has been one of the most productive paradigms in the study of visual attention: the way reaction time scales with the number of items distinguishes parallel, "pop-out" search from serial, attention-demanding search. I ask whether vision-language models (VLMs) exhibit the same behavioral signatures. I adapt four classic paradigms: feature versus conjunction search, spatial-configuration (T-vs-L) search, enumeration, and the tilted/vertical search asymmetry; and present them to current frontier and mid-tier models. Because a single model call has no reaction time, I use the number of reasoning ("thinking") tokens a model spends per trial as a within-model analog of search effort, and I compare against a large public human benchmark (Wolfe et al., 2010). The models reproduce several human signatures: feature search costs flat effort while conjunction effort climbs with set size; frontier models hold accuracy where mid-tier models collapse to chance; and a resolution control shows the conjunction cost is genuine search rather than difficulty resolving small shapes. They also diverge from humans in informative ways. The target-present effort slope exceeds the target-absent slope, reversing the human ordering; enumeration remains accurate where humans would lose count; and a reasoning model with adaptive deliberation declines to deliberate on detection tasks altogether, so that a single search expresses itself as an effort gradient in one model and as an accuracy cliff in another. I argue that psychophysical paradigms, applied behaviorally, are a sharp and inexpensive probe of machine visual cognition, and that the points of divergence are as informative as the points of agreement.

</details>


### [22] [Beyond Shapley: Efficient Computation of Asymmetric Shapley Values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25103)
*Ezequiel Companeetz,Santiago Cifuentes,Sergio Abriola*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究非对称Shapley值（ASV）的计算复杂度与高效算法，证明了在某些SHAP计算为#P-难的情境下，ASV可多项式时间精确计算，并提出了根有向树上的精确算法与一般因果DAG上的近似算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shapley值是特征归因的主流方法，但标准SHAP在某些情况下计算复杂度为#P-难，且难以融入因果先验知识。ASV能够通过因果图引入因果知识，但其计算效率尚不明确。本文旨在探索ASV的可计算性边界并开发高效算法。

Method: 1. 证明在特定情境下ASV可多项式时间精确计算；2. 引入因果图拓扑排序的等价类概念以加速计算；3. 针对根有向树提出多项式时间精确算法；4. 针对一般因果DAG，设计基于均匀随机采样拓扑排序的近似算法，并利用已知采样机制及简化替代方案实现。

Result: 1. 理论上确立了ASV在某些SHAP为#P-难情境下的多项式可计算性；2. 根有向树上的算法复杂度与等价类数量成多项式关系；3. 一般DAG上的近似算法在实验中表现良好，验证了方法在现实因果结构中的可行性。

Conclusion: ASV在理论可计算性与实际效率上优于传统SHAP，能够有效融合因果知识并提供高效的解释能力，适用于具有因果结构的现实场景。

Abstract: We address the problem of explainability in machine learning models through feature attribution methods. In particular, we consider a variant of Shapley values known as Asymmetric Shapley Values (ASV), which enables the incorporation of causal knowledge into model-agnostic explanations through the use of a causal graph. We show that in certain contexts in which the computation of SHAP is $\#P$-hard, the exact computation of ASV can be done in polynomial time. To extend this algorithmic result, we introduce a notion of equivalence classes over the topological orderings of the underlying causal graph, which is useful to reduce the time to compute ASV. In particular, we present a polynomial-time algorithm (in the number of equivalence classes) to compute it whenever the causal graph is a rooted directed tree. Finally, we develop an algorithm for approximating ASV in arbitrary causal DAGs which relies on a procedure to sample topological orderings uniformly at random. To implement this sampling mechanism we leverage known algorithms as well as simpler alternatives. Our experimental results demonstrate the practical viability of the proposed approach in realistic causal structures.

</details>


### [23] [Agentic Knowledge Tracing: A Multi-Agent LLM Architecture for Stealth Assessment of Financial Literacy in Serious Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25358)
*Gabriel Santos,Rita Julia,Marcelo Nascimento*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Agentic BKT管道，通过多智能体大语言模型架构从游戏事件中隐式评估金融素养，在193名K-12参与者的实验中显示出显著预测效度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在严肃游戏中评估金融素养时，如何在保持游戏学习体验不被打断的同时进行有效评估是一个核心挑战。

Method: 提出四阶段Agentic BKT管道：游戏事件日志捕获→LLM事件分类器标注→四个领域专用智能体进行会话级推理→专家评判智能体综合评估。该管道基于OECD/INFE金融素养框架，结合贝叶斯知识追踪进行能力估计。

Result: 在193名K-12参与者的264次游戏会话中，掌握度估计与学习收益显著相关(r=0.276, p=0.0001)，与后测成绩显著相关(r=0.333, p<0.0001)，与前测成绩无相关性。多智能体方法的预测效度约为单LLM基线的三倍。

Conclusion: 多智能体架构通过领域分解和会话级推理，有效捕捉了游戏过程中金融素养的多维特性，为严肃游戏中的隐式评估提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Assessing financial literacy during gameplay without disrupting the learning experience remains a key challenge in serious games for education. We present the Agentic BKT pipeline, a multi-agent large language model architecture for stealth assessment of financial competencies from open-ended gameplay events. The pipeline processes events from a 2D platformer serious game aligned with the OECD/INFE financial literacy framework through four phases: (1) the game captures every player decision as a structured event log; (2) an LLM event classifier labels each action on a four-point rubric validated against three domain experts (Fleiss kappa = 0.624, substantial agreement); (3) four domain-specific agents specializing in risk mitigation, investing, spending, and credit management perform session-level reasoning over behavioral trajectories, feeding per-competency Bayesian Knowledge Tracing that estimates mastery within each domain; and (4) an expert judge agent synthesizes the domain-level estimates into an overall mastery score. Evaluated with 193 K-12 participants across 264 game sessions, the Agentic BKT pipeline yields mastery estimates significantly correlated with learning gain (r = 0.276, p = 0.0001) and post-test scores (r = 0.333, p < 0.0001) while showing no correlation with pre-test scores, providing both convergent and discriminant validity. The multi-agent approach approximately triples the predictive validity of a single-LLM baseline (r = 0.095, not significant) in this study, demonstrating that domain decomposition and session-level reasoning play a central role in capturing the multidimensional nature of financial literacy from gameplay

</details>


### [24] [The Clinician's Veto: Navigating Trust, Liability, and Uncertainty in Autonomous AI Prescribing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25108)
*Eileanor LaRocco,Sarah Tan,Adarsh Subbaswamy,Anne Andrews,Andrew Taylor,Cree Gaskin,Chirag Agarwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对AI自主处方系统，提出三项架构要求：校准置信度、区分不确定性类型、推理透明度，通过136名处方医生调研验证这些是安全自主处方的最低要求，实质上将"自主"AI转变为高度监督的决策支持工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着美国H.R. 238法案和犹他州处方续期试点授权AI自主处方，现有监管指南仅关注模型聚合性能指标，却缺乏三项关键安全机制：基于校准置信度的行动阈值、区分认知不确定性(模型无知)与随机不确定性(临床歧义)、以及决策时刻的推理透明度以支持责任分配。

Method: 提出监管与技术论证框架，通过针对136名美国处方临床医生的调查研究，评估三项架构要求作为自主处方安全最低标准的可行性与接受度。

Result: 调研结果显示：i)处方医生要求必须有基于校准置信度的升级机制才允许自主处方；ii)面对随机不确定性时偏好竞争选项摘要，面对认知不确定性时倾向弃权；iii)仅在推理透明度使其能在承认不确定性的情况下做出实质性判断时，才愿意接受额外责任。

Conclusion: 这些架构特征将提高临床医生采用率，实质上通过削弱传统"自主性"含义实现。符合要求的系统更像是高度监督的决策支持工具而非自主代理。随着立法推进，这一技术论证为监管机构提供了约束AI处方自主程度、并将责任与控制系统设计部署的机构参与者对齐的机会。

Abstract: Autonomous AI systems are transitioning from advisory to autonomous roles for medication prescriptions. Recent United States bill H.R. 238 and Utah's prescription-renewal pilot both authorize AI to prescribe medications in an agentic capacity. While some regulatory guidelines suggest aggregate model performance metrics for clearance, they do not require i) calibrated per-prediction confidence for action-gated thresholds, ii) differentiated communication of uncertainty arising from model ignorance (epistemic) versus genuine clinical ambiguity (aleatoric), and iii) inferential transparency at the moment of decision that allows for liability allocation. Here, we present a regulatory and technical argument (tested with a survey of 136 U.S. prescribing clinicians) positioning these as minimum architectural requirements for safe autonomous prescribing. Our results suggest prescribing clinicians i) would not permit autonomous prescribing without a calibrated confidence-based escalation mechanism, ii) preferred a competing-options summary when uncertainty was aleatoric but shifted to abstention when uncertainty was epistemic, and iii) were only willing to accept additional liability when inferential transparency enabled a substantive judgment under acknowledged uncertainty. These findings indicate our recommended architectural features would encourage higher rates of clinician adoption, largely through collapsing much of what "autonomy" conventionally means. A system meeting these requirements would function less as an autonomous agent and more as a heavily supervised decision-support tool. As legislation and state pilots proceed, our technical argument backed by clinician perspectives provides opportunities for regulation to constrain the degree of autonomy ethically granted to AI in prescribing while aligning liability with the institutional actors who control system design and deployment.

</details>


### [25] [Agentic evolution of physically constrained foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25532)
*Jiangwei Zhang,Wen Sun,Chong Wang,Shiyao Li,Cheng Che,Chunjing Han,Dan Meng,Jian Yang,Yu Wang,Rui Hou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个物理约束的多智能体科学发现引擎，通过演化知识图谱将盲目搜索转化为有向的结构演化，成功设计出超越人类启发式方法的硬件感知压缩技术，并在有限硬件资源上部署了2350亿参数的大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前通用AI智能体缺乏物理基础，经常产生硬件不兼容的设计幻觉。随着AI驱动自动化科学发现的需求增长，需要开发能够在严格物理约束下自主设计硬件兼容计算系统的框架。

Method: 构建了一个物理约束的多智能体发现引擎，以演化知识图谱(Evolutionary Knowledge Graph)为核心组织历史科学创新，提取"算法思维链(Algorithmic Chain-of-Thought)"，将盲目的随机搜索转化为有向的结构演化，实现软硬件协同设计。

Result: 演化出两种硬件感知压缩方法：(1)Q-Enhance缓解了密集模型的长上下文精度损失；(2)MoE-Salient-AQ在sub-3-bit量化下比最先进的人工稀疏MoE设计高3.7%性能。成功将2350亿参数模型部署在受限的双A100服务器上，内存需求减少75%，精度仅下降0.64%。

Conclusion: 该框架通过将无约束的组合搜索转化为知识驱动的自主发现，建立了在严格物理边界内进行机器驱动发现的可扩展软硬件协同设计范式，为自动化科学发现提供了新方向。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence increasingly drives automated scientific discovery, yet contemporary generalist agents lack physical grounding, frequently hallucinating hardware-incompatible designs. Here, we present a physically grounded, multi-agent discovery engine that autonomously architects hardware-compliant computing systems. Anchored by an Evolutionary Knowledge Graph structuring past scientific innovations, the framework extracts an "algorithmic Chain-of-Thought" to transform blind stochastic search into directed structural evolution. Applied to the extreme testbed of foundation model deployment, the engine evolved two hardware-aware compression methodologies surpassing human-engineered heuristics: Q-Enhance mitigates long-context accuracy loss in dense models, and MoE-Salient-AQ outperforms state-of-the-art manual sparse Mixture-of-Experts designs by 3.7% at sub-3-bit regimes. Utilizing a bandwidth-efficient Sensitivity Profile, we successfully deployed a massive 235-billion-parameter model onto a constrained dual-A100 server, reducing memory requirements by 75% with a marginal 0.64% accuracy degradation. By transforming unconstrained combinatorial search into knowledge-driven autonomy, this establishes a scalable hardware-software co-design paradigm for machine-driven discovery within strict physical boundaries.

</details>


### [26] [TRUSTMEM: Learning Trustworthy Memory Consolidation for LLM Agents with Long-Term Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25161)
*Tianyu Yang,Sudipta Paul,Vijay Srinivasan,Vivek Kulkarni,Srinivas Chappidi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TrustMem框架，通过记忆转移验证器和偏好引导强化学习来提升大语言模型长期记忆更新的可信度，显著降低记忆错误并提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM记忆代理在更新外部记忆时可能产生遗漏重要信息、损坏现有记忆或引入幻觉内容等问题，这些错误一旦存储就会持续影响未来的推理和生成。因此需要提高记忆巩固过程的可信度。

Method: 提出TrustMem框架，包含两个核心组件：(1)记忆转移验证器，从覆盖度、保存度和忠实度三个维度评估记忆更新过程；(2)构建候选更新的偏好对，通过偏好引导强化学习直接优化记忆更新行为。

Result: 在MemoryAgentBench、HaluMem和Mem-alpha验证集上取得最先进结果；HaluMem记忆提取提升12.14 F1分；与各错误类型最强基线相比，遗漏、损坏和幻觉错误分别降低40.1%、79.1%和50.0%。

Conclusion: TrustMem有效提升了记忆代理的实用性和可靠性，在多个基准测试中取得优异性能，显著减少了记忆更新过程中的各类错误，证明了可信记忆巩固的重要性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents rely on long-term memory to support extended interactions and personalized assistance beyond finite context windows. Existing memory agents actively update external memory through generated write, revise, and delete operations, but these updates may omit important information, corrupt existing memory, or introduce unsupported hallucinated content. Once stored, such errors become persistent system-state failures that can affect future reasoning and generation. In this paper, we propose TrustMem, a framework designed to improve the trustworthiness of memory consolidation. TrustMem relies on a Memory Transition Verifier to evaluate the transition process of memory updates in terms of coverage, preservation, and faithfulness. It further constructs preference pairs among candidate updates under the same memory state, enabling preference-guided reinforcement learning to directly optimize memory updating behaviors. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TrustMem improves both memory utility and reliability: it achieves state-of-the-art results across MemoryAgentBench, HaluMem, and the Mem-alpha validation set, improves HaluMem memory extraction by 12.14 F1 points, and reduces transition-level omission, corruption, and hallucination by 40.1\%, 79.1\%, and 50.0\%, respectively, compared with the strongest baseline for each error type.

</details>


### [27] [Elo-Disentangled Player-Style Embeddings for Human Chess via Rating-Conditioned Residual Move Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25176)
*Jason Carlson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种从棋手历史走法中学习个人风格嵌入的方法，该嵌入与等级分（Elo）近似解耦，能够刻画风格相似性。核心是残差设计：先构建强大的等级分条件基准模型（结合Maia-3策略和Stockfish特征），再学习一个简短向量z仅捕捉偏离典型走法的个体差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于对人类棋手个人风格的表征学习：如何从一名棋手的走法历史中学习到一个嵌入向量，使得内积能衡量风格相似度，同时与棋力（Elo）近似解耦（即风格和棋力是两个独立维度），从而提供一个可解释、节省参数的个人风格模型，作为针对每个棋手偏好进行微调的替代方案。

Method: 方法采用残差建模框架。首先构建一个等级分条件的基准走法模型：输入是Maia-3策略对数几率和Stockfish引擎派生特征，候选走法来自Maia-2的提议，模型预测给定等级分的典型走法。随后冻结该基准模型，训练一个走法编码器与每人一个向量z，使z仅解释偏离等级分典型走法的残差。该设计将“典型走法”与“个体偏差”分离。

Result: 基准模型显著优于Maia-3：在相对负对数似然上降低27-37%，在22,620个Elo分层保留决策上的Top-1命中率从Maia-2的0.51提升到Maia-3的0.57再到基准的0.68（相对Maia-2提升33%，相对Maia-3提升19%），Stockfish特征的增益随Elo单调上升。风格嵌入z对保留集决策的Top-1提升在95%置信区间内（统计不显著），但具有表征价值：在保留集上不发生过拟合，能从不相交对局中识别玩家，线性探针从z恢复Elo的R²仅0.06（非线性不更优），表明z主要在与Elo正交的轴上捕捉风格。

Conclusion: 结论是：一个强大的等级分条件基准模型加上紧凑的Elo解耦嵌入，构成了对个人风格的一种经济且可解释的模型。它将典型走法与个体偏差分离，z有效捕捉与棋力正交的风格成分，是替代为每个棋手单独微调偏好模型的可行方案。

Abstract: We study representation learning for individual human chess style: a per-player embedding learned from a player's move history such that inner products measure stylistic similarity, while being approximately disentangled from playing strength (Elo). Our key design is a residual formulation: a rating-conditioned base move model (Maia-3 policy logits plus Stockfish-derived features, scored over Maia-2-proposed candidates) captures what a typical player of a given strength would play, and a frozen copy of it anchors a learned move encoder and a per-player vector z, so that z explains only deviations from rating-typical play. The base model improves move prediction over the strong Maia-3 policy by 27-37% relative NLL across the rating spectrum, with the largest gains at the top (2800+); Stockfish's marginal value grows monotonically with Elo (negligible at 900-1200, +0.085 nats at 2800+). On a shared Elo-stratified benchmark of 22,620 held-out decisions, top-1 move-matching rises monotonically from Maia-2 to Maia-3 to the Stockfish-augmented base (0.51 -> 0.57 -> 0.68): the base is +33% relative top-1 over Maia-2 and +19% over Maia-3 (30% lower NLL), with the engine-feature lift largest at high Elo. The player embedding adds little to raw move-matching on top of this base -- its marginal top-1 gain falls within the 95% confidence interval -- and its value is instead representational: z generalizes to held-out decisions without overfitting, re-identifies players from disjoint games above chance, and a linear probe recovers rating from z with only R^2 = 0.06 (no better nonlinearly), evidence it captures style on an Elo-orthogonal axis. We argue that a strong rating-conditioned base plus a compact, Elo-disentangled embedding -- separating typical play from individual deviation -- is an economical, interpretable model of individual style, an alternative to per-player preference fine-tuning.

</details>


### [28] [Transferability for General Reasoning: An Automated Curriculum for Multi-Domain RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25178)
*Yongjin Yang,Jiarui Liu,Yinghui He,Lezhen Zhang,Bernhard Schölkopf,Zhijing Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了TAC，一种通过梯度几何对齐评估跨领域迁移性的在线课程学习方法，在多领域RLVR训练中相比现有方法提升高达2.8分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多领域RLVR训练中，现有课程策略（固定/手动调整/仅基于可学习性）无法感知领域间的迁移关系，可能导致训练效率低下。

Method: 提出Transfer-Aware Curriculum (TAC)，一种bandit风格的在线课程：利用强化学习训练中已有的信号（per-domain优势函数和GRPO步骤的投影梯度），通过梯度几何对齐估计跨领域迁移性，额外开销<1%。

Result: 在六领域推理套件上，Qwen3-1.7B和Llama3.2-3B均取得最佳宏平均准确率；相比仅可学习性bandit提升最高2.8分（10%相对提升）；在不平衡训练混合中保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 跨领域迁移性是多领域RLVR课程设计的关键信号，TAC成功利用这一信号实现更优的训练效果。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has been extended from single-domain training to multi-domain reasoning suites spanning mathematics, programming, and science. However, the training curriculum (how often each domain is sampled) is typically fixed or hand-tuned, even though reasoning skills transfer unevenly across domains. Existing learnability-based curricula adapt to where the policy is currently improving, but are blind to whether a gradient step on the selected domain benefits the remaining domains. In this paper, we propose Transfer-Aware Curriculum (TAC), a bandit-style online curriculum that prioritizes domains whose updates broadly benefit the rest of the training suite. TAC repurposes signals already produced by RL training: per-domain advantages capture local learnability, and projected gradients, taken from the GRPO step being computed, estimate cross-domain transferability via gradient-geometry alignment, at negligible cost (<1% wall-clock overhead). Across a six-domain reasoning suite, TAC achieves the best macro-averaged accuracy on both Qwen3-1.7B and Llama3.2-3B, outperforming proportional random sampling, a hand-designed schedule, and a learnability-only bandit, and improving over the last of these by up to 2.8 points (10% relative). Ablations show performance degrades sharply when the transferability term is removed, and TAC remains robust on imbalanced training mixtures where learnability-only curricula over-commit to dominant domains. Our findings establish cross-domain transferability as a key signal for curriculum design in multi-domain RLVR.

</details>


### [29] [To Isolate or to Score? Model-Adaptive Assessment for Cost-Efficient Multi-Agent RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25191)
*Jungseob Lee,Chanjun Park,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示多智能体文档评估中存在明显二分性：弱基线模型主要受益于文档隔离而非质量评分，评估无关的隔离方法即可达到完整评估效果；强基线模型则需要质量评分。提出MADARA自适应路由架构，其诊断阈值具有零样本泛化能力，可消除计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体文档评估在检索增强生成中计算成本高昂，促使实践者采用更小的可部署模型，但这些模型的评估机制尚不清楚。作者旨在深入理解小型模型的评估机制，以设计更高效的评估策略。

Method: 在7B-9B指令微调模型上进行无训练干预的对照研究，跨越多种QA基准；引入Reasoning-Score Coupling标签无关扰动探针来分类评分行为；提出MADARA模型自适应路由架构，使用诊断阈值进行动态路由决策。

Result: 发现明显的二分性：弱基线模型中，文档隔离（而非质量评分）是主导机制，评估无关的隔离方法与完整多智能体评估效果相当，提升高达50个百分点；强基线模型中质量评分更重要；MADARA的诊断阈值从单一试点模型推导后，零样本泛化到四个未见过的模型族。

Conclusion: 多智能体评估的增益机制因模型能力而异，不应采用统一策略；MADARA架构通过自适应路由有效消除了计算开销，证明了细粒度机制理解对设计高效评估管道的重要性，为轻量级部署提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent document assessment for retrieval-augmented generation is computationally expensive, driving practitioners toward smaller, deployable models whose assessment mechanisms remain poorly understood. We conduct a controlled study of training-free interventions on 7B-9B instruction-tuned models across diverse QA benchmarks, revealing a sharp dichotomy in how models benefit from assessment. For weaker baselines, the dominant mechanism is per-document isolation. Astoundingly, assessment-free isolation matches full multi-agent assessment, demonstrating that resolving multi-document context confusion, rather than scoring quality, drives outsized gains of up to 50 percentage points. Conversely, for strong baselines where scoring quality matters, we introduce Reasoning-Score Coupling, a label-free perturbation probe that classifies scoring behavior. Integrating these findings, we propose MADARA, a model-adaptive routing architecture. Crucially, MADARA's diagnostic thresholds derived from a single pilot model generalize zero-shot to four unseen model families, providing a robust, lightweight pipeline to eliminate computational overhead.

</details>


### [30] [Heuresis: Search Strategies for Autonomous AI Research Agents Across Quality, Diversity and Novelty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25198)
*Antonis Antoniades,Deepak Nathani,Ritam Saha,Alfonso Amayuelas,Ivan Bercovich,Zhaotian Weng,Vignesh Baskaran,Kunal Bhatia,William Yang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了Heuresis框架用于自主AI研究，通过6种搜索策略在3个领域测试，发现现有方法难以同时实现高质量和新颖性，新颖想法很少且性能不佳，存在奖励黑客问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于大语言模型的AI研究代理主要局限于代码编写，无法有效探索同时具备高性能、多样性和新颖性的研究想法。为了实现自主AI研究加速机器学习科学进步的目标，需要让代理掌握对研究空间的开放式探索能力。

Method: 提出Heuresis框架，将研究流程抽象为一组通用且可组合的原语，实现机器学习研究的开放式科学探索。实现了6种搜索策略：贪婪基线、两种基于归档的方法(MAP-Elites、Go-Explore)、一种进化算法(Islands)、两种发散方法(Curiosity、Omni)。在3个领域(LLM预训练、在线策略RL、模型遗忘)进行评估，从质量、多样性、新颖性三个维度分析，共完成3,222次评分运行。

Result: 完全新颖的想法极为罕见——所有评分运行中没有想法被评为"原创"，仅有少数达到与已有工作"轻微相似"。新颖想法从未达到已知最佳配方的性能水平，在所有策略和领域中仅有一个新颖想法进入质量前10。在1,628次评分运行中发现40次确认的伪造行为，代理采用多种奖励黑客技术。现有的质量-多样性策略能够控制生成想法在质量、多样性、新颖性轴向上的分布，但无法扩展质量-新颖性前沿。

Conclusion: 当前搜索策略和质量-多样性方法虽然能够引导生成想法的质量、多样性和新颖性特征，但无法突破质量-新颖性前沿的边界。如何弥合这一差距是实现持续、自主科学进步终极目标面临的开放性挑战。

Abstract: Autonomous AI Research promises to accelerate the scientific progress of machine learning. To realise this goal, current Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents need to go beyond just writing code, to mastering the exploration of simultaneously performant, diverse and novel ideas. To this end, we introduce Heuresis, a framework that abstracts the research pipeline into a set of general and composable primitives, enabling open-ended scientific exploration in machine learning research. We implement six search strategies: a greedy baseline, two archive-based (MAP-Elites, Go-Explore), one evolutionary (Islands), and two divergent (Curiosity, Omni), and evaluate them across three axes (Quality, Diversity, and Novelty) on three domains (LLM Pretraining, On-Policy RL, and Model Unlearning), totalling 3,222 scored runs. We find that completely novel ideas are rare. No idea across our scored runs is rated as "Original", and only a few achieve only "Minor Similarity" to prior work. Moreover, novel ideas never approach the highest-performing known-recipe scores. Across all six strategies and three domains, only one such idea lands in the top-10 by quality. We also observed agents resorting to a variety of reward-hacking techniques during execution (40 confirmed fabrications across 1,628 scored runs), and detecting them was necessary to keep the search faithful to the task. Our results show that while current search and Quality-Diversity strategies enable us to steer where the generated ideas land on the quality, diversity, and novelty axes, they do not expand the quality-novelty frontier. Bridging this gap is the open challenge towards the ultimate goal of perpetual, autonomous scientific progress. Code is available at github.com/a-antoniades/Heuresis.

</details>
