*Zhiyuan Han,Beier Zhu,Wenwen Tong,Pengyang Shao,Peipei Song,Xinyi Wang,Jiangnan Chen,Lewei Lu,Xun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了OPPO，一个用于优化多模态情感推理模型感知能力的强化学习框架，解决了现有模型在多模态线索利用不足和跨模态幻觉方面的问题，并在多个基准测试上取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者发现当前面向情感的全模态多模态大语言模型（Omni-MLLMs）存在两个关键问题：（i）在推理轨迹中对多模态线索利用不足；（ii）表现出不忠实的推理行为，经常从其他模态臆造特定模态的陈述。这些问题严重影响了模型在多模态情感推理任务中的可靠性。

Method: 提出了OPPO（Omni-Perception Policy Optimization）强化学习框架，包含两个核心组件：（1）全感知奖励：将真实推理分解为细粒度的视觉、声学和情感线索，奖励能够语义恢复这些线索的轨迹；（2）全感知损失：对比全输入和单模态遮蔽输入下的策略，仅对特定模态证据token施加KL惩罚，以抑制跨模态幻觉。此外，还提出了MEP-Bench诊断基准来量化利用度和忠实度。

Result: OPPO在MER-UniBench和MME-Emotion基准测试上达到了最先进的性能，同时在MEP-Bench上显著提升了利用度和忠实度评分，证明了充分且忠实的全感知对多模态情感推理的重要性。

Conclusion: 该研究强调了在多模态情感推理中，充分且忠实的全模态感知至关重要。OPPO框架通过显式优化多模态感知，有效解决了现有模型的关键局限性，为提升全模态大语言模型的可靠性提供了新的思路。

Abstract: We find that current emotion-oriented Omni-MLLMs still lack reliable omni-modal perception: they (i) underutilize multimodal cues in their reasoning trajectories and (ii) exhibit unfaithful behavior, often hallucinating modality-specific statements from other modalities. Building on these insights, we propose OPPO (Omni-Perception Policy Optimization), a reinforcement learning framework that explicitly optimizes multimodal perception. First, an Omni-Perception Reward decomposes ground-truth reasoning into fine-grained visual, acoustic, and emotion cues and rewards trajectories that semantically recover these cues. Second, an Omni-Perception Loss compares the policy under full and unimodally masked inputs, applying a KL penalty only to modality-specific evidence tokens to suppress cross-modal hallucination. We further introduce MEP-Bench, a diagnostic benchmark that quantifies utilization and faithfulness. Experiments show that OPPO achieves state-of-the-art performance on MER-UniBench and MME-Emotion, while substantially improving utilization and faithfulness scores on MEP-Bench, highlighting the importance of sufficient and faithful omni perception for multimodal emotion reasoning.

</details>


### [32] [What Actually Works for Spacecraft Fault-Tolerant Control: An Honest Settled-Gate Benchmark of Learned and Classical Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25374)
*Alireza Shojaei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了航天器执行器故障容错控制的基准测试，发现结构化的“估计-控制”设计（结合学习循环模块和解析控制律）在未见故障上表现最佳，并提出了扰动观测器来解决恒定偏差故障问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于学习的故障容错控制（FTC）研究通常在仿真环境中、针对有限的故障集进行评估，并使用瞬态指标（轨迹只需一次满足条件即可），这与实际航天任务中需要持续稳定保持的需求不符。因此，作者旨在探究在训练期间未见的故障情况下，何种方法能够真正实现航天器姿态指向的持续恢复，并建立严格的基准以评估不同控制器的泛化能力。

Method: 作者构建了一个基于“稳定门”（settled gate）的基准测试，要求姿态在时间窗口内持续保持在0.2度以内，评估指标基于真实状态。训练/测试集在惯性、增益、符号模式和偏差上严格分离。在Basilisk 6-DOF测试台上进行验证。研究涵盖了经典控制器、自适应控制器、端到端学习控制器和结构化控制器。核心方法为结构化的“估计-控制”设计：使用学习型循环模块在线推断执行器增益，并将其输入解析控制律。针对恒定偏差故障，引入扰动观测器从动力学中恢复偏差，并对增益估计误差具有自校正能力。

Result: 1. 不感知故障的PD/PID和从头训练的端到端强化学习（RL）方法成功率为0%。
2. 经典自适应律能解决符号故障，但在增益故障上表现不佳（55.2%）；文献中的Nussbaum增益律在符号和增益故障上的成功率分别为45.2%和3.2%。
3. 结构化的“估计-控制”设计在符号和增益故障上取得最佳表现，成功率分别为97.8%和94.4%，接近特权oracle的性能，而非结构化方法则无法处理。
4. 恒定加性偏差故障对所有控制器（包括特权增益oracle）都是最难的挑战，初始成功率均为0%。
5. 提出的扰动观测器与增益估计相结合，在恒定偏差故障上恢复了59.4%的成功率，且未回归符号/增益故障的性能。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，仅靠学习能力并非解决航天器故障容错控制的关键；结构化的估计-控制设计结合学习模块与解析控制律是实现强泛化能力的有效途径。对于恒定偏差故障，必须引入扰动观测器从动力学中估计偏差。研究还指出传感器偏差无法仅从受污染的测量中观测，需要融合其他信息。作者发布了该基准测试，以促进该领域的标准化评估与未来发展。

Abstract: Recent learned fault-tolerant-control (FTC) work reports high success on spacecraft actuator faults, but often in simulation, on narrow fault sets, and with transient metrics that a trajectory need only touch once. We ask what recovers spacecraft pointing when success means holding it on faults never seen in training. We answer with a benchmark built around a settled gate, pointing held within 0.2 deg over a dwell window and scored on the true state, train/test splits disjoint in inertia, gain, sign pattern, and bias, Wilson intervals over n=500 episodes per cell, and one-command reproduction on a 6-DOF Basilisk testbed. Across classical, adaptive, learned end-to-end, and structured controllers, three findings stand out. Fault-unaware PD/PID and from-scratch end-to-end RL score 0%, so learning capacity alone is not the lever. Classical adaptive laws resolve sign faults but handle gain poorly at 55.2%, and a literature-faithful Nussbaum-gain law reaches 45.2% and 3.2%. A structured estimate-then-control design, with a learned recurrent module that infers actuator gain online and feeds an analytic law, wins on sign and gain faults at 97.8% and 94.4%, approaching the privileged oracle while unstructured methods remain at zero. The hard wall is constant additive bias, which is 0% for every controller including the privileged gain oracle, because an integral-free law cannot null a constant disturbance. We close it with a disturbance observer that recovers bias from the dynamics and is self-correcting for gain-estimate error. Composed with the gain estimate, it recovers 59.4% of held-out bias faults with no sign/gain regression, moving that class off zero. We classify sensor-fault regimes similarly, show that sensor bias is unobservable from the corrupted measurement alone and therefore requires fusion rather than an observer, and release the benchmark so the gate is shared.

</details>


### [33] [Offline Multi-agent Continual Cooperation via Skill Partition and Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25389)
*Yuchen Xiao,Lei Yuan,Ruiqi Xue,Tieyue Yin,Yang Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出COMAD框架，通过技能分区与重用实现持续离线多智能体技能发现，有效解决了固定技能库在顺序任务设置中的分布偏移和干扰问题，在MARL基准测试中实现了优越的前向和后向迁移性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多智能体离线数据集中提取技能可提高学习效率，但在任务顺序出现且技能空间呈指数增长的环境中，现有基于启发式设计和固定大小技能库的方法难以解决分布偏移和干扰问题，面临灾难性遗忘和可塑性丢失的挑战。

Method: 首先使用自编码器从混合多智能体行为数据中发现技能，将协调知识转化为可重用的协调技能；然后构建多头架构的技能增强策略学习目标，通过基于密度的可重用性估计器识别可重用技能，显式引导优势函数。

Result: 理论分析表明该方法近似于持续技能发现问题的最优解；在多个MARL基准测试上的实证结果表明，COMAD能够持续扩展技能库以缓解干扰，相比多个基线方法在任务流中实现了更优的前向和后向迁移性能。

Conclusion: COMAD为开放环境中的多智能体系统提供了一个原则性框架，使智能体能够持续发现和重用协调技能，有效解决了持续学习中的灾难性遗忘和可塑性丢失问题。

Abstract: Extracting skills from multi-agent offline dataset improves learning efficiency via sharing task-invariant coordination skills among tasks. In settings where tasks occur sequentially and the space of skills grows exponentially, existing approaches that rely on heuristically designed and fixed-sized skill libraries struggle to resolve the problem of distributional shift and interference, facing catastrophic forgetting and plasticity loss. To address this problem and endow agents with the ability to continually discover and reuse coordination skills in open-environment, we propose COMAD, a principled framework for Continual Offline Multi-agent Skill Discovery via Skill Partition and Reuse. We first discover skills from mixed multi-agent behavior data with an auto-encoder to transform coordination knowledge into reusable coordination skills. Then we construct a skill-augmented policy learning objective with multi-head architectures, explicitly guiding the advantage function with reusable skills identified via a density-based reusability estimator. Theoretical analysis shows our method approximates the optimum of a continual skill discovery problem. Empirical results across diverse MARL benchmarks show that COMAD continually expands its skill library to mitigate interference, achieving superior forward and backward transfer for task streams compared to multiple baselines.

</details>


### [34] [Long-Term Simulation Exposes Cognitive-Developmental Risks in AI Companions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25396)
*Kaicheng Shen,Lingyu Li,Wen Wu,Yan Teng,Liang He,Yingchun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了TSJ框架用于评估AI伴侣对认知发展期用户（儿童、青少年）的长期风险，发现短时测试会系统性地低估风险，识别出幼儿期和成年早期为最脆弱阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI安全评估主要依赖单轮或短会话测试，无法捕捉长时间交互中才显现的风险，而大语言模型驱动的AI伴侣日益与认知发展中的用户（如儿童和青少年）互动，存在累积风险。

Method: 提出TSJ（Theater-Stage-Judge）纵向框架，结合角色驱动的用户模拟、动态心理状态更新和回溯性评估。在四个发展阶段、二十四个风险维度和三种心理脆弱性角色下评估六个主流模型，覆盖12,960个模拟人日交互。

Result: 短视测试系统性低估发展风险；TSJ在长期模拟关系中的140轮后才产生稳定的风险估计；幼儿期和成年早期被识别为最脆弱阶段；认知信任和情感依赖是最薄弱的领域。

Conclusion: TSJ为AI伴侣系统中的纵向认知发展风险评估提供了可扩展的方法论，有助于更好地理解和防范AI伴侣对认知发展期用户的长期潜在危害。

Abstract: AI companions powered by large language models increasingly interact with cognition-developing users, including children and adolescents, creating risks that may accumulate over time. Existing safety evaluations largely rely on single-turn or short-session tests, which cannot capture risks that emerge only through prolonged interaction. To address this gap, we propose TSJ (Theater-Stage-Judge), a longitudinal framework combining persona-driven user simulation, dynamic psychological-state updating and retrospective evaluation. We evaluate six mainstream models across four developmental stages, twenty-four risk dimensions and three psychological-vulnerability personas, covering 12,960 simulated person-day interactions. TSJ shows that short-horizon testing systematically underestimates developmental risks, for which TSJ yields a stable risk estimate only after 140 turns within prolonged simulated relationships. Applying TSJ further identifies early childhood and emerging adulthood as the most vulnerable stages, with cognitive trust and emotional dependency as the weakest domains. TSJ provides a scalable methodology for longitudinal cognitive developmental risk evaluation in AI companion systems.

</details>


### [35] [BrainAgent: A Large Language Model-Driven Multi-Agent Framework for Autonomous Brain Signal Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25400)
*Yangxuan Zhou,Sha Zhao,Jiquan Wang,Shijian Li,Gang Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了BrainAgent，一个基于大语言模型的多智能体框架，旨在通过将抽象的自然语言意图转化为可执行的端到端处理流程，降低脑信号分析的技术门槛并实现复杂任务的自动化，从而推动脑信号理解的普及化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管脑机接口（BCI）和脑信号理解在临床健康和下一代交互中具有重要意义，但在实际场景中的广泛应用仍受限制。主要原因在于当前的分析范式缺乏足够的智能代理能力：现有方法技术门槛高，需要大量专业知识；且方法本质上是静态和特定任务的，无法执行实际部署所需的复杂、长周期工作流。

Method: 提出BrainAgent，一个由大语言模型驱动的多智能体框架。该框架采用层次化架构，由中央监督者协调专门的子智能体，实现自适应的任务分解与执行，将抽象的自然语言意图转化为严谨、可执行的端到端处理流程。此外，还建立了首个全面、系统的基准，用于评估脑信号分析中的智能代理系统。

Result: 实验结果表明，BrainAgent能够以卓越的可靠性自动化执行复杂工作流，有效解决了传统方法的技术门槛高和静态任务限制问题。

Conclusion: BrainAgent标志着向普及化脑信号理解迈出了重要一步，代表了该领域的范式转变，为降低脑信号分析门槛和实现实际部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Brain-Computer Interfaces (BCIs) and brain signal understanding are pivotal for clinical health and next-generation interactions. Despite this significance, its widespread adoption in real-world scenarios remains restricted, primarily because current analytical paradigms lack sufficient agentic intelligence. First, existing methodologies impose prohibitive technical barriers, requiring extensive specialized expertise. Second, they remain inherently static and task-specific, failing to execute the complex, long-horizon workflows essential for real-world deployment. To accelerate the democratization of brain signal understanding, we draw inspiration from Large Language Models (LLMs) to introduce BrainAgent, an LLM-driven multi-agent framework designed to ground abstract natural language intent into rigorous, executable, and end-to-end processing pipelines. BrainAgent employs a hierarchical architecture where a central supervisor orchestrates specialized sub-agents for adaptive task decomposition and execution. Furthermore, we establish a comprehensive, systematic benchmark for evaluating agentic systems in brain signal analysis. Empirical results demonstrate that BrainAgent effectively automates complex workflows with superior reliability, marking a paradigm shift toward democratized brain signal understanding.

</details>


### [36] [Quantization Inflates Reasoning: Token Inflation as a Hidden Cost of Low-Bit Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25519)
*Xinyu Lian,Walid Krichene,Beichen Huang,Masahiro Tanaka,Olatunji Ruwase,Li Zhang,Minjia Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究发现低比特量化虽能保持推理模型的准确性，但会导致思维链变长，抵消了每词元加速带来的收益。建议在评估量化推理模型时，应同时报告推理词元使用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的量化评估方法（最终答案准确性和每词元延迟）未能全面捕捉量化对推理模型的影响，特别是量化可能引入的隐藏测试时计算成本尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 在数学推理、代码生成、科学问答和代理工具使用等基准测试上，对INT4/INT3量化模型与全精度模型进行比较分析；引入CoT Token Inflation Ratio指标来衡量推理长度变化；分析推理轨迹中的行为变化；评估提示工程、解码时采样和量化感知训练等缓解策略。

Result: INT4/INT3量化虽能保持准确性，但会增加推理词元使用量，抵消预期的每词元加速效果。量化导致推理轨迹发生行为变化，包括更多中间步骤和更大的语义重复。这些变化转化为可测量的端到端现实世界服务惩罚。量化感知训练在减少准确性下降和词元膨胀方面表现出更好的效果。

Conclusion: 量化推理模型的评估应同时报告推理词元使用量和准确性。量化感知训练相比提示工程和解码时采样，在缓解词元膨胀和准确性下降方面更具潜力。研究揭示了量化在推理模型中被忽视的隐藏成本。

Abstract: Quantization is widely used to reduce the inference cost of large language models, but its effect on reasoning models is not fully captured by final-answer accuracy or per-token latency. We show that low-bit post-training quantization can introduce a hidden test-time compute cost: quantized reasoning models often generate longer chains of thought even when they still answer correctly. Across mathematical reasoning, code generation, scientific question answering, and agentic tool-use benchmarks, we find that INT4/INT3 quantization can preserve accuracy but increase reasoning-token usage, offsetting the expected per-token speedup. To measure this effect, we introduce the CoT Token Inflation Ratio, which compares reasoning length between quantized and full-precision models averaged across all evaluation benchmarks. We further show that token inflation is accompanied by behavioral changes in the reasoning trace, including more intermediate steps and greater semantic repetition. These changes translate into measurable end-to-end real-world serving penalties. Finally, we evaluate mitigation strategies and find that prompting and decoding-time sampling offer inconsistent accuracy-length trade-offs, while quantization-aware training shows more promise in reducing both accuracy degradation and token inflation. Our results suggest that reasoning-token usage should be reported alongside accuracy when evaluating quantized reasoning models.

</details>


### [37] [Cliff Tokens: Identifying Single-Token Failure Triggers in LLM Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25524)
*Jaeyong Ko,Pilsung Kang,Yukyung Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“悬崖词元”概念，精确定位大语言模型数学推理中的失败触发点。删除首个悬崖词元并重采样可恢复pass@64至1.0，否则恢复受限；基于此的分类法与优化方法可提升模型准确率高达+6.6。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在数学推理任务上整体准确率高，但对同一问题的不同推理轨迹差异显著：部分轨迹正确，部分失败。已有工作在步骤、句子或已失败词元层面分析失败，均无法精确定位导致推理转向失败的触发词元。作者旨在识别并解释这一关键触发点。

Method: 基于词元级势能的相对下降，提出悬崖词元：在某词元处，词元级势能显著下降且超过自适应阈值，该阈值由局部势能缩放并通过单侧双比例z检验判定。进一步构建悬崖分类法：确定性悬崖、不确定悬崖与采样偏离悬崖，依据贪婪选择与词元熵定义。基于此提出Cliff-DPO方法，在悬崖位置进行单词元偏好优化。

Result: 在7个模型与3个数学推理基准（GSM1K、MATH500、AIME 2025）上，悬崖词元作为失败触发点得到验证：删除首个悬崖词元并重采样可将pass@64恢复至1.0，保留则恢复受限（0.71-1.00）。三种悬崖类型具有不同概率特性，分类法在模型尺度间泛化。Cliff-DPO在GSM8K训练后，各基准准确率提升最高达+6.6；优化不确定悬崖与采样偏离悬崖提升推理性能，确定性悬崖则无效。

Conclusion: 悬崖词元精确定位了大语言模型数学推理轨迹中的失败触发点，其分类法与优化策略有效且跨尺度泛化。针对特定悬崖类型的偏好优化可显著提升模型推理能力，为推理失败分析与模型改进提供了新路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) reach high accuracy in mathematical reasoning, but individual traces on the same problem diverge; some arrive at the correct answer while others fail. Prior work analyzes failure at the step, chunk, or sentence level, or at tokens where failure has already occurred. Neither identifies the precise token that triggers the shift toward failure. We introduce the cliff token, a token where the token-wise potential drops significantly under an adaptive threshold that scales with the local token-wise potential, based on a one-sided two-proportion z-test. Across seven models and three mathematical reasoning benchmarks (GSM1K, MATH500, AIME 2025), cliff tokens act as failure triggers; deleting the first cliff token and resampling recovers pass@64 to 1.0, while keeping it limits recovery to between 0.71 and 1.00. We further introduce a cliff taxonomy of deterministic, uncertain, and sampled-off cliffs, defined by greedy choice and token entropy. Each type has distinct probabilistic characteristics, and the taxonomy generalizes across model scales. Finally, we validate the taxonomy via single-token preference optimization at cliff positions (Cliff-DPO). Trained on GSM8K, Cliff-DPO improves accuracy across benchmarks by up to +6.6. Optimizing at uncertain and sampled-off cliffs improves reasoning, while deterministic cliffs do not.

</details>


### [38] [Reasonable Motion: A General ASP Foundation for Environment Constrained Movement Trajectory Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25626)
*Julius Monsen,Jakob Suchan,Mehul Bhatt,Lars Karlsson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于答案集编程的混合定量-定性方法，用于计算移动对象的约束分支轨迹模式，并在大规模真实自动驾驶数据集Argoverse 2上验证了其有效性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对移动对象（如自动驾驶车辆）在真实环境中轨迹计算的需求，现有纯学习方法缺乏可解释性和可验证性，难以提供轨迹背后的推理依据，因此需要一种既能处理复杂约束又能保证可解释性的轨迹计算方法。

Method: 采用基于答案集编程（ASP）的混合定量-定性方法，通过对环境图进行约束遍历，将几何上可接受的运动行为枚举为稳定模型。每个稳定模型代表一个独特的轨迹模式，由领域相关和领域无关因素（如派生事件序列、地图拓扑和领域规范）共同刻画。该方法结合了几何约束推理与逻辑规则建模。

Result: 在Argoverse 2大规模真实世界自动驾驶基准数据集上进行了实证评估，验证了方法的适用性。计算出的轨迹可追溯到其底层的稳定模型，提供了纯学习方法无法实现的可验证可解释性。方法能够有效处理多样化动态领域中典型的运动特征。

Conclusion: 所提出的混合轨迹计算方法具有广泛的适用性，能够处理多种动态场景下的运动特征。通过答案集编程提供的稳定模型机制，实现了轨迹的可追溯性和可解释性，弥补了纯学习方法在这一方面的不足，为安全关键应用（如自动驾驶）提供了可靠的决策支持。

Abstract: We present a general answer set programming based hybrid quantitative-qualitative method for computing constrained branching trajectory modes for moving objects in real-world settings. The method performs constrained traversal of an environment graph, enumerating geometrically admissible motion behaviours as stable models, each constituting a distinct trajectory mode characterised by both domain-dependent and independent factors such as derived event sequence, map topology, and domain norms. The hybrid trajectory computation method is generally applicable across motion characteristics typically encountered in diverse dynamic domains with moving objects, e.g., autonomous driving. We demonstrate applicability and highlight how computed trajectories are traceable to their underlying stable model, thereby affording verifiable interpretability that purely learned approaches cannot provide. We also perform an empirical evaluation with Argoverse 2, a large-scale real-world autonomous driving benchmark representative of the class of dynamic domains within the scope of the proposed method.

</details>

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly being used to automate tasks for users within an open GUI environment. They inevitably encounter screens containing user-sensitive information, for which takeover of task execution by the user is highly desirable or even necessary. State-of-the-art LLM-driven agents are usually fine-tuned to complete tasks regardless of the safety implications of their actions. This makes their real-world deployment difficult and adversely affects the reliability. Therefore, it is crucial to identify and categorize user-sensitive states and define user-sensitive queries. This dataset would be to engineers to recognize and request handover to the user in critical scenarios. This short paper develops an explorer agent that systematically explores the query space starting from one demonstrated task to identify queries that, if executed, would lead to user-sensitive states in a GUI environment.

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce position graphs, a graph-based reasoning framework based on the formalization of position spaces. This framework utilizes two strict partial orders, representing horizontal and vertical alignment and precedence, to model the relative positions of discrete tokens. Unlike general qualitative spatial calculi, position graphs are constrained by a chain condition and compatibility requirements that focus on rows and columns. We provide a comprehensive theoretical analysis of this representation, beginning with a characterization of graph consistency. Conditions to ensure the consistency of position graphs are established. Furthermore, we investigate the computational complexity of structural pattern discovery, modeled as the induced subgraph isomorphism problem. We demonstrate that this problem remains NP-complete even within the restricted class of position graphs. While initially motivated by document processing, this work focuses on the underlying mathematical properties and algebraic consistency of position-based constraints, providing a formal logical layer that is independent of specific data extraction techniques.

</details>

Abstract: This paper presents a versatile framework for evaluating fuzzy quantification queries over both standard and fuzzy ontologies as well as knowledge graphs. The primary objective is the retrieval of individuals that satisfy queries articulated via Type I or Type II fuzzy quantified expressions. A key advantage of the proposed approach is its inherent adaptability: it remains entirely agnostic to the quantifier type, the underlying evaluation method, and the specific data source of the ontology (i.e., OWL ontologies or RDFS knowledge graphs). Furthermore, we present Q2S2, a publicly accessible implementation of this system developed to support future research.

</details>

*Konstantin Kueffner,Tobias Meggendorfer,Maximilian Weininger,Patrick Wienhöft*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了专为在线统计模型检验设计的置信序列方法，相比现有技术平均减少50倍采样量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统马尔可夫决策过程假设已知精确的转移概率，但在实际应用（如信息物理系统、生物过程建模）中往往无法获得。现有统计方法存在实现不正确或效率低下的问题，迫切需要更高效的在线统计推断方法。

Method: 提出多种专门针对在线场景设计的置信序列方法，并将所有方法集成到高效的工具中实现。这些方法避免了经典联合界方法的缺陷，能够更紧凑地估计转移概率。

Result: 实验表明新方法优于经典的联合界方法，相比先前最先进的技术，平均需要减少50倍的采样数量即可获得相同质量的统计保证。

Conclusion: 所提出的置信序列方法为不确定环境下MDP的统计模型检验提供了高效、正确的解决方案，显著提升了实际应用的可行性。

Abstract: Markov decision processes (MDPs) are a classic model of decision making under uncertainty, exhibiting both non-deterministic choice as well as probabilistic uncertainty. Traditionally, exact knowledge of the underlying probabilities is assumed. However, this often is unrealistic, e.g.\ when modelling cyber-physical systems or biological processes. Here, statistical methods provide a way towards obtaining meaningful guarantees. The classical approach is to gather samples in the MDP, use these to draw statistical conclusions about the transition probabilities, and from there obtain bounds on the true value; then, if these bounds are too broad, repeat. However, existing implementations of this approach are either subtly incorrect or sub-optimal, and quite often both. We present several \emph{confidence sequences}, which are specifically designed for such \enquote{online} settings, implement all of them in an efficient tool, and show their practical applicability. In particular, we show that they outperform classical \enquote{union-bound} style approaches, and overall our implementation requires 50x less samples on average than previous state of the art.

</details>

*Hyejun Jeong,Dzung Pham,Amir Houmansadr,Eugene Bagdasarian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次提出并形式化定义了“智能体监控（Agentic Surveillance）”问题，通过构建SurveilBench数据集评估了AI智能体在不同场景下的监控能力，并开发了三种规避监控的技术，呼吁建立全面框架来保护用户隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体的广泛应用带来了一种新的风险：拥有数据访问权限的智能体可能被滥用于监控用户行为。用户可能缺乏控制或阻止此类监控的能力，这一问题在雇主或国家提供的AI系统中尤为突出，亟需研究其可行性与防范方法。

Method: 1) 形式化定义“智能体监控”问题，即AI智能体分析可用信息、撰写报告并通过可用工具发送的能力；2) 构建SurveilBench数据集，涵盖企业、教育和警务三个领域的多样化报告场景以评估模型监控能力；3) 将提示注入技术重新用于反监控，开发三种规避技术：隐藏、欺骗和诱导过度升级。

Result: 实验发现部分模型表现出涌现性的（即未被提示的）协助监控倾向，但同时也会向政府报告监控尝试。所提出的三种规避技术在隐藏、欺骗或干扰监控智能体方面展现出有效性，证明了现有AI系统已具备实施监控的能力。

Conclusion: 智能体监控已经可以被轻易实现，这构成了实质性隐私威胁。研究呼吁建立涵盖技术、伦理和立法层面的综合性框架，以保护用户免受此类监控风险的侵害。

Abstract: To better assist users with completing challenging tasks, AI agents mediate communications, access data, and interact with different APIs. Many employers (and even nation-states) already provide their users with this technology. However, widespread adoption of AI agents creates a new risk to abuse access to user data for another goal: surveilling users. These users might not even have the ability or permission to control the actions and data accesses of the surveilling agents.
  We introduce and formalize the problem of agentic surveillance: the ability of an AI agent to analyze available information, craft a report, and send it out using available tools. To evaluate surveillance capabilities across different models, we create SurveilBench, a dataset of various reporting scenarios focusing on three domains: corporate, education, and police. We find that some models exhibit emergent (i.e., unprompted) tendencies to help surveillance, but they also report the attempts to surveil users to the government.
  Finally, we repurpose prompt injections for evading surveillance and develop three evasion techniques that hide from, deceive, or induce over-escalation in surveillance agents. We conclude that agentic surveillance can already be easily implemented and, therefore, call for a comprehensive technical, ethical, and legislative framework to protect users.

</details>

*Zihan Qin,Hongrui Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于“压缩即智能”的分析框架，通过编码长度度量智能体系统的性能，在五个实验场景中验证了智能体组件能有效减少编码长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型正从孤立预测器转变为智能体系统，具备调用工具、检索证据、遵守环境约束等能力。然而，如何系统地分析和评估这些智能体系统的性能尚缺乏理论框架。本文旨在基于信息论中的“压缩即智能”观点，提出一种量化评估智能体系统的方法。

Method: 采用算术编码、种子编码和回退机制来操作化编码长度度量。在固定任务分布、接口和计算预算的条件下，更强的智能体系统应能用更少的比特重建目标对象。在五个受控实验场景中进行评估：反向文本、国际象棋走法、蛋白质序列、检索增强问答和语义故事压缩。

Result: 在所有五个实验场景中，引入智能体组件（如工具调用、证据检索、验证器等）均能显著减少编码长度。这些小型受控实验涵盖了典型智能体系统的组件类型，证明了编码长度可以有效分析组件、观察者和预算如何改变残余不确定性。

Conclusion: 编码长度可作为分析和评估智能体系统的有效指标，为理解智能体组件如何降低任务不确定性提供了理论视角，并为实际智能体系统的评估和设计提供了指导原则。该框架有助于更系统地理解和改进智能体系统的架构。

Abstract: Large language models are turning from isolated predictors into agentic systems: they call tools, retrieve evidence, obey environment constraints, use verifiers, and complete tasks through search and multi-turn interaction. We adopts an analytical viewpoint based on "compression is intelligence": under a fixed task distribution, interface, and compute budget, a stronger agentic system lets a target object be reconstructed with fewer bits. We operationalize the measure with arithmetic coding, seed coding, and a fallback, and evaluate it in five settings: reversed text, chess moves, protein sequences, retrieval-augmented question answering, and semantic story compression; in all of them agentic components reduce codelength. These small, controlled experiments cover component types typical of real agentic systems, show that codelength can analyze how components, observers, and budgets change residual uncertainty, and offer guidance for evaluating real agent systems.

</details>


### [45] [WinDOM: Self-Family Distillation for Small-Model GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25964)
*Chengheng Li-Chen,Zhiqian Zhou,Hao Chen,Nicolas Chauvin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了WinDOM数据集和SFD训练方法，使2B小模型GUI定位性能显著提升。关键发现是欠饱和的冷启动模型比收敛模型更适合作为强化学习初始化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小规模（约2B）GUI定位模型在设备端部署、辅助工具和低成本迭代方面具有吸引力，但面临两大挑战：如何在没有昂贵人工标注的情况下获取边界框训练数据，以及如何将监督微调与强化学习有效结合。本文旨在提升小模型性能而非简单扩大模型规模。

Method: 1) WinDOM：包含54,425条记录的定位语料库，通过在headless Playwright下驱动开源Windows 11 Web重实现来收集，边界框直接从DOM读取，无需OCR或人工标注；2) SFD（Self-Family Distillation）：单次拒绝采样冷启动方法，可选择EMA学生模型（无需外部模型）或冻结的同家族更大教师模型；3) 将SFD冷启动的饱和深度作为GRPO显式超参数，研究欠饱和与收敛冷启动对强化学习初始化的影响。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-2B学生模型上，欠饱和冷启动比收敛冷启动更适合作为GRPO初始化：SFD-4B配合Early-init RL相比基础模型在OOD均值上提升+5.4（ScreenSpot-Pro +3.5，OSWorld-G +7.0，ScreenSpot-V2 +5.8）。同尺寸EMA模式在无外部教师的情况下达到OOD均值约65.2，接近跨尺寸4B变体的66.3。

Conclusion: 证明了小规模GUI定位模型可以通过自动化数据收集和精心设计的蒸馏-强化学习流程获得显著性能提升。关键洞察是欠饱和的教师模型状态能提供更好的强化学习初始化，而非传统的收敛模型。这为在资源受限场景下构建高性能GUI智能体提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Small ($\sim$2B) GUI-grounding agents are attractive for on-device deployment, accessibility tooling, and low-cost iteration, but at this scale they face two open recipe questions: how to obtain bounding-box training data without expensive human annotation, and how to combine supervised fine-tuning with reinforcement learning. We address both, with the explicit goal of pushing small-model performance rather than scaling up. WinDOM is a $54{,}425$-record grounding corpus harvested by driving an open-source Windows 11 web reimplementation under headless Playwright, with bounding boxes read directly off the DOM and no OCR or human annotation. Self-Family Distillation (SFD) is a single rejection-sampling cold-start parameterised only by the teacher choice: either an EMA of the student (no external model) or a frozen larger same-family teacher. We then treat the saturation depth of the SFD cold-start as an explicit GRPO hyperparameter. On a Qwen3.5-2B student, the under-saturated cold-start is a better GRPO initialiser than the converged one: SFD-4B with Early-init RL gains $+5.4$ OOD-mean ($+3.5$ ScreenSpot-Pro, $+7.0$ OSWorld-G, $+5.8$ ScreenSpot-V2) over the base. The same-size EMA mode lands within roughly one OOD-mean point of the cross-size $4$B variant ($65.2$ vs $66.3$) without an external teacher.

</details>


### [46] [InvestPhilBench: A Multi-Layer Dynamic Benchmark for Evaluating Large Language Model Procedural Reasoning in Expert Investment Philosophy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25984)
*Mingguang Chen,Bo Qu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了InvestPhilBench基准，用于测试大语言模型作为投资研究助手时是否能够准确重建和应用专家投资者的程序化决策框架。发现现有复合评分方法会掩盖模型在程序推理上的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地被部署为投资研究助手，但目前缺乏基准测试来评估它们是否能准确重建和应用专家投资者的特定程序化决策框架。现有评估方法可能无法有效衡量模型在投资推理方面的真实能力。

Method: 构建了一个多层动态基准InvestPhilBench，涵盖从原则识别(L1)到新颖框架外推(L8)的八个认知层级。v0.6版本包含118张经原始来源验证的投资原则卡、25张带有明确拓扑元数据的决策框架卡以及243个QA问题。引入了基准自动评分管道(BASP)，包含五种算法指标(OGRS, KCCS, SAP@k, IVP, CKCA)、失效模式检测协议(FMDP)以及门重建准确率(GRA)指标。

Result: 在188题开发集上的四模型测试显示了明显的提供商层级差异(BASP 0.906 vs 0.438)。关键发现：BASP复合得分在前沿模型上趋于饱和(Claude L4 = 0.932)，而GRA仍暴露出程序化缺陷(前沿模型L4 GRA约0.77，L7 GRA 0.57-0.62)。复合评分会奖励流畅的文本而掩盖程序化差距。在100项专家标注的金标准集上，BASP复合得分与人类参考值的相关性为Pearson r = 0.72 (MAE = 0.10)。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了现有复合评分方法的一个关键问题：它们可能掩盖模型在程序推理方面的实质性缺陷。InvestPhilBench v0.6主要贡献了基准和方法论，去混淆的多模型排行榜和完整三条件运行将作为v1.0版本的交付成果。该基准为评估LLM在投资决策框架重建方面的能力提供了更准确的方法。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed as investment research assistants, yet no benchmark tests whether they can accurately reconstruct and apply the specific procedural decision frameworks of expert investors. We introduce InvestPhilBench, a multi-layer dynamic benchmark spanning eight cognitive tiers, from principle identification (L1) to novel framework extrapolation (L8). The v0.6 release comprises 118 primary-source-verified investment principle cards, 25 decision framework cards with explicit topology metadata, and 243 QA questions (197 dev / 46 held-out test). For reproducible scoring at scale we introduce the Benchmark Automated Scoring Pipeline (BASP) -- five algorithmic metrics (OGRS, KCCS, SAP@k, IVP, CKCA) -- the Failure Mode Detection Protocol (FMDP) with computable rules for six failure modes, and Gate Reconstruction Accuracy (GRA), a per-gate metric for questions with gold reasoning programs. In this release, InvestPhilBench is primarily a benchmark-and-methodology contribution. A four-model sanity wave on the 188-question development split shows a sharp provider-tier split (BASP 0.906 vs. 0.438); these mixed-judge numbers are confounded upper bounds. The central finding: the BASP composite saturates at the frontier (Claude L4 = 0.932) while GRA still exposes a procedural deficit (frontier L4 GRA approx. 0.77, L7 GRA 0.57-0.62) -- composite scoring rewards fluent prose and hides the procedural gap. v0.6 implements a unified judge and true model-in-the-loop retrieval/oracle conditions; the de-confounded multi-model leaderboard and full three-condition run are v1.0 deliverables. On a 100-item expert-annotated gold set the automated BASP composite tracks the human reference at Pearson r = 0.72 (MAE = 0.10), with attribution (SAP@3) the weakest sub-metric and the failure-mode detector running sensitive-but-over-flagging.

</details>

Conclusion: Agentic数据创建方法成功实现了将增加的推理计算转化为更高质量的模型训练。这一方向具有改变AI数据构建方式的潜力，为未来AI系统自主创建高质量训练数据提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We introduce Autodata, a general method that enables AI agents to act as data scientists who build high quality training and evaluation data. We show how to train (meta-optimize) such a data scientist agent, so that it learns to create even stronger data. We describe the overall formulation, and a specific practical implementation, Agentic Self-Instruct. We conduct experiments on computer science research tasks, legal reasoning tasks and reasoning with mathematical objects, where we obtain improved results compared to classical synthetic dataset creation methods. Further, meta-optimizing the data scientist agent itself delivers an even larger performance uplift. Agentic data creation provides a way to convert increased inference compute into higher quality model training. Overall, we believe this direction has the potential to change the way we build AI data.

</details>


### [48] [The Unfireable Safety Kernel: Execution-Time AI Alignment for AI Agents and Other Escapable AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26057)
*Seth Dobrin,Łukasz Chmiel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对可逃逸AI系统（控制机制位于智能体运行时内部易被绕过），提出了不可逃逸安全内核架构，通过进程隔离、预动作强制执行、故障关闭和外部化签名证据四项属性实现执行时AI对齐。Rust实现经机器验证（Z3定理证明和Kani有界模型检验），在实际可逃逸AI系统中成功阻止了所有704次逃逸尝试，验证了架构控制的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体控制方法（系统提示、输出过滤器、护栏库）均部署在智能体自身运行时内部，形成可逃逸AI系统，任何影响输入的攻击者都可能绕过这些控制机制。需要一种架构级的控制机制，而非依赖智能体配合的请求式控制。

Method: 识别授权机制必须满足的四项属性：(1)进程隔离，(2)结构唯一路径上的预动作强制执行，(3)请求和系统层面的故障关闭，(4)可在受控系统信任边界外验证的外部化签名证据。提出不可逃逸安全内核作为执行时AI对齐层，用Rust实现参考实现，并通过Z3 SMT定理证明器和Kani有界模型检验进行机器验证。

Result: 在控制实时可逃逸AI系统（确定性自改进世界模型）对抗逃逸寻求攻击者的评估中：1000次自修改中所有704次针对安全关键核心的尝试均被拒绝，无逃逸发生；操作员终止开关下的300次也被拒绝；6240次授权往返无成功绕过。与3个声称控制智能体的现代系统对比，本方法使智能体无法调用控制权。Python到Rust迁移通过1000/1000测试用例和17/17对抗类验证字节等价性。

Conclusion: 不可逃逸安全内核成功实现了架构级AI控制，补充了训练时对齐（RLHF、宪法AI）和推理时对齐，为可逃逸AI系统提供了有效的执行时对齐机制，证明了进程隔离和故障关闭架构在防止AI智能体逃逸方面的可行性。

Abstract: AI agents are granted access to tools, APIs, and other infrastructure, making them active principals in those systems. The dominant approach places controls inside the agent's own runtime: system prompts, output filters, and guardrail libraries. Any control in the agent's address space is reachable by inputs that influence it; this generalizes to any AI system with sufficient reach into its own runtime, a class we term escapable AI systems.
  We identify four properties that an authorization mechanism must satisfy for architectural control rather than for cooperative requests: process separation, pre-action enforcement on a structurally only path, fail-closed at both the request and system levels, and externalized signed evidence verifiable outside the controlled system's trust boundary. We position this layer as execution-time AI alignment, complementing training-time alignment (RLHF, Constitutional AI) and inference-time alignment.
  We present the Unfireable Safety Kernel, a Rust reference implementation realizing all four. Its fail-closed invariant is machine-checked at two levels: an SMT theorem (Z3) and an exhaustive bounded-model-checking proof of the production decision function (Kani, 4/4 harnesses). A Python-to-Rust migration was gated on byte-equivalence (1000/1000 fixtures; 17/17 adversarial classes). We evaluate the kernel governing a live, escapable AI system, a deterministic, self-improving world model, against an escape-seeking adversary driving its real self-modification seam: across 1,000 self-modifications, all 704 attempts on the safety-critical core are refused, with no escape; a further 300, under the operator kill switch, are also refused. A separate campaign of 6,240 authorization round-trips had no successful bypass. Against 3 contemporary systems claiming the agent control plane, the agent invokes control; here, it lacks that choice.

</details>


### [49] [BluTrain: A C++/CUDA Framework for AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24780)
*Adhitya Charan,Adwaid Suresh,Anuj Kumar,Aparna A,Dhanakumar K,Dharun M S,Dinesh G,Goutham Kumar Reddy K,Harshini V M,Jenifa D,Jona Delcy C A,Kathirvel S,Killi Uma Maheswara Rao,Kiruthik Kanna M,Kurra Vishnu Sai,Madhumithaa G K,Navin Kumar,Ram Charan Golla,Revathi T,Rishikkanth R,Sanjay Krishna M,Surendra Vendra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BluTrain是一个用标准C++和CUDA从第一性原理构建的轻量级通用深度学习训练框架，在124M参数GPT-2训练中，以407K tokens/s吞吐量和22%内存优化超越PyTorch基线，同时保持数值精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习训练性能主要取决于系统级工程实现而非模型架构本身。现有框架在硬件表达能力、系统复杂度抽象和避免重复编排逻辑方面存在不足，需要从底层重新设计一个能精确控制硬件表达、抽象系统复杂性并支持原生调优的训练框架。

Method: 采用标准C++和核心CUDA编程模型从第一性原理构建框架，每一层均原生实现：包含带反向模式自动微分的类型化张量模块、线性代数库、缓存分配器、多模式分布式执行模块，以及基于MLIR的深度学习编译器，实现对硬件表达的绝对控制。

Result: 在8卡RTX 6000 Ada系统上训练124M参数GPT-2模型（FP32）的评估中，BluTrain达到平均407K tokens/s吞吐量（PyTorch为395K tokens/s），内存占用降低高达22%，同时严格保持数值精度并收敛至略低的最终验证损失。

Conclusion: BluTrain证明了从底层原生构建训练框架的有效性，在吞吐量和内存效率上均超越行业标准基线。框架每层均开放原生调优，其性能天花板由框架自身决定，为深度学习训练系统优化提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Progress in deep learning is, at scale, more a matter of systems engineering than of modelling: the behaviour of a model in training (its throughput, its memory footprint, and the numerical fidelity of the result) is determined less by the architecture itself than by how that architecture is expressed on the hardware. To achieve absolute control over this hardware expression while abstracting away systems complexity to make modelling seamless and eliminating the need for repetitive orchestration logic, BluTrain was architected from first principles as a robust, lightweight, and architecture-general training framework in standard C++ and the core CUDA programming model. Every layer is implemented natively: a typed tensor module with reverse-mode autograd, a linear-algebra library, a caching allocator, a multi-mode distributed-execution module, and an MLIR-based deep-learning compiler. In formal evaluations training a 124M-parameter GPT-2 baseline in FP32 on an 8-GPU 6000 Ada system, BluTrain outperforms industry-standard baselines in both throughput (sustaining an average of 407K tokens/s versus PyTorch's 395K tokens/s) and memory efficiency (achieving up to a 22% footprint reduction), while strictly preserving numerical fidelity and converging to a marginally lower final validation loss. With every layer explicitly open to native tuning, the performance ceiling is the framework's own to raise.

</details>

### [50] [Energy Efficient Scheduling of AI/ML Workloads on Multi Instance GPUs with Dynamic Repartitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25082)
*Ellie Lipe,Neel Karia,Connor Espenshade,Clifford Stein,Asser Tantawi,Olivier Tardieu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于强化学习的MIG动态重分区调度框架，在真实数据中心工作负载模式下，相比静态分区节能31%，相比无分区提升68%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI/ML工作负载导致数据中心能耗急剧上升。NVIDIA的多实例GPU(MIG)技术提供了灵活性和计算能力的改进，为数据中心在保持可接受性能的同时以节能方式管理工作负载提供了机会。然而，在MIG环境中通过作业调度实现能耗和性能的多目标优化面临两个挑战：1)需要为给定MIG配置设计简单易实现的调度算法来选择作业和分配切片；2)随着工作负载波动，特定MIG配置可能需要重新分区。

Method: 使用仿真和强化学习(RL)方法解决上述两个问题。首先将单MIG的动态重分区调度框架建模为具有抢占的多目标异构机器调度问题。比较了四种调度算法并识别出最优算法。然后采用强化学习方法在一天内执行动态重分区决策。使用基于真实数据中心跟踪数据的昼夜工作负载模式进行验证。

Result: 在多目标函数(能耗和延迟)下，动态重分区算法显著优于其他策略：比每日两次重分区提升26%，比静态分区提升31%，比无分区提升68%。结果表明在不同时间和队列条件下存在特定的偏好配置，为预测性和自动重新配置提供了策略指导。

Conclusion: 动态重分区调度框架能够有效平衡能耗和性能目标。强化学习方法能够根据工作负载变化自动调整MIG配置，在一天中的不同时间和队列条件下选择最优配置。研究为数据中心的预测性和自动化GPU重配置管理提供了可行的策略方案。

Abstract: Increasing demand from AI/ML workloads is exacerbating the rising energy consumption of data centers. Recent advances in hardware such as NVIDIA's Multi Instance GPUs (MIGs) offer improvements in flexibility and computational power and the opportunity for data centers to manage incoming jobs in energy-efficient ways, while maintaining acceptable performance. The challenge in achieving this multi-objective in a MIG environment through job scheduling is multi-faceted. Firstly, for a given MIG configuration, one seeks an easy-to-implement scheduling algorithm which selects a job from the queue as well as decides on which slice in the configuration the job runs. Secondly, for the identified scheduling algorithm, a particular MIG configuration may not always be suitable (as the workload fluctuates) and may need to be repartitioned. We tackle both problems using simulations and reinforcement learning (RL). We present a dynamic repartitioning scheduling framework for a single MIG as a solution to a multi-objective heterogeneous machine scheduling problem with preemption. In particular, we compare four scheduling algorithms and identify a promising one. Then, we employ reinforcement learning to perform dynamic repartitioning over a day. Furthermore, using a diurnal workload pattern based on real-world data center traces, we demonstrate the superiority of our dynamic repartitioning algorithm over twice-daily repartitioning ($26\%$), static partitioning ($31\%$) and no partitioning at all ($68\%$) according to a multi-objective function of energy consumption and tardiness. Our results indicate specific preferred configurations at different times of the day under different queue conditions, suggesting a policy for predictive and automatic reconfiguration.

</details>


### [51] [Speculation at a Distance: Where Edge-Cloud Speculative Decoding Actually Pays Off](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25091)
*Yuan Lyu,Bharath Irukulapati,Jaya Prakash Champati*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 分布式推测解码(DSD)在WAN环境下单请求延迟优势有限，但在多租户场景下可提升服务器容量和吞吐量，应从多租户角度而非仅从延迟角度评估其价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码(SD)在模型共存时可加速LLM推理1.5-3倍，促使研究者探索将草稿模型置于边缘设备、目标模型留在云端的分布式变体(DSD)。作者旨在分析DSD在WAN边缘-云通信环境下的实际性能表现和适用场景。

Method: 通过闭式不等式分析DSD在WAN环境下的延迟限制，对比共存SD、同步DSD和流水线DSD的性能；分析DSD在多租户场景下的容量增益，推导跨客户端重叠时的并发客户端数量提升公式。

Result: (1)在WAN RTT下，共存SD延迟和通信均低于同步DSD；(2)流水线DSD仅在RTT短于边缘起草时间窗口时有竞争力；(3)DSD相对于云端自回归解码的延迟降低存在有界窗口；(4)DSD不适用于无验证器接口的闭源API；(5)在多租户场景下，DSD可使饱和云服务器承载(1+γ·td/tv)倍并发客户端。

Conclusion: DSD的主要价值在于多租户容量提升而非单请求延迟降低。卸载草稿计算可让饱和云服务器在保持单客户端速率的同时支持更多并发用户。DSD应主要从多租户容量和服务器吞吐量角度评估，而非仅关注单请求延迟。

Abstract: Speculative decoding (SD) accelerates LLM inference by $1.5$-$3$ times when the draft and target models are co-located. This has motivated a distributed variant (DSD) that places the draft model on an edge device while the target stays in the cloud. We show with closed-form inequalities that DSD's per-request latency benefit is limited under WAN edge-cloud communication. If the server can host both models, co-located SD has lower latency and communication than synchronous DSD, with the same per-output FLOPs and model-weight memory. Pipelining can make DSD competitive with co-located SD only in low-RTT regimes where the round trip is shorter than the edge drafting time window; at WAN RTTs, the cloud round trip remains too large for pipelined DSD to beat co-located SD. Against cloud autoregressive decoding, DSD can reduce latency only inside a bounded window given the target-model speed, acceptance rate, and RTT. DSD is also infeasible against closed-source APIs without a verifier-only interface. The main case for DSD appears in multi-tenant capacity. Under cross-client overlap, offloading draft compute lets a saturated cloud server sustain $(1 + γ\,t_d/t_v)$ times more concurrent clients at the same per-client rate, where $γ$ is the speculation length and $t_d, t_v$ are the per-step draft and verification times. DSD should therefore be evaluated primarily by multi-tenant capacity and server throughput, not only by single-request latency.

</details>


### [52] [Power-Flexible AI Data Centers: A New Paradigm for Grid-Responsive Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25098)
*Chris Williams,Philip Colangelo,Ayse Coskun,Ethan Levine,Andy Neale,Ciaran Roberts,Shayan Sengupta,Nikhil Shirolkar,Varun Sivaram,Sarah Soares,Ethan Tiao,Scott Underwood,Daniel Wilson,Frank Sharp,Luke Wainwright,Harry Petty,Scott Wallace,Brandon Records*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出将现代GPU集群作为电网互动资源，通过软硬件协同实现灵活用电调控，在真实130kW集群上验证了该方案能有效支持电网可靠性并加速并网进程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能基础设施的快速扩张导致数据中心用电需求激增。传统电力系统规划将大型计算设施视为不可调度的峰值负荷，造成昂贵的设施升级和漫长的并网延迟。为解决这一挑战，需要探索将AI数据中心转化为灵活电网资源的可行性。

Method: 提出一种集成电网信号、工作负载调度和功率遥测的架构，实现对GPU集群功率的细粒度控制。通过软件工作负载编排技术，实现多种灵活性操作，包括快速负荷削减、持续限电、碳感知运行以及跨地理分布式集群的性能感知负荷迁移。

Result: 在真实部署的130kW GPU集群上的实验表明：系统能够实现快速负荷削减、持续限电和碳感知运行，同时在为优先任务保持服务水平的前提下，还能实现跨地理分布式集群的性能感知负荷迁移，使工作负载向电网压力较低的区域迁移。

Conclusion: 这些能力将AI基础设施从静态电力消费者转化为灵活资源，能够支持电网可靠性、加速并网进程并提高计算可持续性，为解决AI发展与电网规划之间的矛盾提供了有效途径。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of artificial intelligence (AI) infrastructure is driving unprecedented growth in electricity demand from data centers. Traditional power-system planning treats large computing facilities as inflexible peak loads, leading to costly infrastructure upgrades and long delays in grid interconnection. Recent work has shown that AI clusters can reduce electricity consumption during peak demand through software-based workload orchestration. This article explores how modern GPU-based AI data centers can operate as grid-interactive assets that respond dynamically to power system conditions. We describe an architecture integrating grid signals, workload scheduling, and power telemetry for fine-grained cluster power control. Experimental results from a real-world deployment on a 130 kW GPU cluster demonstrate multiple forms of flexibility, including rapid load reduction, sustained curtailment, and carbon-aware operation while preserving service levels for priority jobs. We further demonstrate performance-aware load shifting across geographically distributed clusters, enabling workloads to migrate toward regions with lower grid stress. Together, these capabilities transform AI infrastructure from static electricity consumers into flexible resources that support grid reliability, accelerate interconnection, and improve computing sustainability.

</details>


### [53] [Ambulance: saving BFT through racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25099)
*Neil Giridharan,Shubham Mishra,Lorenzo Alvisi,Natacha Crooks,Benjamin Marsh,Hein Meling,Kartik Nayak,Grzegorz Prusak*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Ambulance是一种BFT状态机复制协议，通过协议驱动的副本竞争机制，在保持高吞吐量和低延迟的同时，实现了对系统减速的快速恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的实用拜占庭容错(BFT)状态机复制部署在面对系统减速时存在脆弱性。核心问题在于超时机制：激进的超时会错误地触发昂贵的领导者变更，而保守的超时则导致系统空闲并让延迟严重增加。现有替代方案如对冲方法仍存在时间延迟，协作异步协议则面临高延迟和低吞吐量的问题。

Method: 提出Ambulance协议，采用协议安排的竞争(Protocol-rigged Races)机制。副本之间不与时钟竞争，而是通过执行协议步骤相互竞争。这种设计使协议能够快速检测和处理系统减速，而无需依赖传统的超时机制。

Result: Ambulance实现了与最先进的基于超时的BFT协议相当的高吞吐量和低延迟性能，同时具备与协作方法相当的鲁棒性，能够快速从系统减速中恢复。

Conclusion: 通过协议驱动的竞争机制，Ambulance成功规避了传统超时机制带来的性能与鲁棒性之间的权衡问题，为BFT状态机复制提供了一种既高效又鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Today's practical Byzantine Fault Tolerant (BFT) state machine replication deployments are vulnerable to slowdowns. The main culprit is timeouts. Aggressive timeouts spuriously trigger expensive leader changes, while conservative timeouts leave the system idle and let slowdowns severely inflate latency. Two main alternatives exist: hedging, which improves recovery from slow leaders but still incurs a time-based hedging delay, and cooperative asynchronous protocols, which recover quickly from slowdowns but suffer from high common-case latency and low throughput. This paper presents Ambulance: a BFT state machine replication protocol that sidesteps this trade-off through protocol-rigged races, where replicas, rather than race against the clock, race against each other by executing protocol steps. This enables Ambulance to achieve high throughput and low latency comparable to state-of-the-art timeout-based BFT, while matching the robustness of cooperative approaches.

</details>


### [54] [Programmable Probabilistic Computer with 1,000,000 p-bits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25313)
*Navid Anjum Aadit,Xiuqi Zhang,Shuvro Chowdhury,Kevin Callahan-Coray,Kyle Lee,Saleh Bunaiyan,Sanjay Seshan,Clayton Thomas,Jason Twigg,Andrew Seawright,Forrest Brewer,Tathagata Srimani,Kerem Y. Camsari*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文构建了首个百万p-bit规模的可编程概率计算机，通过FPGA网络突破单芯片限制，发现边界交换频率与局部更新频率的比值η是决定分布式采样器性能的关键参数，为概率计算机的规模化设计提供了定量指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的概率计算机系统受限于单芯片的容量和内存带宽，无法满足大规模Ising模型采样和优化的需求。为突破这一限制，需要构建一个远超单个器件容量的分布式概率计算系统。

Method: 将多个FPGA网络化连接，构建包含一百万p-bit的可编程概率计算机，所有耦合权重保存在本地片上存储器中，设备间仅交换1位边界状态。通过调节边界交换频率与局部p-bit更新频率的比值η，研究分布式采样器的行为特性。

Result: 该机器以超过万亿次翻转/秒的速度执行Gibbs采样。当η高于拓扑相关的阈值时，分布式机器与单片式GPU参考实现性能匹配；低于阈值时，残差能量仍呈幂律衰减但指数降低。理论簇平均场模型重现了相同的行为，表明该权衡是分区随机动力学的普遍属性。

Conclusion: 成功演示了跨越自旋玻璃、Max-Cut和布尔可满足性问题的百万p-bit平台，确立了概率计算机超越单芯片限制的定量设计规则，揭示了分布式随机动力学系统中并行度与精度之间的基本权衡关系。

Abstract: Probabilistic computers built from p-bits have been proposed as hardware accelerators for sampling and optimizing Ising models, but existing systems have been confined to a single chip, capped by its capacity and memory bandwidth. Here we break this limit by networking FPGAs into a single Ising machine far larger than any one device could hold, realizing a programmable probabilistic computer with one million p-bits. The machine performs Gibbs sampling at over a trillion flips per second while keeping every coupling weight in local on-chip memory. During execution, devices exchange nothing but 1-bit boundary states. This architecture exposes a question fundamental to any distributed sampler: how frequently boundary information must be refreshed for a partitioned machine to behave as an unpartitioned one. Using three-dimensional Edwards-Anderson spin glasses, we show that the answer is set by a single timing ratio, eta = f_comm/f_p-bit, of the boundary-exchange frequency to the local p-bit update frequency. Above a topology-dependent threshold, the distributed machine matches a monolithic GPU reference. Below it, residual energy still decays as a power law but with a reduced exponent, turning parallelism into a quantifiable throughput-accuracy tradeoff. A theoretical cluster mean-field model reproduces the same behavior, showing that this tradeoff is a universal property of partitioned stochastic dynamics. These results provide a programmable million-p-bit platform, demonstrated across spin glasses, Max-Cut, and Boolean satisfiability, together with a quantitative design rule for scaling probabilistic computers beyond the single-chip limit.

</details>


### [55] [EmuGEMM: Fused Tensor Core Kernels for Precision Emulation in Matrix Multiplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25453)
*Denghui Lu,Alexander Maeder,Mathieu Luisier,Alexandros Nikolaos Ziogas*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了EmuGEMM，一种融合整数张量核心内核，通过消除冗余内存往返优化Ozaki方案，在现代GPU上实现了高精度矩阵乘法的显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代GPU不断增加低精度矩阵乘法单元的硅预算，导致科学计算工作负载中精度与吞吐量之间的差距扩大。现有的基于Ozaki方案的高精度矩阵乘法实现存在性能瓶颈，特别是中间结果需要反复在全局内存中物化，使得数据移动成为主要瓶颈。

Method: 提出EmuGEMM，一种针对NVIDIA Hopper和Blackwell GPU的融合整数张量核心内核，通过消除Ozaki方案I和II中冗余的内存往返来优化性能。该方法将低精度操作重构为高精度通用矩阵乘法(GEMM)。

Result: 使用方案I时，EmuGEMM在Hopper上达到最高1,639 Top/s（INT8峰值的83%），在Blackwell上达到3,654 Top/s（81%）。对于大矩阵，在相当精度下，吞吐量在Hopper上超过cuBLAS TF32最高1.4倍，在Blackwell上超过1.7倍。使用方案II时，EmuGEMM扩展到复数运算，在Hopper上性能超过cuBLAS ZGEMM最高2.3倍，在Blackwell上超过5.5倍。

Conclusion: EmuGEMM成功利用现代GPU的低精度硬件单元实现了高精度矩阵运算的显著性能提升，证明了通过融合内核消除内存往返瓶颈的有效性，为科学计算工作负载在精度与吞吐量之间提供了更好的平衡方案。

Abstract: Modern GPUs devote an increasing silicon budget to low-precision matrix-multiplication units, widening the precision-throughput gap for scientific computing workloads. Ozaki Schemes I and II offer an alternative by reconstructing high-precision general matrix multiplication (GEMM) from low-precision operations, yet existing implementations leave substantial performance untapped. In particular, intermediate results are repeatedly materialized in global memory, making data movement the dominant bottleneck. We present EmuGEMM, fused integer Tensor Core kernels for NVIDIA Hopper and Blackwell GPUs that eliminate redundant memory round-trips in both Ozaki schemes. Using Scheme I, EmuGEMM sustains up to 1,639 Top/s on Hopper (83% of INT8 peak) and 3,654 Top/s on Blackwell (81%). For large matrices, EmuGEMM surpasses cuBLAS TF32 throughput by up to 1.4x on Hopper and 1.7x on Blackwell, at comparable accuracy. Using Scheme II, EmuGEMM extends to complex arithmetic and outperforms cuBLAS ZGEMM by up to 2.3x on Hopper and 5.5x on Blackwell.

</details>


### [56] [Latency-Aware Service Placement using Neural Combinatorial Optimisers for Edge--Cloud Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25553)
*Kimia Abedpour,Mohammadsadeq Garshasbi Herabad,Zheng Li,Javid Taheri*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出EP-NCO学习框架，通过双图模型和强化学习解决边缘-云计算 continuum 平台的服务放置问题，显著降低响应时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着物联网应用和延迟敏感服务的增长，跨计算continuum平台（如边缘-云系统）的高效服务放置需求日益增加。现代应用被分解为相互依赖的微服务，部署在异构基础设施上，使得在资源和网络约束下的放置成为一个难处理的NP难组合优化问题。

Method: 提出延迟感知的边缘放置神经组合优化器（EP-NCO），这是一个基于学习的框架。EP-NCO采用双图模型来捕获计算基础设施和应用结构中的资源关系和服务依赖关系。图神经网络（GNN）学习基础设施节点和服务组件的结构嵌入，而强化学习策略构建可行的放置方案，考虑执行延迟、通信链路延迟和带宽共享效应。

Result: 在多种系统规模下的广泛仿真表明，EP-NCO始终能够做出高质量的放置决策。与元启发式算法（遗传算法和粒子群优化）相比，总服务响应时间减少46%-50%；与受控的RL消融基线相比，减少25%-35%。训练完成后，EP-NCO能够实现快速的在线推理。

Conclusion: EP-NCO是动态大规模边缘-云环境的实用解决方案，能够处理数百个计算节点、托管数千个应用，这显著超出了当前调度系统的能力范围。

Abstract: The growth of Internet of Things (IoT) applications and latency-sensitive services has increased the demand for efficient service placement across compute continuum platforms, such as edge--cloud systems. Modern applications are decomposed into interdependent microservices deployed over heterogeneous infrastructures, making placement under resource and network constraints an intractable NP-hard combinatorial optimisation problem. This study proposes a latency-aware Edge Placement Neural Combinatorial Optimiser (EP-NCO), a learning-based framework for service placement in compute continuum platforms. EP-NCO employs a dual-graph model to capture resource relationships and service dependencies within both computing infrastructure and application structure. Graph neural networks (GNNs) learn structural embeddings of infrastructure nodes and service components, whereas reinforcement learning policies construct feasible placements that account for execution latency, communication link delays, and bandwidth-sharing effects. Extensive simulations across multiple system scales demonstrate that EP-NCO consistently achieves high-quality placement decisions, reducing the total service response time by 46%--50% compared with metaheuristics (genetic algorithm and particle swarm optimisation) and by 25%--35% compared with controlled RL ablation baselines. Once trained, EP-NCO enables fast online inference, making it a practical solution for dynamic large-scale edge--cloud environments with hundreds of computing nodes, hosting thousands of applications, which is significantly beyond the capability of current scheduling systems.

</details>


### [57] [TwoStepDemocracy: Prototyping of self-evolving, democratic, and decentralized systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25559)
*Stan Verlaan,Johan Pouwelse*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了TwoStepDemocracy，一个去中心化软件演化的技术原型，将需求表达、方案批准和资金支持分离，实现协议原生的治理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去中心化系统在演化过程中往往依赖中心化平台、非正式的维护者权威和大量无偿贡献，存在去中心化理想与现实实践之间的不匹配问题，需要构建协议原生的软件演化机制。

Method: 设计并实现了一个结合高成本加密身份、点对点传播、问题和方案投票以及基于比特币的众筹活动的原型系统。系统将需求、批准和支付三个环节进行分离：用户通过提议和投票表达需求，开发者提交解决方案，被接受的工作可链接到自愿的非托管资金支持，但资金不会转化为更大的投票权。系统采用点对点实现、本地存储、签名治理对象和比特币集成。

Result: 在存储、身份管理和资金等方面评估了系统的性能、可扩展性和成本，结果证明了技术可行性。然而，系统尚不具备社会可行性，需要进行更大规模的用户研究来评估实际社区是否会使用该机制进行投票、资助和协调。

Conclusion: TwoStepDemocracy展示了在技术上构建去中心化软件演化协调层的可行性，成功实现了需求表达、方案批准和资金支持的分离机制。但技术可行性与社会可行性之间存在差距，需要进一步研究真实社区的采用意愿和实践效果。

Abstract: Decentralised systems are often built to avoid central control, but their evolution almost always depends on centralised platforms, informal maintainer authority, and a surprising amount of unpaid goodwill. To address this uncomfortable mismatch, we introduce TwoStepDemocracy, a technical proof-of-concept for protocol-native software evolution.
  The prototype combines costly cryptographic identities, peer-to-peer dissemination, issue and solution voting, and Bitcoin-based funding campaigns. Users can express demand by proposing and voting on issues; developers can submit concrete solutions; and accepted work can be linked to voluntary, non-custodial funding. The design deliberately separates demand, approval, and payment. This way, money can support a solution, but it never buys more voting power.
  The prototype demonstrates that such a coordination layer can be built as a peer-to-peer implementation with local storage, signed governance objects, and Bitcoin integration. We studied performance, scalability, and costs across storage, identity management, and funding. The results show technical feasibility, but not yet social viability. A larger user study is still needed to evaluate whether real communities would, in practice, vote, fund, and coordinate through this mechanism.

</details>


### [58] [Dynamic Load Balancing for Uncertainty Quantification with Applications in Bayesian Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25693)
*C. M. Loi,M. Wille,A. Reinarz*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文为UM-Bridge框架引入了改进的负载均衡器，用于解决高性能计算环境下不确定性量化（UQ）工作流中异构任务调度的挑战，在海啸模拟案例中实现了接近毫秒级的平均节点空闲时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不确定性量化工作流在高性能计算环境中会产生大量异构模型评估任务，这些任务之间存在松散但非平凡的依赖关系。传统的静态"一刀切"调度方法无法最优地处理异构任务，因此需要一种能够有效处理此类工作负载的调度方案。

Method: 在UM-Bridge（一个用于耦合UQ软件与数值模拟的语言无关接口）框架中引入改进的负载均衡器。通过一个实际案例进行测试：使用多层延迟接受采样求解贝叶斯反问题，底层正问题是由ExaHyPE支持的海啸模拟层次结构，其运行时间跨越多个数量级且层级间存在松散依赖关系。

Result: 负载均衡器能够有效地分配采样请求，平均节点空闲时间接近一毫秒，且无需对工作负载做任何先验假设。

Conclusion: 该负载均衡器成功解决了UQ工作流中异构任务的调度难题，在不依赖工作负载先验知识的情况下实现了高效的任务分配，为高性能计算环境下的UQ应用提供了有效的调度解决方案。

Abstract: Uncertainty Quantification (UQ) workflows present a particular scheduling challenge in high performance computing environments, as they typically generate large numbers of heterogeneous model evaluations with loose but non-trivial dependencies between tasks. A static one-size-fits-all approach in traditional schedulers is inadequate to handle heterogeneous tasks optimally. We introduce an improved load balancer in the UQ and Modelling Bridge (UM-Bridge) framework aimed at mitigating these issues; UM-Bridge is a language-agnostic interface developed to couple UQ software with numerical simulation. As a realistic example, we test the load balancer with a Bayesian inverse problem solved via multilevel delayed acceptance sampling. The underlying forward problem is a hierarchy of tsunami simulations enabled through ExaHyPE, whose runtimes span several orders of magnitude and loose dependencies between levels make the workload particularly challenging to schedule. Our results indicate the load balancer is effective at distributing the sampling requests with an average node idle time of close to a millisecond, while not making any prior assumptions about the workload.

</details>


### [59] [Endeavor: Efficient PairHMM for Detection of DNA Variants in Genome-Scale Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25738)
*Miguel Graça,Aleksandar Ilic*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了Endeavor，一种新的PairHMM并行化策略，通过行级细粒度并行实现了CPU和GPU上DNA变异检测的显著加速，相比现有方法性能提升最高达2.14倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNA变异检测是生物信息学流程的关键操作，但随着基因组数据爆炸式增长，现有高性能、可移植的变异检测方案不足。PairHMM算法作为主要瓶颈，在大规模基因组数据集中占用高达70%的执行时间，而现有的CPU和GPU加速方法无法扩展到长DNA序列，仅探索有限的反对角线数据并行性，导致性能不佳。

Method: 提出Endeavor作为PairHMM的新型并行化策略，重新定义其传统公式以探索行级细粒度并行性，在不损失解精度的情况下，推导出一种新颖且可移植的基于SIMD的方法，利用新型并行级别和同步机制，在CPU和GPU上高效处理短序列和长序列，首次实现长达100k碱基对序列的高吞吐量处理。

Result: 在Intel和AMD CPU上，Endeavor峰值吞吐量比GKL提升高达2.14倍，在真实数据集上比GATK HaplotypeCaller快至少2倍；在NVIDIA和AMD GPU上，基因组规模数据集相比最先进的GPU方法实现高达2.05倍的加速。

Conclusion: Endeavor成功解决了PairHMM算法在处理长DNA序列时的可扩展性问题，通过创新的行级并行策略和SIMD优化，实现了CPU和GPU平台上的显著性能提升，为大规模基因组数据分析提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: DNA variant calling represents a key operation in bioinformatics pipelines that aims at identifying genetic variants. Given an evidenced explosion in genomic data availability, there is an urgent need for a high-performant, portable and efficient solution for variant calling, which can further improve our understanding of genomic structure and genetic basis for complex diseases. In its most common formulation, the Pair Hidden Markov Model (PairHMM) algorithm for variant calling stands as the main bottleneck in the pipeline, accounting for up to 70% of the execution time in large-scale genomic datasets. The state-of-the-art approaches for accelerating PairHMM in CPUs and GPUs do not scale to long DNA sequences and only explore very limited anti-diagonal data parallelism, which yields poor performance. In this work, Endeavor is proposed as a new parallelization strategy for PairHMM that redefines its traditional formulation to explore row-level fine-grained parallelism without loss in solution accuracy. Based on this, a novel and portable SIMD-based approach is derived for efficient and high-performance processing of short and long sequences in CPUs and GPUs, leveraging novel levels of parallelism and synchronization to achieve high throughput in sequences up to 100k basepairs for the first time. Evaluation on Intel and AMD CPUs shows that Endeavor outperforms GKL up to 2.14x in peak throughput and GATK HaplotypeCaller by at least 2x in real-world datasets, while NVIDIA and AMD GPUs achieve up to 2.05x speedups in genome-scale datasets when compared to state-of-the-art GPU-based methods.

</details>


### [60] [NEURON-Fabric: Architecture-Runtime Co-Design for Controlled Low-Bit Gradient Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25759)
*Ziqiang Wang,Changcheng Huang,Chung-Horng Lung*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: NEURON-Fabric提出了一种基于性能分析引导的运行时系统，通过动态决策机制实现低比特梯度通信，在保证训练精度的同时有效降低了分布式训练中的通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模神经网络训练中，梯度聚合的通信开销是核心瓶颈。虽然低比特梯度聚合可以降低通信成本，但静态应用会破坏训练稳定性，因为安全精度取决于训练阶段、模型结构、运行时分桶和通信底层设施等多个因素。

Method: 提出NEURON-Fabric系统，采用校准的操作配置文件、模型感知的运行时绑定、在线训练健康监控和reducer容量检查。该系统动态决定何时允许低比特聚合、何时回退到FP32、以及哪些模型区域适用于哪种路由，将reducer准入视为架构-运行时协同设计问题而非独立的压缩算子。

Result: 在视觉、Transformer和自回归语言模型工作负载上验证了从校准到分布式通信钩子执行的完整路径。静态低比特通信会导致训练精度崩溃，而性能分析引导的控制方法能够保持接近全精度的精度或达到校准目标，并降低了建模的梯度通信流量。在Transformer和十亿参数语言模型上的测试表明，相同的路由和回退机制可跨模型族和多节点部署执行。

Conclusion: NEURON-Fabric成功证明了通过性能分析引导的动态控制机制，可以在保证训练精度稳定性的前提下实现低比特梯度通信的有效应用，为分布式学习中的通信优化提供了一种安全可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Large-scale neural-network training repeatedly aggregates gradients across devices, making communication a central cost in distributed learning. Low-bit gradient aggregation can reduce this cost, but applying it as a static replacement for full-precision communication can destabilize training because safe precision depends on training phase, model structure, runtime bucketization, and the communication substrate.
  This paper presents NEURON-Fabric, a profile-guided runtime system for controlled low-bit gradient communication. NEURON-Fabric uses calibrated operating profiles, model-aware runtime bindings, online training-health monitoring, and reducer-capacity checks to decide when low-bit aggregation should be admitted, when execution should fall back to FP32, and which model regions are eligible for each route. The runtime preserves model semantics inside mixed DDP buckets and treats reducer admission as an architecture-runtime co-design problem rather than as a standalone compression operator.
  Across vision, Transformer, and autoregressive language-model workloads, NEURON-Fabric validates the path from calibration to distributed communication-hook execution. Static low-bit communication can collapse training accuracy, while profile-guided control preserves accuracy near full-precision references or calibrated targets and reduces modeled gradient-communication traffic in the evaluated settings. Transformer and billion-parameter language-model checks show that the same routing and fallback mechanisms execute across model families and multi-node deployments. Reducer-side replay and reducer-path measurements identify when compact sign-count aggregation is expected to reduce communication cost and when endpoint capacity should trigger fallback.

</details>

*Komal Thareja,Paul Ruth,Berent Aldikacti,Michael Zink*

TL;DR: 本文展示了结合FABRIC测试平台与LoomAI大语言模型编程助手，可以显著降低实验复现的难度，在三个案例中将复现工作量减少了4-6倍。AI助手擅长环境搭建和代码调试，但在分析阶段需要人类指导。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管计算可复现性对科学研究至关重要，但目前实现起来仍然非常困难。作者希望探索如何利用国际FABRIC测试平台结合大语言模型（LLM）编程助手来简化已发表实验的复现过程。

Method: 在FABRIC测试平台上复现了三个跨领域的案例研究：BBR系列拥塞控制评估、CPU MPI集群上的LAMMPS分子动力学扩展基准测试，以及应激蛋白稳态基因组学流程。研究重点不在于匹配数值输出，而是评估复现实验是否支持与原始研究相同的科学结论。

Conclusion: 研究证明了AI辅助方法在实验复现方面的有效性，并提出了改进研究测试平台上AI辅助可复现性的实用建议。

Abstract: Computational reproducibility remains difficult despite being central to scientific research. In this paper, we show how the international FABRIC testbed, combined with large language model (LLM) coding assistants through LoomAI, can simplify reproducing published experiments across multiple domains. We reproduced three case studies on FABRIC, covering BBR-family congestion-control evaluations, LAMMPS molecular dynamics scaling benchmarks on a CPU-only MPI cluster, and stress protein homeostasis genomics pipelines.
  Rather than focusing only on matching numerical outputs, we evaluate whether the reproduced experiments support the same scientific conclusions as the original studies. The AI assistant was effective in setting up the environment, adapting code, and debugging, but struggled with the analysis stages that lacked clearly defined workflows, which required human guidance to establish execution order and data dependencies.
  Across the case studies, the AI-assisted workflow reduced reproduction effort by roughly 4--6 times. We conclude with practical recommendations for improving AI-assisted reproducibility on research testbeds.

</details>


### [62] [Interference-Aware Cross-Application Placement: A Multi-Objective Optimization Approach for Microservice Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25922)
*Iqra Zafar,Christian Medeiros Adriano,Holger Giese*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于因果效应的干扰感知服务放置方法，通过时空数据结构捕获跨应用干扰的必要和充分条件概率，并利用多目标优化器在真实多应用工作负载上显著减少了干扰并提升了响应性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在云环境中，多应用共享资源会导致跨应用干扰，引起延迟增加和系统稳定性下降。现有微服务方法要么仅检测定位性能问题，要么仅优化延迟，无法解释特定共置为何导致干扰，也未能将干扰因果机制用于指导服务放置优化。

Method: 构建时空数据结构捕获跨应用干扰的因果效应，将其数学形式化为必要和充分条件概率；利用跨应用画像模拟追踪并估计干扰概率，结合p95响应时间等服务性能基线；将这些因果度量输入Optuna多目标优化器，支持网络惩罚、应用隔离需求及因果指标的可调权重。

Result: 在真实多应用工作负载上的实验表明，干扰感知放置显著降低了跨应用干扰，并改善了响应性能；因果驱动的多目标方案使云运维人员能够显式控制干扰、延迟和通信开销。

Conclusion: 通过将跨应用干扰的因果关系数学化并融入多目标优化，本文填补了现有方法在解释干扰成因与指导放置优化之间的空白，为云原生环境下的服务放置提供了可解释、可控且性能优越的解决方案。

Abstract: In modern cloud architectures, multiple applications often run within the same clustered environment, sharing underlying resources. This resource sharing can cause interference among applications, leading to degraded latency and reduced system stability. As containerized microservices become increasingly central to cloud-native applications, their performance can suffer from complex interference scenarios related to resource competition. Meanwhile, most existing microservice approaches address interference either by detecting and localizing performance issues or by optimizing latency alone, without explaining why specific co-locations cause cross-application interference, and how this can inform service placement optimization. This work closes that gap by building a spatio-temporal data structure that captures the causal effects of cross-application interference. These causal effects are mathematically formalized as necessary and sufficient conditional probabilities that inform a multi-objective optimizer (Optuna). Cross-application profiling is used to simulate traces and estimate interference probabilities, while per-service latency baselines are provided by performance data, such as 95th-percentile response times (p95). Our approach supports network penalties, application isolation requirements, and adjustable weighting of necessary and sufficient causal metrics. Experimental results on real multi-application workloads show that interference-aware placements significantly reduce cross-application interference and improve response performance. Ultimately, the causality-driven multi-objective formulation gives cloud operators explicit control over interference, latency, and communication overhead when configuring service placements.

</details>
