### [63] [Dense Supervision Is Not Enough: The Readout Blind Spot in Looped Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24898)
*Rituraj Sharma,Tu Vu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 循环语言模型中，逐循环交叉熵损失仅控制读出层暴露的变量，而非循环递归中的所有变量。规范化读出层（如RMSNorm/LayerNorm）隐藏了隐藏状态的径向尺度，导致尺度失控。解决方案是让尺度对损失可见或从循环中移除尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究循环语言模型中的基本监督问题：逐循环交叉熵损失实际控制哪些状态变量？特别是在使用尺度不变读出层（如RMSNorm、LayerNorm）和预归一化残差递归时，隐藏状态尺度这一具体失效模式如何影响模型训练。

Method: 通过理论分析揭示规范化读出层隐藏径向尺度的问题机制；设计对比实验，在44M和129M参数的循环Transformer上测试不同方案：尺度可见读出层、显式范数惩罚、尺度移除递归架构；建立变深度基准测试评估模型性能。

Result: 无跨循环归一化时，使用RMSNorm读出的模型隐藏状态范数飙升至数千甚至数万；尺度可见读出层和显式范数惩罚将范数控制在数十量级；尺度受控变体在匹配推理深度操作点上实现更低困惑度。

Conclusion: 提出简洁设计原则：密集监督训练出口层；循环尺度控制需要让尺度对损失可见或从循环中移除。该原则指导下的尺度控制架构在变深度基准测试中表现更优。

Abstract: Looped language models turn hidden states into runtime state: each state is decoded for prediction and fed back into future computation. This creates a basic supervision question: which state variables does cross-entropy actually control? We show that dense per-loop cross-entropy controls the variables exposed by the readout, not every variable active in the recurrent transition. Hidden-state scale gives a concrete failure mode. Scale-invariant readouts such as RMSNorm and LayerNorm hide radial scale from the immediate cross-entropy loss, while pre-norm residual recurrence continues to carry and update that same scale. Thus per-loop loss can make early exits usable without controlling recurrent scale. In 44M and 129M looped transformers without inter-loop normalization, per-loop cross-entropy through RMSNorm readouts still drives final hidden-state norms into the thousands or tens of thousands. Scale-visible readouts and explicit norm penalties keep norms in the tens, and scale-removing recurrence is the complementary architectural fix. The resulting design rule is simple: dense supervision trains exits; recurrent scale control requires either making scale visible to a loss or removing it from the loop. Consistent with this rule, scale-controlled variants achieve lower perplexity at matched inference-depth operating points in our variable-depth benchmarks.

</details>


### [64] [From Meta Idea to Advanced Mathematical Discovery -- Human-AI Co-Discovery of Sign-Embedding Quantum Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24899)
*Yanqiao Wang,Jin-Peng Liu,Peng Li,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过符号嵌入量子算法的案例研究，展示了AI辅助数学研究在问题形成阶段的协作价值，强调了人机协作在科研直觉转化、路线探索和方案优化中的重要作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI辅助数学研究主要评估解决预定义问题的能力，而在实际科研中，许多重要进展始于将模糊的研究直觉转化为具体问题、可行路线和定理家族的阶段。作者旨在研究这一被忽视的阶段，探索AI在问题形成和早期探索中的作用。

Method: 采用案例研究方法，记录了符号嵌入量子算法（用于矩阵方程和矩阵函数的基础原语）的发现过程。通过AI辅助探索工作流（后整合到AIM系统中），将"有理逼近对跳跃型函数（如符号函数）特别有效"的人类直觉扩展为研究路线图，比较候选方案，最终收敛到符号嵌入作为核心框架。AIM帮助连接已知矩阵符号恒等式与更广泛的矩阵方程和矩阵函数类别，并协助证明和复杂度计算。人类研究者保留关键科学判断：选择值得追求的人机扩展路线，拒绝需要隐藏条件的Cayley梯形逼近，并将Sylvester实现从粗糙的二次间隙查询路线优化为最终的因子化和缩放分析。

Result: 成功开发了符号嵌入量子算法，用于矩阵方程和矩阵函数求解，这是量子线性代数和算子输出量子算法的基础原语。研究证明了AI辅助探索在问题形成阶段的价值：扩展科研直觉、比较方案、发现连接关系。同时验证了人类在关键决策中不可替代的作用：路线选择、方案否决和精细优化。

Conclusion: 人机协作发现工作流（如AIM系统）的价值不在于作为独立的定理证明器，而在于作为科研伙伴，在人主导的研究循环中参与问题形成、连接发现、推导和批判性审查。最有价值的人机协作模式是AI作为研究助手，人类保留最终的科学判断和决策权。

Abstract: AI-assisted mathematics is often evaluated on solving predefined problems. In practice, however, many important advances begin earlier, when a vague research intuition is transformed into a concrete problem, a promising route, and a theorem family worth proving. This report studies that stage through a case study that led to sign-embedding quantum algorithms for matrix equations and matrix functions, foundational primitives in quantum linear algebra and operator-output quantum algorithms. The project began with a human-originated intuition that rational approximation is especially effective for jump-type functions such as the sign function, and might therefore serve as a design principle for quantum algorithms. Rather than merely assisting after the problem was fixed, AI-assisted exploration, including workflows later integrated into the agentic AI-mathematician system AIM, played a key role in expanding this intuition into a route map, comparing candidate formulations, and converging toward sign embedding as the central framework. AIM then helped connect a known matrix-sign identity to wider classes of matrix equations and matrix functions, and drafted proof and complexity calculations. The decisive scientific judgments remained human: selecting which human-AI-expanded routes were worth pursuing, rejecting a Cayley-trapezoidal approximation when its validity required a hidden condition, and refining the Sylvester implementation from a coarse quadratic-gap query route to the final factorized and scaled analysis. The report argues that human-AI co-discovery workflows, with systems such as AIM as important components, are most valuable not as standalone theorem provers, but as research partners for problem formation, connection discovery, derivation, and skeptical review inside a human-gated research loop.

</details>


### [65] [On-Device Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24900)
*Andrea Mattia Garavagno,Edoardo Ragusa,Paolo Gastaldo,Antonio Frisoli,Claudio Loconsole*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在部署设备上直接执行轻量级神经架构搜索(NAS)，为传感器数据分析寻找最优微型神经网络架构，在人机接口和智能故障诊断任务上实现了超越SOTA的性能，同时显著降低了内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经网络在处理生物特征数据时面临个体间数据变化的问题，特别是在人机接口应用中，不同用户之间的生物信号差异显著。现有方法无法自适应地为每个用户定制最优网络架构，因此需要一种能够在部署设备上实时搜索和优化神经网络架构的方法来应对这一挑战。

Method: 设计了一种新型轻量级神经架构搜索(NAS)方法，直接在部署设备上执行NAS来寻找最适合分析传感器实时数据流的微型神经网络架构。该方法包含引导式数据收集程序，当用户变化时可重新设计神经网络以适应个体差异。在意大利手语数据集(ISL，包含意大利字母符号的表面肌电信号sEMG)和凯斯西储大学数据集(CWRU，智能故障诊断基准)上进行验证，并在多个嵌入式系统(包括Raspberry Pi 4)上实现。

Result: 在Raspberry Pi 4上运行时：(1)ISL数据集：提出的微型神经架构相比SOTA减少0.63倍RAM占用，准确率提高5.96个百分点；(2)CWRU数据集：相比SOTA减少0.44倍RAM占用，准确率提高0.2个百分点。证明了该方法在降低内存占用的同时提升或保持了模型精度。

Conclusion: 该研究成功实现了近传感器计算的自适应能力，通过在部署设备上直接执行NAS，能够为不同用户和应用场景定制最优微型神经网络架构。这种方法特别适合需要处理个体差异的人机接口应用，同时也展现了在智能故障诊断等其他领域的应用潜力，为资源受限的嵌入式设备上的神经网络优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: This paper proposes a new approach to near-sensor computing, in which a lightweight Neural Architecture Search (NAS) is performed directly on the deployment device to find the best tiny neural architecture for analyzing the real-time data acquired through sensors. This new adaptation capability can be particularly useful in the case of human-machine interfaces for which the neural network analyzing the biometrical data can be re-designed each time the user changes, after a guided data collection procedure, fighting the typical data variations between individuals on a new level. To implement the proposed approach a new NAS has been designed and then validated on the Italian Sign Language dataset (ISL), a collection of surface electromyography (sEMG) signals of the signs of the Italian alphabet, using several embedded systems. Moreover, further validation on the Case Western Reserve University dataset (CWRU), a benchmark for intelligent fault diagnosis, is presented to suggest another possible application of the proposed approach. When run on a Raspberry Pi 4, the proposed NAS performs beyond the state of the art proposing a tiny neural architecture having 0.63 times less RAM occupancy and 5.96 percentage points of more accuracy in the case of the ISL dataset; and 0.44 times less RAM occupancy and 0.2 percentage points of more accuracy in the case of the CWRU dataset.

</details>


### [66] [LLM Evolution as an Industry-Scale Ecosystem: A Lifecycle Perspective on Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24901)
*Hao Jiang,Enneng Yang,Guojie Zhu,Yibin Chen,Yunkun Xu,Zifu Kou,Jiayi Li,Chong Chen,Zhao Cao,Li Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于工业大语言模型持续学习的综述，将ICL重新定义为版本化生态系统中的闭环更新发布问题，提出五项生命周期设计原则，并评估技术成熟度与实际部署蓝图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业部署的LLM需要持续更新以适应不断变化的需求和环境，而非从零重新训练。然而现有研究多聚焦静态基准改进，未能捕捉真实工业需求，因此需要从生态系统视角重新审视工业持续学习的挑战与解决方案。

Method: 将工业持续学习重构为版本化生态系统中的闭环更新-发布问题，识别三大核心挑战（重复适应侵蚀模型可塑性、基础模型升级破坏能力继承、长期可持续性受部署约束），围绕五项生命周期设计原则组织技术全景：保留可塑性余量、将升级视为能力迁移、实现可信持续强化学习、使训练配方自优化、将问责机制作为长期迭代基础层。

Result: 系统梳理了ICL的技术景观，为每项设计原则综合代表性技术方向；通过证据视角评估各原则及其技术组件的成熟度，识别阻碍实际部署的关键差距，提出实用ICL部署蓝图。

Conclusion: 本文为工业LLM持续学习建立了系统性框架，明确了从学术研究向工业实践转化的路径，为未来研究与实践部署提供了清晰指导。

Abstract: Continual learning capability is critical for Industrial LLMs, as deployed models must be continuously updated to meet evolving requirements and environments, rather than repeatedly retrained from scratch. However, most existing research focuses on improvements on static benchmarks, failing to capture real industrial needs. In this survey, we reformulate Industrial Continual Learning (ICL) for LLMs as a closed-loop update-and-release problem in a versioned ecosystem, where updates propagate hierarchically to industrial, application-specific models and LLM-powered applications, with capability inheritance and transfer across versions and model families. From this ecosystem perspective, we identify three core challenges: repeated adaptation erodes model plasticity, foundation-model upgrades break capability inheritance, and long-term sustainability is constrained by deployment requirements. We then organize the technical landscape of ICL around five lifecycle design principles: preserving plasticity headroom, treating upgrades as capability transfer, enabling trustworthy continual reinforcement learning, making training recipes self-optimizing, and building accountability as a base layer for long-term iteration. For each principle, we synthesize representative technical directions. Finally, we evaluate the maturity of each principle and its technical components via an evidence-based lens, identify key gaps hindering real-world deployment, and outline a practical ICL deployment blueprint and a pathway for feeding industrial realities back into academic research.

</details>


### [67] [GCT-MARL: Graph-Based Contrastive Transfer for Sample-Efficient Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25073)
*Animesh Animesh,Satheesh K Perepu,Kaushik Dey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了GCT-MARL迁移学习框架，通过自适应对齐损失和两阶段训练协议，有效解决了合作多智能体强化学习中跨不同规模和组成的智能体群体迁移问题，显著加速了目标任务的收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在合作多智能体强化学习中，针对每个新环境或任务从头训练智能体具有挑战性且成本高昂。现有MARL迁移方法在应对不同规模和组成的智能体群体迁移时存在关键局限性。

Method: 提出GCT-MARL框架，基于MAIL的多视图图对比骨干网络，引入：(1)每个视图的自适应加权对齐损失；(2)专门设计的两阶段训练协议，用于处理跨不同规模和组成的智能体群体迁移。该方法支持通过顺序链接两阶段迁移协议实现持续学习。

Result: 在同构迁移（同一阵营、变化的N）和异构迁移（跨阵营和混合单位类型）场景中，相比从头训练，该框架显著加速了目标任务的收敛。同时证明了框架通过顺序链接迁移协议可自然支持持续学习。

Conclusion: 本工作提供了统一的方法来缓解当前MARL迁移方法的关键局限性，在方法论和实证层面都提供了新见解。该方法在多种迁移场景下均表现出良好的加速效果和持续学习能力。

Abstract: In cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL), from a deployment perspective, it is challenging and expensive to train agents from scratch for each new environment or task. In this work, we propose GCT-MARL, a transfer learning framework that builds on the multi-view graph contrastive backbone of MAIL and augments it with a per-view, adaptively weighted alignment loss and a two-phase training protocol specifically designed for transfer across populations of varying sizes and compositions. We empirically demonstrate that the proposed framework markedly accelerates convergence on the target task relative to from-scratch training, in both homogeneous (within-faction, varying N) and heterogeneous (cross-faction and mixed unit-type) transfer scenarios. Furthermore, we show that the framework naturally supports continual learning by sequentially chaining the two-phase transfer protocol across a series of related tasks. Overall, this work provides a unified approach to mitigating key limitations in current MARL transfer methods with new insights at both methodological and empirical levels.

</details>


### [68] [A Spectral Phase Diagram for Binary Few-Shot Classification: Intrinsic Dimensionality, Geometric Saturation, and Representational Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24903)
*Arnav Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了饱和度指数S(K)用于解决机器学习中何时停止标注样本的问题，该指数仅需支持特征即可在O(d³)时间内计算，在16/17个任务中与精度增益呈正相关（中位ρ=0.811），作为停止规则达到AUC=0.752，为标注决策提供了有效的概率指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在应用机器学习中，决定何时停止收集标注样本是一个基础但理论化不足的问题。标注成本高昂，过度收集造成资源浪费，收集不足则影响模型性能，因此需要一个可靠的理论指标来指导标注停止时机。

Method: 提出饱和度指数S(K) = erank(Σ̂_W^(K)) / K，测量类内样本协方差的有效秩与样本数量的比值。证明当协方差估计量围绕总体协方差良好集中且线性判别器稳定时，该指数低于阈值。该方法仅需支持特征，无需测试标签或训练分类器，可在O(d³)时间内计算。在17个二元分类任务、6个数据集、246个倍增对观测上进行验证。

Result: (1) 16/17个任务中S(K)与边际精度增益呈正Spearman相关（中位ρ=0.811），合并ρ=0.548（p=1.1×10⁻²⁰）；(2) 识别出三阶段模式（探索、过渡、饱和），平均边际增益分别为3.48%、2.40%、0.82%（所有成对显著性检验p≤0.008）；(3) 作为二元停止规则AUC=0.752；(4) 渐近有效秩与峰值精度无显著单调关系；(5) 小饱和指数配合低精度可诊断表征不足问题。

Conclusion: 饱和度指数为标注停止决策提供了可计算、有理论保证且实用有效的指标。研究限于二元分类和固定线性分类器，未来可扩展至N类场景和预训练骨干网络表示。该方法能帮助实践者在标注成本与模型性能间做出合理权衡。

Abstract: Deciding when to stop collecting labeled examples is a fundamental but undertheorized problem in applied machine learning. The saturation index $S(K) = \operatorname{erank}(\widehatΣ_W^{(K)}) / K$ measures the ratio of the effective rank of the pooled within-class sample covariance to the shot count; we prove it falls below a threshold precisely when the covariance estimator is well-concentrated around the population covariance and the linear discriminant has stabilized. The index is computable in $O(d^3)$ time from support features alone, requiring no test labels or trained classifier.
  Evaluated across $N = 246$ doubling-pair observations from seventeen binary tasks and six datasets, sixteen of seventeen tasks have a positive within-task Spearman correlation between $S(K)$ and marginal accuracy gain (median $ρ= 0.811$). The pooled Spearman correlation is $ρ= 0.548$ ($p = 1.1 \times 10^{-20}$, $N = 246$). A three-phase diagram (exploration, transition, saturation) with mean marginal gains of $3.48\%$, $2.40\%$, and $0.82\%$ is supported by all pairwise significance tests ($p \leq 0.008$). As a binary stopping rule, the index achieves AUC $= 0.752$, providing meaningful probabilistic guidance for annotation decisions. Asymptotic effective rank and peak accuracy show no significant monotone relationship across tasks (Spearman $r_s = 0.380$, $p = 0.133$, $N = 17$). A small saturation index paired with low accuracy diagnoses representational inadequacy. All results are for binary classification with a fixed linear classifier; extensions to $N$-way settings and pretrained backbone representations are discussed as future work.

</details>


### [69] [When Do Conservation Laws Survive Learned Representations? Certified Horizons for Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24945)
*Hongbo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的保守系统认证方法，通过解码潜在表示来认证物理守恒不变量，而非直接认证潜在哈密顿量。研究揭示了硬性辛结构在已知相坐标中表现最佳但难以跨域学习表示，而软性对齐不变量则能在学习表示设置中保持认证能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理世界模型在学习潜在表示后，难以确保护物理守恒律是否仍可认证。传统方法认证学习到的潜在哈密顿量或标量见证函数，但模型可能在学习空间中漂移而保持这些量守恒，导致无法保证真实物理不变量的守恒。因此需要一种能够直接认证解码后物理不变量的方法。

Method: 提出"认证视界"概念，该视界可从可测量的模型缺陷预先界定展开在物理不变量水平集上可证明保持的步数。核心设计是认证解码后的物理不变量：将潜在状态解码并评估已知不变量。推导了外壳视界证书，其预算分解为表示缺陷、读出缺陷和潜在动力学缺陷，并通过单调对齐桥接机制，使软性学习见证函数为解码不变量提供认证视界。方法在状态观测、学习提升观测和像素观测三种设置下进行了测试。

Result: 守恒证书可以在学习表示后继续存在，但不同几何先验的存活能力不同：硬性规范辛结构在已知相坐标中产生最长视界，但无法跨越学习图；受控Lipschitz对齐的软性不变量在测试的学习表示设置中能够存活；像素观测认证在稳定读出子管上得以恢复；开普勒问题暴露了几何边界限制。

Conclusion: 中心认证对象不是潜在哈密顿量，而是解码后的物理不变量，其对表示学习的鲁棒性可以被测量、认证和证伪。这为物理世界模型的可信度评估提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: We ask a representation-learning question about physical world models: when does a conservation law remain certifiable after a model learns a latent representation? A certified horizon bounds -- in advance, from measurable model defects -- how many steps a rollout provably stays on a physical invariant's level set. The key design choice is what is certified: not a learned latent Hamiltonian or a learned scalar witness (a model can conserve either while drifting in true energy), but the decoded physical invariant obtained by decoding the latent state and evaluating the known invariant. Around this object we derive shell-horizon certificates whose budget decomposes into representation, readout, and latent-dynamics defects, with a monotone alignment bridge through which a soft learned witness yields a certified horizon for the decoded invariant, and test them across state, learned-lift, and pixel observations on conservative systems. Conservation certificates can survive learned representation, but not all geometric priors survive equally: hard canonical symplectic structure yields the longest horizons in known phase coordinates yet does not cross a learned chart, whereas a controlled-Lipschitz-aligned soft invariant survives in the learned-representation settings we test; pixel certification is recovered on a readout-stable sub-tube; and the Kepler problem exposes a geometric boundary. The central object is therefore not a latent Hamiltonian, but a decoded physical invariant whose robustness to representation learning can be measured, certified, and falsified.

</details>


### [70] [Low Variance Trust Region Optimization with Independent Actors and Sequential Updates in Cooperative Multi-agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25526)
*Bang Giang Le,Viet Cuong Ta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对独立行动者设置下的多智能体信任区域方法在序列更新中面临的高方差问题，提出了一种裁剪目标函数来控制优势函数波动的上界，实现了稳定的收敛性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在合作多智能体强化学习的独立行动者设置中，序列更新框架需要在每个智能体策略步进后重新估计联合优势函数。尽管重要性采样在实践中取得成功，但更新后的优势函数存在指数级高方差问题，导致收敛不稳定。

Method: 提出一种裁剪目标函数（clipping objective）来控制序列更新中优势函数波动的上界；基于该目标推导出单调边界并证明其具有到ε-纳什均衡的次线性收敛性；进一步导出两个实用的算法实现。

Result: 在三个流行的多智能体强化学习基准测试中，所提方法在大多数环境中优于测试的基线方法；通过分析不同训练设置，验证了该方法具有稳定的收敛特性和期望的低优势方差估计。

Conclusion: 所提出的裁剪目标方法有效解决了独立行动者设置下序列更新的高方差问题，实现了稳定的收敛和优越的性能，为多智能体强化学习提供了可靠的解决方案。源代码已公开以便复现。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning assumes each agent shares the same reward function and can be trained effectively using the Trust Region framework of single-agent. Instead of relying on other agents' actions, the independent actors setting considers each agent to act based only on its local information, thus having more flexible applications. However, in the sequential update framework, it is required to re-estimate the joint advantage function after each individual agent's policy step. Despite the practical success of importance sampling, the updated advantage function suffers from exponentially high variance problems, which likely result in unstable convergence. In this work, we first analyze the high variance advantage both empirically and theoretically. To overcome this limitation, we introduce a clipping objective to control the upper bounds of the advantage fluctuation in sequential updates. With the proposed objective, we provide a monotonic bound with sub-linear convergence to $ε$-Nash Equilibria. We further derive two new practical algorithms using our clipping objective. The experiment results on three popular multi-agent reinforcement learning benchmarks show that our proposed method outperforms the tested baselines in most environments. By carefully analyzing different training settings, our proposed method is highlighted with both stable convergence properties and the desired low advantage variance estimation. For reproducibility purposes, our source code is publicly available at https://github.com/giangbang/Low-Variance-Trust-Region-MARL.

</details>


### [71] [Conformal Orbit-Valid Trust Horizons for Equivariant World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24946)
*Hongbo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对具有已知群对称性的潜在世界模型的信任时域认证方法，通过共形校准实现保守的误差控制，并证明精确等变性可以在群轨道上传递认证结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习到的世界模型仅在其展开误差保持可控的时域范围内才有用。为了确保模型的可信度，需要一种能够认证潜在世界模型信任时域的方法，特别是在已知群对称性的情况下。

Method: 给定单步潜在残差和有限时间展开估计，形成原始时域曲线并使用split-conformal乘法因子进行校准。在可复现审计集上验证校准因子的保守性，并利用精确等变性将校准后的信任时域曲线在群轨道上传递。

Result: 在50次稳定审计中观察到零反保守违规（95%置信上限5.8%）。等变模型的轨道传输残差中位数为1.1%，最大4.1%。认证非空（认证与测量时域比中位数0.67）。在2D对称基底上所有模型都轨道有效；在3D偏航审计中等变模型获得单扇区安全认证，而非等变基线需付出违规、松弛或额外扇区代价。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了保守的分布审计而非全局可达性保证，证书引导的子目标间距在当前3D CEM-MPC行为层未获确认。等变性在复杂环境中显示出明显的认证优势。

Abstract: Learned world models are useful only over horizons on which their rollout error remains controlled. We study trust-horizon certification for latent world models with known group symmetries. Given a one-step latent residual and a finite-time expansion estimate, we form a raw horizon curve and calibrate it with a split-conformal multiplicative factor. On the reproducible audit set, the conformal factor is $γ_α=1.0$: the raw certificate is already conservative under the audit protocol. Across 50 stable audits, we observe zero anti-conservative violations, corresponding to an exact-binomial 95% upper bound of 5.8% on the violation rate. Our main structural result is that exact equivariance transports a calibrated trust-horizon curve over the group orbit: when the environment dynamics, encoder, predictor, action transform, and latent metric satisfy the stated equivariance/invariance conditions, rollout errors and trust horizons are orbit-constant. Empirically, the implemented models exhibit small orbit-transport residuals, with median 1.1% and maximum 4.1% over 14 orbit audits. The certificate is also non-vacuous (median certified-to-measured horizon ratio 0.67). A certificate-level calibration-cost study shows two complementary regimes. On a symmetric 2D substrate, equivariant, plain, and augmented models are all orbit-valid from a single calibration sector -- no separation, because the substrate already makes non-equivariant baselines approximately orbit-robust. A 3D yaw audit shows the other regime: the equivariant model obtains a one-sector safe and non-vacuous orbit-valid certificate, while healthy non-equivariant baselines pay violation, slack, sharpness, or additional-sector cost. The certificate is a conservative, distributional audit rather than a global reachability guarantee, and certificate-guided subgoal spacing is not confirmed in the current 3D CEM-MPC behavior layer.

</details>


### [72] [Forget to Improve: On-Device LLM-Agent Continual Learning via Budget-Curated Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25115)
*Beining Wu,Zihao Ding,Jun Huang,Yanxiao Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了\sys{}系统，通过计算“单位字节净值”分数来统一管理设备端语言模型代理的经验记忆生命周期，实现了内存占用减少2.7倍、上行流量减少2.4倍、将注入攻击成功率从75%降至零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设备端语言模型代理通过检索式记忆积累经验，但这种记忆面临三大挑战：（1）内存和能耗的硬性资源限制；（2）与对等节点共享记忆的窄带上行链路开销；（3）记忆可被代理读取的内容写入，成为攻击面。现有系统各自只解决部分问题：代理式记忆无预算增长、设备端方法仅保留成功经验、中毒攻击研究未从记忆治理角度考虑。

Method: 提出\sys{}系统，采用单一的“单位字节净值”分数（价值减去危害）作为统一标准来管理记忆生命周期的三个关键决策：（1）KEEP决策：在RAM和能耗预算约束下驱逐低价值字节；（2）SHARE决策：仅当洞察的价值超过其上行成本时才发送；（3）TRUST决策：根据来源证明来筛选对等节点条目。核心思想是让预算充当策展人，用一个统一的规则决定保留、共享和信任什么。

Result: 在语言模型代理任务漂移基准测试和真实异构Jetson测试平台（包含两个机械臂节点和一个中心枢纽）上评估：内存占用减少2.7倍，上行流量减少2.4倍，注入攻击成功率从75%降至零，在受中毒或陈旧记忆损坏的案例上提高了准确率。通过净值筛选记忆在减少足迹、能耗、上行流量和注入成功率的同时不降低准确率。

Conclusion: 通过净值进行记忆管理能够在不损害代理性能的前提下，同时减少资源消耗和安全风险。在这种设定下，按净值进行遗忘不仅不会削弱代理，反而能改善代理性能，证明了统一的价值导向记忆治理框架的有效性。

Abstract: On-device language-model agents improve by accumulating experience in retrieved memory rather than by updating weights. This memory is hard-bounded and exposed: it consumes RAM and energy, reaches peers through a thin uplink, and becomes an attack surface because it is writable by what the agent reads. Existing systems each cover one part of this problem: agentic memories grow without a budget, on-device methods keep entries by success alone, and poisoning is studied mainly as an attack rather than as a memory-governance problem. We propose \sys{}, a single net-value-per-byte score that governs an agent's experience-memory lifecycle. The main idea is to let the budget act as the curator: each entry is scored as value minus harm, per byte, so one ruler decides what to keep, share, and trust. \sys{} makes three decisions: (1) \textbf{KEEP} evicts low-value bytes under the RAM and energy budget; (2) \textbf{SHARE} sends an insight only when its value exceeds its uplink cost; and (3) \textbf{TRUST} gates a peer entry by provenance. On language-model-agent task-drift benchmarks and a real heterogeneous Jetson testbed with two robot-arm nodes and a hub, \sys{} reduces memory by $2.7\times$ and uplink by $2.4\times$, drives injection success from 0.75 to zero, and raises accuracy on cases corrupted by poison or stale memory. Curating by net value reduces footprint, energy, uplink, and injection success together without reducing accuracy. In this setting, forgetting by net value improves the agent rather than weakening it.

</details>


### [73] [Supervised Reinforcement Learning for the Coordination of Distributed Energy Resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24947)
*Haoyuan Deng,Yihong Zhou,Thomas Morstyn,Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受大语言模型训练范式启发的监督强化学习(SRL)框架，通过在演示数据上进行监督预训练，再经过离线和在线两步微调，有效解决了分布式能源协调中传统强化学习方法样本效率低和次优的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式能源资源(DERs)对电力系统脱碳至关重要，但其固有的不确定性和建模复杂性使得解锁其灵活性面临挑战。传统优化方法难以处理DERs的不确定性和复杂性，而标准强化学习方法存在样本效率低和从头训练次优的问题。

Method: 提出监督强化学习(SRL)框架，包含三个阶段：(1)在演示数据上以监督学习方式预训练策略；(2)离线微调以增强策略性能；(3)在线微调以适应真实世界动态。该方法借鉴了大语言模型的训练范式。

Result: 实验表明，基于所提出框架的强化学习实现显著优于所有基准方法，即使在低质量演示数据条件下也能实现高成本效益。

Conclusion: SRL框架成功将监督学习与强化学习相结合，通过预训练-微调范式有效提升了DER协调策略的学习效率和性能，为解决DER管理中的不确定性和复杂性提供了新思路。

Abstract: The increasing integration of distributed energy resources (DERs) is crucial for power system decarbonization, yet unlocking DERs' flexibility is challenged by their inherent uncertainties and modelling complexity. As traditional optimization methods struggle with such uncertainty and complexity of DERs, reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a promising alternative for DER management. However, standard RL methods suffer from sample inefficiency and sub-optimality when trained from scratch. Inspired by the training paradigms in large language models, this paper proposes a Supervised Reinforcement Learning (SRL) framework for learning DER coordination policies. This framework first pre-trains a policy on demonstration data in a supervised-learning fashion, which is then further fine-tuned using RL. Furthermore, we propose a two-step fine-tuning process: offline fine-tuning for enhancing policy performance and online fine-tuning for adapting it to the real-world dynamics. Experiments demonstrate that RL implementations based on the proposed framework significantly outperform all benchmarks, achieving high cost efficiency even under low-quality demonstration data.

</details>


### [74] [Cellular Predictions on the Move: What about Data?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25709)
*Natalia Vesselinova,Pauliina Ilmonen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过引入人口动态数据（潜在流量源数量和移动性）来改进蜂窝网络负载预测，在高速公路场景下预测精度提升约60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动蜂窝负载预测研究主要关注开发强大的学习结构以提高预测精度，所使用的数据传统上属于蜂窝领域，最多包含基站周围的外生信息。作者从数据作为学习过程关键组件的角度出发，假设当数据能够揭示产生蜂窝负载的过程时，可以获得显著的改进。

Method: 提出在传统历史时间序列移动数据流量基础上，增加表征人口动态的数据——即潜在蜂窝流量源数量及其移动性特征，来刻画产生蜂窝负载的根本过程。在较少研究的高速公路场景中验证该假设。

Result: 综合实验表明，仅通过使用人口动态数据，蜂窝负载预测的性能提升约60%，验证了所提假设的有效性。

Conclusion: 数据质量及其对负载生成过程的表征能力对预测性能至关重要。引入能够反映蜂窝流量产生机制的人口动态数据，可显著提升预测精度，为网络资源优化和服务质量保障提供了新思路。

Abstract: Mobile cellular load forecasting is native to network resource optimization and delivery of services with reliability, latency and quality guarantees. The mainstream of machine learning research in the area is focused primarily on developing powerful learning structures for improved prediction accuracy. The data used for forecasting traditionally belong to the cellular domain and at most contain exogenous information about the surroundings of the base stations. We approach the prediction task from the perspective of data as a vital component of any data learning process. We hypothesize that substantial improvements could be achieved when the data inform on the processes that create the cellular load. Specifically, we propose to characterize the population dynamics -- the potential number of cellular traffic sources and their mobility -- in addition to employing historical time series of mobile data traffic. We validate our hypothesis for the rarely examined highway scenario. Comprehensive experiments show forecasting improvements on the order of $60\%$ due to the use of these data alone.

</details>


### [75] [Holographic Memory for Zero-Shot Compositional Reasoning in Knowledge Graphs: A Mechanistic Study of Where and Why It Fails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24948)
*Randhir Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了全息记忆模型（HRR/FHRR）在知识图谱零样本组合查询中的表现，发现虽然单跳预测性能良好，但组合查询完全失败，并通过机制分析定位了失败的根本原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱嵌入模型在单跳链接预测上表现良好，但缺乏处理零样本组合查询（训练时未见过的多跳问题）的机制。全息约简表示（HRR）通过循环卷积实现符号的结合与解结合，理论上具有近似可逆和结合性，因此被认为是有潜力的候选方法。本文旨在验证这一理论承诺是否成立。

Method: 在FB15k-237数据集上测试两种全息记忆变体：实值HRR和相位-only傅里叶HRR（FHRR），均配备现代Hopfield清理机制。使用五种随机种子进行实验，并通过探测实验进行机制分析：hop-1探测评估中间实体恢复质量，以及独立原子查询探测评估组合链中事实的可检索性。

Result: 四个主要发现：（1）两种方法在单跳检索上具有竞争力（过滤MRR：HRR为0.358，FHRR为0.350）；（2）零样本组合完全失败，准确率始终处于随机水平；（3）失败机制定位：尽管hop-1探测显示中间实体恢复质量很高（HRR MRR 0.896），但组合链中的事实本身就更难检索（仅为平均原子准确率的0.26-0.48倍）；（4）证明FHRR的softmax清理机制缺乏相位等变性，加剧了少数hop-1错误的链的失败。

Conclusion: 零样本组合查询失败的瓶颈不在于结合-解结合代数或清理机制，而在于叠加记忆检索固有的容量和干扰问题——组合链中的事实在单跳层面就已经更难检索。解决该问题需要提高叠加条件下的检索容量，而非仅仅重新设计清理机制。

Abstract: Knowledge graph embedding (KGE) models predict single-hop links well but have no mechanism for zero-shot compositional queries: multi-hop questions whose relation chains never appeared during training. Holographic Reduced Representations (HRR), which bind and unbind symbols via circular convolution, are a theoretically attractive candidate, since binding is approximately invertible and associative. We test whether this promise holds.
  We study two holographic memory variants, real-valued HRR and phase-only Fourier HRR (FHRR), each with a modern Hopfield cleanup, on FB15k-237 over five seeds. Four findings follow. First, both are competitive single-hop retrievers (filtered MRR 0.358 +/- 0.002 for HRR, 0.350 +/- 0.021 for FHRR). Second, neither composes zero-shot: accuracy stays at chance across all cleanup temperatures. Third, the main contribution, we localise the failure mechanistically. A hop-1 probe shows the memory recovers the correct intermediate entity with high fidelity (MRR 0.896 +/- 0.002 for HRR), yet composition still fails even with a verified-correct intermediate. A second probe shows why: posing the ground-truth second-hop fact as a standalone atomic query, bypassing composition entirely, already recovers it at only 0.26 to 0.48x average atomic accuracy, uniformly across relation fan-out. The bottleneck is not the bind-unbind algebra or the cleanup; it is that facts compositional chains pass through are intrinsically harder for the superposed memory to retrieve, a capacity and interference effect present already at a single hop. Fourth, we prove (Lemma 4.1) that FHRR's softmax cleanup is not phase-equivariant, compounding the primary failure on the minority of chains where hop-1 itself errs. Fixing zero-shot composition requires improving retrieval capacity under superposition, not just redesigning the cleanup.

</details>


### [76] [MacroLens: A Multi-Task Benchmark for Contextual Financial Reasoning under Macroeconomic Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24950)
*Patara Trirat,Jin Myung Kwak,Jay Heo,Heejun Lee,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发布了MacroLens基准数据集，覆盖4,416只美国中小盘股票（2021-2026），整合价格、会计基本面、宏观经济和文本四类信号，支持金融决策的时序评估，解决文本发布日期、财报滞后、信息冗余和宏观状态跨期泄露四大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融决策具有情境依赖性，需综合考量价格历史、会计基本面、宏观经济状态和同期文本。然而，构建跨四类信号的基准面临四大时序评估假设违背问题：文本须按发布日期设门控以防前视偏差；季报基本面存在1-90天滞后；财报文本与数值字段部分冗余；宏观状态跨日历分割存在泄露。现有公开基准均未同时处理这四类信号，因此需要构建统一框架。

Method: 构建MacroLens数据集，覆盖2021-2026年4,416只美国中小盘股票，包含时点价格面板、4,680万条XBRL会计事实、53个宏观经济序列、295,860份SEC文件、215,882篇新闻文章，以及覆盖49类1,130个宏观经济事件的场景层（自动检测并渲染为自然语言）。设计七项任务：情境预测、公开/私有估值、基于基本面和描述的报表生成、场景条件回报、房地产估值。评估19种方法（朴素启发式、时序基础模型、微调LLM时序模型、零样本大语言模型），并对两类前沿LLM和梯度提升基线进行五步特征-情境消融实验。

Result: 成功构建首个同时整合价格、会计基本面、宏观经济和文本四类信号且严格处理时序假设违背的公开基准。MacroLens支持七项金融决策任务，覆盖多资产类别和多数据模态。对19种方法的评估揭示了不同模型家族在多信号融合下的性能差异，消融实验量化了特征与情境对模型表现的贡献。数据集已在HuggingFace公开发布。

Conclusion: MacroLens填补了金融时序评估基准的空白，为研究价格、基本面、宏观和文本信号的联合建模提供了标准化的实验平台，有助于推动金融决策中多模态融合方法的发展。

Abstract: Financial decision-making is contextual: forecasting prices, valuing companies, and assessing event exposure weigh price history, accounting fundamentals, macroeconomic regime, and contemporaneous text. A benchmark over these four signals is hard to build because finance violates four assumptions of time-series evaluation: text must be gated by its publication date to prevent look-ahead, quarterly fundamentals are reported with a one- to ninety-day lag, filing text is partly redundant with the numerical statement fields it accompanies, and macroeconomic regimes leak across calendar splits. No public benchmark addresses all four signals jointly. MacroLens covers 4,416 U.S. small- and micro-cap equities over 2021-2026. Seven tasks share one point-in-time panel of prices, 46.8M XBRL accounting facts, 53 macroeconomic series, 295,860 SEC filings, and 215,882 news articles, plus a scenario layer of 1,130 macroeconomic events across 49 types automatically detected and rendered as natural language. Tasks span contextual forecasting, public and private valuation, statement generation from fundamentals and descriptions, scenario-conditioned returns, and real-estate valuation. We evaluate 19 methods across six families spanning naive heuristics through time-series foundation models, fine-tuned LLM-based time-series models, and zero-shot large language models (LLMs), plus a five-step feature-context ablation on two frontier LLMs and a gradient-boosted baseline. MacroLens is released at https://huggingface.co/datasets/DeepAuto-AI/MacroLens.

</details>


### [77] [TL++: Accuracy and Privacy Preserving Traversal Learning for Distributed Intelligent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25627)
*Erdenebileg Batbaatar,Young Yoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TL++框架，一种双模式遍历学习方法，通过跨节点构建虚拟批次来恢复中心化小批量梯度行为。基础模式仅交换切割层激活和梯度，安全模式通过秘密共享保护切割层数据，在CIFAR-10上准确率超过基线12个百分点，通信量降低13.1倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式智能系统需要在数据孤岛间训练而不集中原始数据。联邦学习虽然保持数据本地化，但在异构数据分区下性能受损且需要反复传输完整模型。拆分学习通过切割层激活减少通信，但标准协议无法恢复中心化小批量梯度行为，且激活和梯度以明文形式暴露，存在隐私风险。

Method: TL++框架包含两种模式：(1)基础模式：交换切割层激活和梯度而非完整模型；(2)安全模式：将切割层激活和梯度在编排器和非合谋辅助服务器之间进行秘密共享，防止单个服务器观察明文切割层张量。该方法通过跨节点构建虚拟批次来恢复中心化小批量梯度行为，并进行了形式化分析和通信计算成本分析。

Result: 在CIFAR-10数据集上，TL++基础模式切割层1和精确安全模式切割层3分别达到91.41%(SD 0.19)和90.93%(SD 0.17)的准确率，超过最强的非TL++基线12个百分点以上。TL++基础模式切割层1相比完整模型同步，每步通信量减少13.1倍。PubMedQA结果同样显示TL++的优越性。

Conclusion: TL++框架成功接近中心化训练性能，同时减少通信量并提供激活级别的秘密共享保护。该方法在保持模型性能的同时解决了分布式学习中的通信效率和隐私保护问题，为数据孤岛场景下的模型训练提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Distributed intelligent systems increasingly need to train across data silos without centralizing raw data. Federated learning keeps data local but can suffer under heterogeneous partitions and requires repeated full-model exchange. Split learning reduces communication through cut-layer activations, but standard protocols generally do not recover centralized mini-batch gradient behavior and may expose activations and gradients in plaintext. We present TL++, a two-mode traversal-learning framework that constructs virtual batches across nodes to recover centralized mini-batch gradient behavior under explicit synchronization assumptions. Base mode exchanges cut-layer activations and gradients rather than full models. Secure mode secret-shares each cut-layer activation and gradient between an orchestrator and a non-colluding helper, preventing either server from observing plaintext cut-layer tensors. This protection is limited to a semi-honest two-server setting; labels and loss-related outputs remain visible to the orchestrator. In the lightweight secure path evaluated here, exactness requires a linear or affine server path, while nonlinear operations require nonlinear MPC or approximation. We formalize TL++, analyze communication and computation costs, and evaluate it against federated and split-learning baselines on CIFAR-10 and BioGPT/PubMedQA using full fine-tuning and LoRA. On CIFAR-10, TL++ base cut 1 and exact secure cut 3 achieve accuracies of 91.41% (SD 0.19) and 90.93% (SD 0.17), respectively, exceeding the strongest measured non-TL++ baseline by more than 12 percentage points. TL++ base cut 1 also reduces per-step communication by 13.1-fold relative to full-model synchronization. PubMedQA results similarly favor TL++. Overall, TL++ approaches centralized-training performance while reducing communication and providing activation-level secret sharing.

</details>


### [78] [How Complexity Contributes to Learning Opacity in Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24953)
*Joachim Stein,Eric Raidl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从复杂动力系统视角分析神经网络学习过程的不透明性，指出其对初始权重、梯度优化反馈和训练数据的敏感性导致学习过程本质不透明，部分不透明性可能不可消除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注机器学习预测结果的不透明性，而学习过程本身的不透明性（如神经网络权重值的时空演化）尚未得到充分探索。作者旨在揭示学习过程不透明的根本原因，以填补这一研究空白。

Method: 从复杂动力系统的理论视角分析神经网络学习过程，识别并论证了训练复杂性的三个关键特性：（1）对权重初始化的敏感性；（2）基于梯度的优化中的反馈机制；（3）对训练数据的敏感性。通过分析每个特性如何导致学习不透明性，建立理论框架。

Result: 研究表明学习不透明性源于动力复杂性和由此产生的认识论挑战。三个关键特性均对学习不透明性有贡献，且这些特性是学习过程的本质组成部分。抑制或消除这些特性将根本改变机器学习系统的学习方式。

Conclusion: 机器学习中某些不透明性来源可能是不可约减的，因为导致不透明性的特性是学习过程的基础要素。这表明完全透明化机器学习系统可能存在根本性障碍。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) algorithms are known to be opaque. We do not know the reasons for their predictions. The learning process leading to the prediction function is also opaque. We do not fully understand the time evolution of the weight values of neural nets (NN) and related dynamical phenomena. While prediction opacity is widely studied, learning opacity remains largely underexplored. This article studies learning opacity trough the lens of complex dynamical systems. We argue that NN learning is essentially a complex system and that learning opacity is due to dynamical complexity and the epistemological challenges that arise from it. We identify three key properties of training complexity -- sensitivity to weight initialization, feedback in gradient based optimization, and sensitivity to the training data -- and show how each contributes to learning opacity. As these properties are fundamental to the learning process damping or eliminating them would fundamentally alter how ML systems learn. Some sources of opacity in ML may hence be irreducible.

</details>

*Jinghan Wang,Yanjun Chen,Wei Zhang,Wentao Wu,Tianchen Liu,Gaoliang Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的故障特征对齐方法，通过近端策略优化学习故障类型特定的仿射变换，有效解决了旋转机械振动监测中仿真到实际数据的异质迁移问题，在多个数据集上实现了92.8%的跨设备线性探测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转机械振动健康监测面临实际运行数据中故障样本稀缺和数字孪生信号仿真到实际之间存在异质差距的挑战。现有领域自适应方法采用类别无关的全局变换，无法在保持类间可分性的同时弥合所有故障特定差距，且统一的源-目标混合会向数据丰富的正常类引入分布噪声。这些局限性源于将序列化、状态依赖的对齐问题作为一次性优化处理，每次校正变换同时重塑所有类分布，产生静态梯度下降无法解决的状态依赖性。

Method: 将特征对齐建模为连续动作马尔可夫决策过程，采用近端策略优化(PPO)求解。学习到的策略发出响应当前特征空间配置的故障类型特定仿射变换校正，通过双目标奖励机制平衡差距最小化与可分性保持。提出非对称感知策略：保留真实数据用于正常类，同时用策略对齐的仿真样本增强故障类。

Result: 在XJTU-SY、CWRU和自建回转支承轴承测试平台三个数据集上验证了强化学习驱动对齐方法的优越性能增益。跨设备线性探针（不重新训练编码器）实现了92.8%的准确率，证明了可迁移的监测能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的强化学习驱动的特征对齐方法有效解决了传统领域自适应方法在故障诊断任务中的局限性，通过故障类型特定的自适应校正策略，实现了仿真到实际的有效迁移，为旋转机械的跨设备健康监测提供了可靠的技术方案。

Abstract: Vibration-based health monitoring of rotating machinery requires reliable fault diagnosis under operational data constraints, yet condition assessment remains challenged by structural scarcity of fault events and heterogeneous sim-to-real gaps in digital twin-generated signals. Each fault type generates impulses with distinct periodicity, amplitude modulation, and spectral character, making feature-space discrepancies fundamentally heterogeneous across fault classes. Existing domain adaptation methods apply a class-agnostic global transformation that cannot close all fault-specific gaps without distorting inter-class separability, while uniform source-target mixing introduces distributional noise into the data-abundant Normal class. These limitations stem from treating a sequential, state-dependent alignment problem as a one-shot optimization. Each corrective transformation simultaneously reshapes all class distributions, creating state dependencies that static gradient descent cannot resolve. We formulate feature alignment as a continuous-action Markov decision process solved via Proximal Policy Optimization, where the learned policy issues fault-type-specific affine corrections responsive to the current feature-space configuration, with a dual-objective reward balancing gap minimization against separability preservation. An asymmetry-aware strategy reserves real data for the Normal class while augmenting fault classes with policy-aligned simulated samples. Validation across XJTU-SY, CWRU, and a self-built slewing bearing testbed confirms the dominant gain from reinforcement learning-driven alignment, and cross-equipment linear probing achieves 92.8% without encoder retraining, demonstrating transferable monitoring capability.

</details>


### [80] [Towards Continuous Power Forecasting: Practical Continual Learning for Real-World Energy Systems in Nonstationary Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24955)
*Yujiang He,Frederic Uhrweiller,Bernhard Sick*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出连续电力预测范式，将电力预测建模为持续学习问题，系统评估六种持续学习方法在真实电力数据集上的表现，证明该方法能在无大规模历史数据存储的情况下自适应分布漂移并缓解灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实能源市场中的电力预测模型必须在非平稳条件下运行，数据分布因天气变化、基础设施升级和消费行为改变而持续演化。现有模型面临严格的操作约束：历史数据有限或无法用于重复训练，且需要不间断的长期服务。传统静态离线预测方法难以应对这些挑战。

Method: 提出连续电力预测范式，基于自适应持续学习框架进行回归任务，系统研究三个方法论类别的六种代表性持续学习方法。在不同数据可访问性和更新策略的现实假设下进行评估，在真实电力数据集上进行实验验证。

Result: 实验表明持续学习方法使预测模型能够自适应分布漂移、随时间积累知识，并在不依赖大规模历史数据存储的情况下缓解灾难性遗忘。研究提供了不同持续学习方法在现实操作约束下的稳定性和适应性行为的实践见解。

Conclusion: 本研究展示了如何将持续学习务实地集成到工业电力预测流程中，为动态环境中的长期部署提供可扩展和可持续的解决方案，为能源市场的长期预测服务提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Power forecasting models deployed in real-world energy markets must operate under nonstationary conditions, where data distributions continually evolve due to weather variability, infrastructure upgrades, and changing consumption behaviors. In practice, these models face strict operational constraints: historical data may be limited or unavailable for repeated retraining, and uninterrupted long-term service is often required. This paper addresses these challenges by proposing the paradigm of Continuous Power Forecasting, which views power forecasting as a continual learning problem rather than a static offline task. Based on an adaptive continual learning framework for regression, we systematically investigate the practical effectiveness of six representative continual learning approaches from three methodological categories. These approaches are evaluated under different realistic assumptions regarding data accessibility and update policies. Experimental validation on real-world power datasets demonstrates that continual learning enables forecasting models to self-adapt to distributional drift, accumulate knowledge over time, and mitigate catastrophic forgetting without relying on large-scale historical data storage. Beyond performance gains, our study provides practical insights into the stability and adaptation behaviors of different continual learning approaches under realistic operational constraints. Overall, this work illustrates how continual learning can be pragmatically integrated into industrial power forecasting pipelines, offering a scalable and sustainable solution for long-term deployment in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [81] [Convex--Concave Quadratic Spectral Filtering for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24956)
*Ranhui Yan,Jia Cai,Mengzhu Chen,Haodong Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DCQ-GNN，一种基于自适应凸凹二次滤波器组的谱图神经网络，在保持低阶计算效率的同时实现高谱选择性，在同配和异配图上表现优异且对结构扰动具有强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有谱图神经网络存在权衡难题：低阶谱滤波器虽然高效但频谱选择性有限，导致通带外衰减不足；高阶滤波器虽能提升选择性却引入优化挑战。作者旨在设计一种既能保持低阶计算优势又能实现高谱选择性的滤波器架构。

Method: 提出DCQ-GNN模型，采用紧凑的自适应凸凹二次滤波器组。通过将滤波器阶数限制为二阶并显式利用互补曲率特性，在不使用高阶多项式展开的情况下提升谱选择性。模型通过节点自适应门控机制融合滤波器输出，实现节点级的结构感知谱选择。

Result: 在10个数据集上的广泛实验表明：DCQ-GNN在异配图上并列第一（平均排名3.0），在同配图上获得第二名（排名4.2），与代表性高阶多项式谱滤波器保持竞争力。在强结构扰动下，相比一阶和高阶基线方法，DCQ-GNN的性能下降显著更小。

Conclusion: 基于曲率感知的二次滤波器组提供了高阶谱模型的鲁棒高效替代方案，在保持优化稳定性和计算效率的同时实现了优异性能，验证了低阶滤波器通过恰当设计也能达到高谱选择性。

Abstract: Spectral graph neural networks (GNNs) interpret message passing as frequency-selective filtering. While low-order spectral filters are efficient, their limited selectivity often leads to weak attenuation outside the passband, whereas high-order alternatives introduce optimization challenges. We propose DCQ-GNN, a spectral GNN based on a compact bank of adaptive convex--concave quadratic filters. By restricting the filter order to two while explicitly exploiting complementary curvature, DCQ-GNN improves spectral selectivity as quantified by Dirichlet energy and entropy measures without resorting to high-order polynomial expansions. The model fuses filter outputs through a node-adaptive gating mechanism to enable node-wise structure-aware spectral selection. We provide a formal spectral analysis grounded in Dirichlet energy attenuation, von Neumann entropy, and curvature polarity, and derive explicit characterizations of filter behavior across varying levels of homophily and structural perturbations. Extensive benchmarks on 10 datasets show that DCQ-GNN ties for the top average rank (3.0) on heterophilic graphs and obtains the second-best rank (4.2) on homophilic graphs, remaining competitive with representative high-order polynomial spectral filters. Furthermore, under strong structural perturbations, DCQ-GNN exhibits substantially smaller performance degradation compared to both first-order and high-order baselines. These results demonstrate that curvature-aware quadratic banks provide a robust and efficient alternative to high-order spectral models while preserving optimization stability and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [82] [Swarm-Inspired Generation of Collective Behaviors in Graph Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24958)
*Ji Chen,Song Chen,Chengzhang Gong,Li Fan,Chao Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SIES框架，通过学习可泛化的局部交互规则，在同步控制、机器人协调和异质图表示学习等任务上实现了跨网络规模、动态和任务的集体行为控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群体行为研究的核心挑战不仅在于解释集体行为如何涌现，更在于设计能够产生期望全局组织且跨图结构、动力学和任务泛化的局部交互规则。

Method: 提出群体启发式涌现同步器(SIES)框架，将节点建模为具有状态和任务线索的智能体式动态单元，采用有符号源-目标条件注意力作为显式演化模型内的自适应耦合项，结合显式动力学引擎与局部智能体智能。

Result: 在同步控制任务中，SIES学习的耦合算子可跨未训练网络规模、目标相位关系和内在节点动力学产生规定同步模式，且比三个振荡器基线更快达到步态相关模式；在图表示学习中，在异质节点分类基准上达到最优性能。学习到的算子还可泛化到不同规模的模拟多足机器人及腿部失效后的物理六足机器人。

Conclusion: SIES是一个可泛化、可学习的图-动态交互框架，在同步控制、自适应机器人协调和异质图表示学习方面具有广阔应用前景。

Abstract: Collective behavior arises when locally interacting units produce coordinated global organization, from synchronization in dynamical systems to task-relevant information flow on graphs. The central challenge is not only to explain how collective behavior emerges, but to design local interaction rules that can produce desired global organization and generalize across graphs, dynamics and tasks.To address this challenge, we introduce the Swarm-Inspired Emergent Synchronizer (SIES), a graph-dynamical framework that learns generalizable local-interaction laws for controllable collective organization. Each node is an agent-like dynamical unit with a state and task cue, and signed source-target-conditioned attention acts as an adaptive coupling term inside an explicit evolution model. Therefore, SIES combines an explicit dynamical engine with local agent intelligence, similar to biological swarms. For synchronization control, SIES learns a generalizable coupling operator that produces prescribed synchronization patterns for CDSs across untrained network scales, target phase relations, and intrinsic node dynamics without retraining. The learned operator also reaches gait-related modes faster than three oscillator baselines and generalizes synchronization-driven locomotion to simulated multi-legged robots of different scales and a physical hexapod after leg disablement. For graph representation learning, SIES applies the same signed interaction principle to message passing and achieves the highest performance among the compared methods on heterophilous node-classification benchmarks. Together, these results position SIES as a generalizable and learnable graph-dynamical interaction framework with promise for synchronization control, adaptive robot coordination, and heterophilous graph representation learning.

</details>


### [83] [Reliable Conformal Prediction for Ordinal Classification Using the Ranked Probability Score](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24959)
*Stefan Haas,Luca Killmaier,Alireza Javanmardi,Eyke Hüllermeier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于排位概率得分（RPS）的保序分类保形预测方法，能够生成以中位数为中心的连续预测集，在预测集宽度和保序误覆盖严重程度之间取得了更好的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医疗和金融等高风险领域的保序分类问题中，不确定性量化必须考虑保序错误的严重程度。虽然保形预测提供了具有边缘覆盖保证的无分布预测集，但其实际效果关键取决于非一致性函数的选择。RPS作为定义在累积预测分布上的正则评分规则，能够自然反映保序风险，但在保形保序预测中一直被忽视。

Method: 作者提出了一种基于排位概率得分（RPS）的保形预测方法用于保序分类。RPS被用作非一致性度量，能够通过构造生成以中位数为中心的连续预测集。该方法具有模型无关性，支持评估型和分组型有序分类结果，并且相比贪婪区间选择程序支持高效实现。

Result: 在多个保序图像和表格数据集上的实验表明，基于RPS的保形预测方法能够生成连续预测集，并且在预测集宽度和保序误覆盖的严重程度之间，相对于现有保形预测方法取得了有利的平衡。

Conclusion: RPS是一种适合保序分类的非一致性度量，能够自然地生成连续的、以中位数为中心的预测集，为高风险领域的保序分类问题提供了一种有效的不确定性量化方法。

Abstract: Ordinal classification (OC) arises in high-stakes domains such as medicine and finance, where uncertainty quantification must account for the severity of ordinal errors. Conformal prediction (CP) provides distribution-free prediction sets with marginal coverage guarantees; however, its practical effectiveness depends critically on the choice of nonconformity function. We introduce a CP method for ordinal classification based on the ranked probability score (RPS), a proper scoring rule defined over cumulative predictive distributions. Although it reflects ordinal risk quite naturally, it has largely been neglected in conformal ordinal prediction (COP). When used as a measure of nonconformity, RPS yields median-centered contiguous prediction sets by construction. The method is model-agnostic, supports both assessed and grouped ordered categorical outcomes, and permits efficient implementation compared to greedy interval selection procedures. Across multiple ordinal image and tabular datasets, RPS-based CP produces contiguous prediction sets and strikes a favorable balance between prediction set width and the magnitude of ordinal miscoverage relative to existing CP methods.

</details>


### [84] [Enhancing Clinician Decision-Making via Uncertainty-Aware Multi-Expert Fusion for Stroke Rehabilitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24960)
*Tamim Ahmed,Thanassis Rikakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了xAARA系统，通过多视角视频为105名卒中患者提供带有校准不确定性和可解释性的ARAT评估，任务准确率达94.2%，将预测不确定性降低96.1%，并获得临床医生认可。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有卒中康复运动质量评估方法存在瓶颈：传统量表（如ARAT）将丰富的行为观察压缩为单一序数终点，丢失了区分恢复与代偿的运动质量细节；自动化方法通常追求不透明评分的准确率，难以应用于临床实践。

Method: 将临床评分视为不适定推理问题，从多视角视频中提取信息。系统由动态贝叶斯网络通过基于熵的门控机制组合692个校准的多模态模型，在任务、运动阶段和运动质量三个层级返回带有校准不确定性和解释的ARAT评估，并根据临床有效性规则对结果进行验证，对低置信度案例进行延迟处理。

Result: 在105名卒中患者（788项运动）中，任务准确率达94.2%（Cohen's kappa=0.934），运动阶段准确率达81.3%（kappa=0.727）。与单一临床医生评分相比，预测不确定性降低96.1%。对于主观案例，100%与至少一位评分者匹配，且从未返回超出范围的评分。四位独立临床医生验证了评估结果并表示愿意采用该系统。

Conclusion: 原则性的不确定性量化和与临床医生对齐的可解释性，是将自动化评估从技术演示转化为可部署临床工具的关键桥梁，系统设计旨在增强而非替代临床判断。

Abstract: Tailoring stroke rehabilitation requires assessing how movements are organized, not merely if they succeed. Currently, this assessment is a rate-limiting bottleneck. Instruments like the Action Research Arm Test (ARAT) compress rich behavioral observations into single ordinal endpoints, discarding the movement-quality details that distinguish recovery from compensation. Automated alternatives typically chase accuracy on noisy, single-observer labels to output opaque scores - a technology-centric approach that rarely reaches clinical practice. To address this, we present xAARA: an engine designed to augment rather than replace clinical judgment. From multi-view video, xAARA returns ARAT assessments with calibrated uncertainty and explanations across task, movement-phase, and movement-quality levels. Treating clinical scoring as an ill-posed inference problem, xAARA composes 692 calibrated multimodal models via a Dynamic Bayesian Network with entropy-based gating. It qualifies results against clinical validity rules and defers low-confidence cases. In 105 stroke survivors (788 exercises), xAARA achieved 94.2% task accuracy (Cohen's kappa=0.934) and 81.3% movement-phase accuracy (kappa=0.727), reducing predictive uncertainty by 96.1% compared to single-clinician scoring. For subjective cases, it matched at least one rater 100% of the time and never returned out-of-range scores. Four independent clinicians validated the assessments and indicated willingness to adopt the system. We argue that principled uncertainty quantification and clinician-aligned explainability are the critical bridges moving automated assessment from technical demonstration to a deployable clinical tool.

</details>


### [85] [Towards Scalable Multi-Task Reinforcement Learning with Large Decision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24962)
*Thibaut Kulak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了LDM-v0，这是一个基于Transformer的大型决策模型，通过在数千个异构强化学习环境上进行离线预训练，单一模型即可在约1000个不同任务上达到专用策略的性能水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受大规模序列建模进展的启发，研究者旨在探索是否可以跨大量异构强化学习环境训练一个统一的Transformer策略，从而打破传统强化学习中每个任务需要单独训练模型的局限性。

Method: 构建了LDM-v0模型，这是一个多任务、多模态的Transformer策略网络，以观测、动作、奖励和终止信号的历史序列为条件输入。采用离线学习方式，通过对来自数千个环境（涵盖机器人、自动驾驶、库存管理、网络安全、交易和视频游戏等多个领域）的轨迹数据进行监督式下一动作预测来训练模型。

Result: 实验结果表明，单个预训练的LDM-v0模型在约1000个不同环境中的性能表现，能够与独立训练的任务特定参考策略相媲美，证明了该方法的广泛适用性和有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究成功证明了使用单一Transformer策略进行大规模跨异构强化学习环境离线预训练的可行性，为构建通用的决策大模型提供了实证支持和方法论参考。

Abstract: Recent progress in large-scale sequence modeling has shown that a single model can learn useful representations across highly diverse data distributions. Inspired by these advances, we investigate whether a unified transformer policy can be trained across large collections of heterogeneous reinforcement learning environments.
  We introduce LDM-v0, a Large Decision Model trained offline on trajectories collected from thousands of environments spanning multiple domains and modalities. LDM-v0 is a multi-task, multi-modal transformer policy conditioned on histories of observations, actions, rewards, and termination signals, and trained through supervised next-action prediction over offline trajectories. We describe the environment infrastructure, automated data generation pipeline, model architecture, and training methodology used to build LDM-v0, and evaluate its performance across diverse environments. We show that a single pretrained model matches the performance of independently trained task-specific reference policies on approximately 1,000 environments including robotics, autonomous driving, inventory management, cybersecurity, trading, and video games. These results demonstrate the feasibility of large-scale offline pretraining across heterogeneous reinforcement learning environments using a single transformer policy.

</details>


### [86] [Evidence for feature-specific error correction in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24964)
*Francisco Ferreira da Silva,Stefan Heimersheim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次实证验证了大语言模型(LLMs)在超位置(superposition)中进行计算时存在误差修正机制，且特征方向相比随机方向具有特权性(privileged)，支持了超位置计算的理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解大语言模型的特征表示是可解释性的核心目标。理论上预测LLMs使用超位置表示超出其维度的特征，并在超位置中进行计算需要误差修正机制来赋予特征方向特权性，但这一预测缺乏实证检验。

Method: 提出基于激活扰动的实证测试方法：1) 对残差流激活进行扰动，观察其对扰动的鲁棒性；2) 对比候选特征方向（对比提示对构建的"纯"方向）与混合方向的鲁棒性；3) 将扰动效应建模为特征分量Lp范数的函数，通过p值量化特征方向的特权性。

Result: 发现激活对小幅扰动具有鲁棒性（形成激活高原），且纯方向比混合方向更脆弱，表明特征方向被特权化；对于对比方向、MELBO和SAE解码器方向测得p>2（符合特征特定误差修正），而随机和PCA方向（控制组）测得p≈2。结果在Gemma-2-9B、Qwen3-1.7B、Llama-3.1-8B、Mistral-7B-v0.3、Aya-Expanse-8B和Yi-1.5-9B上复现，且在具有已知真实特征的玩具模型上得到验证。

Conclusion: 首次为超位置计算中的误差修正机制提供了实证证据，证明LLMs中存在特征方向的特权化现象，支持了超位置计算的理论框架，为理解LLMs的内部表征机制提供了新的实验方法和理论洞见。

Abstract: Understanding the features of large language models (LLMs) is a central goal of interpretability. LLMs are commonly assumed to use superposition to represent more features than they have dimensions. They may not only represent features in superposition but also perform computation in superposition. Theory predicts that computing in superposition requires error correction that privileges feature directions over generic ones, but this prediction has not been tested empirically. We propose an empirical test of error correction in LLMs based on activation perturbations. Perturbing residual-stream activations, we find that they are robust to small perturbations--forming activation plateaus consistent with error correction--but less robust along candidate feature directions ("pure" directions, constructed from contrastive prompt pairs) than along mixtures of two such directions, indicating that the pure directions are privileged. We quantify this privilegedness by modeling the perturbation effect as a function of the $L^p$-norm of its decomposition into feature components. For $p=2$ the response is a quadratic form with at most as many nonzero eigenvalues as the residual-stream dimension, which cannot privilege the many feature directions superposition requires. $p>2$ lifts this constraint and is consistent with feature-specific error correction. We find $p>2$ for contrastive, MELBO, and SAE-decoder directions, and $p\approx2$ for random and PCA directions (controls). These results replicate across Gemma-2-9B, Qwen3-1.7B, Llama-3.1-8B, Mistral-7B-v0.3, Aya-Expanse-8B, and Yi-1.5-9B. We further validate our method on a toy model of error correction with known ground-truth features, recovering $p>2$ for true feature directions, degrading toward $2$ as we rotate away from them.

</details>


### [87] [Learning Dynamical Systems from Multiple Sparse Datasets: A Hierarchical Bayesian Modeling Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24966)
*Cristian Brugnara,Lea Multerer,Marco Forgione,Laura Azzimonti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于动力系统的分层贝叶斯元学习框架，通过建模共享总体分布来有效利用多个相关数据集，在稀疏、噪声和不规则采样数据下实现了改进的参数估计和预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏、噪声和不规则采样数据中估计动力系统参数通常是严重病态的问题。当存在多个相关数据集时，如果能正确建模共享结构和变异性，可以提供额外的信息来改善参数估计。

Method: 提出分层贝叶斯框架进行动力系统的概率元学习，将数据集特定参数建模为从共享总体分布中抽取的样本。在基于梯度的MCMC中嵌入数值ODE求解器，实现对共享总体和数据集特定参数分布的高效后验推断。

Result: 实验结果表明，与无池化方法相比，该方法在预测性能上有所改善，展示了在稀疏数据设置下数据高效系统辨识的潜力。

Conclusion: 该分层贝叶斯框架能够有效利用多个相关数据集的共享信息，为解决稀疏、噪声和不规则采样数据下的动力系统参数估计问题提供了一种有前景的方法。

Abstract: Estimating parameters of dynamical systems from sparse, noisy, and irregularly sampled data is often severely ill-conditioned. When multiple related datasets are available, they provide additional information if the shared structure and variability are properly modeled. We propose a hierarchical Bayesian framework for probabilistic meta-learning in dynamical systems, modeling dataset-specific parameters as draws from a shared population distribution. A numerical ODE solver is embedded within gradient-based MCMC to enable efficient posterior inference of the shared population and dataset-specific parameter distribution. Experiments show improved predictive performance over unpooled methods, highlighting the potential for data-efficient system identification in settings with sparse data.

</details>


### [88] [What Do Language Priors Contribute to Darcy-Flow Inversion? A Mechanistic Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24967)
*Taiga Saito,Yu Otake,Daijiro Mizutani,Sopheakpolin Mom*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出使用句子嵌入作为接口，将地质描述注入学习的达西流反演求解器，相比无文本基线重建误差降低81%，证明语言先验可有效融合工程知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不适定反问题中，恢复解高度依赖先验信息，但工程知识通常以定性描述而非数学形式记录。现有方法难以将非结构化的地质描述有效整合到学习型反演求解器中，亟需一种能将定性工程知识注入求解过程的接口机制。

Method: 采用句子嵌入作为推理时接口，将地质文本描述条件化注入学习的达西流反演求解器。在六个合成地质类别和SPE10基准油藏模型上进行实验，仅改变条件表示方式，对比文本条件与无文本反事实场景，分析类级约束与几何细节的贡献差异。

Result: 文本条件相比无文本基线重建误差降低81%。主要增益来自类级分类约束，其价值集中在水头使传导率场欠定的区域，类内几何细节为次要且依赖模式。与离散类标签相比，句子嵌入对密集观测精度提升有限，但改善训练稳定性，支持转述敏感性分析和开放词汇输入。

Conclusion: 语言先验可作为工程信息学接口，有效将地质知识注入学习的反演求解器。研究明确了语言先验的作用条件与信号本质，为定性工程知识在反问题求解中的形式化应用提供了新途径。

Abstract: In ill-posed inverse problems, the recovered solution depends as much on the prior as on the data, yet much of the engineering knowledge that could serve as that prior is recorded qualitatively rather than in formal mathematical form. Here we test whether sentence embeddings can act as an inference-time interface for injecting geological descriptions into a learned Darcy-flow inverse solver. Across six synthetic geological classes and an exploratory transfer to a benchmark reservoir model (SPE10), we vary only the conditioning representation and find that text conditioning reduces reconstruction error by 81 % relative to a no-text counterfactual. Most of this gain comes from a categorical, class-level constraint whose value concentrates where the hydraulic head leaves the conductivity field underdetermined, while within-class geometric detail is secondary and pattern-dependent. Compared with a discrete class label, sentence embeddings add little dense-observation accuracy but improve training stability and enable paraphrase-based sensitivity analysis and open-vocabulary inputs. These results show that language priors can serve as an engineering-informatics interface for injecting geological knowledge into learned inverse solvers, while clarifying when they help and what signal they actually carry.

</details>


### [89] [Training Dynamics of Neural Software Defect Predictors under Coupled Data-Quality Issues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24968)
*Emmanuel Charleson Dapaah,Philip Makedonski,Jens Grabowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究旨在探究深度学习软件缺陷预测中，类别不平衡与类别重叠及其耦合如何影响神经网络训练动态，提出交互感知的实证协议和训练动态模式分类体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于度量的软件缺陷预测（SDP）数据集常同时存在类别不平衡和类别重叠这两种数据质量问题。先前研究主要通过最终性能评估其影响，且多孤立考察单一问题，缺乏对耦合数据质量问题下神经网络内部训练动态（梯度、权重、偏置、误差轨迹）表现模式的深入理解。

Method: 在类级别UBD数据集上开展控制干预研究，训练固定的多层感知机（MLP）。实验设置包括仅不平衡、仅重叠以及两者耦合三种条件，每种条件重复五个随机种子。逐轮次记录训练动态，通过耦合比率监控保真度，并利用效应量、轨迹分析、敏感性分析和基于规则的分类方法来刻画训练动态模式。

Result: 预期产出交互感知的实证协议以及针对度量型SDP中耦合数据质量问题的训练动态模式候选分类法。

Conclusion: 该研究将填补耦合数据质量问题下神经网络训练动态模式认知的空白，为深度学习在软件缺陷预测领域的应用提供更深入的理论依据和分析框架。

Abstract: Context: Software defect prediction supports maintenance decisions such as testing prioritization, release-risk assessment, and quality monitoring. However, metric-based SDP datasets often contain coupled data-quality issues, especially class imbalance and class overlap. Prior work has mainly measured their impact through endpoint performance, while recent evidence suggests that such issues may also appear in neural training dynamics (gradients, weights, biases, error trajectories). However, these studies examine issues in isolation, leaving open how internal neural network training patterns manifest when data quality issues are coupled.
  Objective: We investigate how training-dynamics patterns from class imbalance, overlap, and their coupling can be characterized under interaction-aware conditions in deep learning-based SDP.
  Method: We conduct a controlled intervention study on class-level UBD datasets, training a fixed MLP under imbalance-only, overlap-only, and joint conditions across five seeds. Training dynamics are logged per epoch; fidelity is monitored via coupling ratios. Patterns are characterized using effect sizes, trajectories, sensitivity analyses, and rule-based classification.
  Expected contribution: The study will produce an interaction-aware empirical protocol and a candidate taxonomy of training-dynamics patterns for coupled data-quality issues in metric-based SDP.

</details>


### [90] [Frequency Domain Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24969)
*Klaus Schertler,Xiomara Runge,Andrea Ceni,David Kappel,Claudio Gallicchio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FRESCO，一种完全在频域运行的回声状态网络(ESN)架构，通过避免域转换开销将复杂的非线性循环更新复杂度从O(N²)降低到O(N)，在降低计算成本和能耗的同时在多项基准测试中达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer的二次序列长度瓶颈促使循环模型复兴，但现有方法难以平衡高效训练与高表达性潜在状态。回声状态网络(ESN)通过固定循环权重避免了时间反向传播，但在处理复杂任务时需要大型储备池，导致O(N²)的状态更新瓶颈，无法匹配当代循环模型的规模。

Method: 提出了频域储备池计算(FRESCO)架构：(1)采用新颖的维度零填充输入嵌入；(2)使用打包FDh读出；(3)原生应用频域非线性。该架构完全在频域中运行，避免了域转换开销，实现了O(N)复杂度的密集非线性循环更新。

Result: FRESCO显著降低了训练和推理的计算成本与能耗，同时在记忆基准测试、序列分类和多变量长期预测任务中匹配了最先进的预测性能。

Conclusion: FRESCO为密集循环架构提供了一条可扩展的前进道路，成功解决了ESN的状态更新瓶颈问题，使其能够匹配当代大规模循环模型的规模，同时保持高效性和表达性。

Abstract: While the quadratic sequence-length bottleneck of transformers has fueled a resurgence in recurrent models, effectively capturing complex dynamics requires architectures that balance efficient training with highly expressive latent states. Echo State Networks (ESNs) offer a compelling approach by utilizing fixed recurrent weights to circumvent backpropagation through time, enabling a closed-form training solution. However, achieving the expressivity needed for complex tasks demands large reservoirs, exposing an $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ state-update bottleneck that prevents ESNs from matching the scale of contemporary recurrent models. To address this limitation, we introduce Frequency Domain Reservoir Computing (FRESCO), an ESN architecture operating entirely in the frequency domain while avoiding domain-shift overheads to achieve $\mathcal{O}(N)$ complexity for dense, non-linear recurrent updates. By employing a novel dimensional zero-padding input embedding, a packed \FDh readout, and a natively applied frequency-domain non-linearity, FRESCO drastically reduces computational costs and energy consumption of training and inference. Furthermore, FRESCO matches the state-of-the-art predictive performance on memory benchmarks, sequential classification, and multivariate long-horizon forecasting, offering a scalable path forward for dense recurrent architectures.

</details>


### [91] [Don't Go Breaking My LLM: The Impact of Pruning Attention Layers on Explanation Faithfulness and Confidence Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24970)
*Pietro Tropeano,Maria Maistro,Tuukka Ruotsalo,Christina Lioma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，对大型语言模型进行注意力层剪枝虽然能保持较高准确率，但会显著降低模型的可解释性（忠实度）和置信度校准性能，揭示了准确率与可解释性、可靠性之间的错位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已证明可以剪枝高达33%的注意力层且准确率损失最小，但注意力剪枝对模型可解释性（特别是忠实度和置信度校准）的影响尚未被研究。本文旨在填补这一研究空白。

Method: 在五个大型语言模型和八个数据集上系统研究注意力层剪枝对解释忠实度和置信度校准的影响，并与模型准确率进行对比分析。

Result: 剪枝后的模型虽然通常能保持高准确率，但其忠实度和校准性能经常出现下降。值得注意的是，即使准确率保持稳定，忠实度和校准性能也会出现显著波动，揭示了模型置信度、可解释性和准确率之间的错位。

Conclusion: 层剪枝会以准确率和效率指标无法捕捉的方式影响LLM的可解释性和可靠性，建议在评估剪枝模型时应纳入可解释性和校准指标，而不仅仅依赖准确率和效率度量。

Abstract: Pruning Large Language Models (LLMs) reduces memory and inference costs by removing parts of the network, producing smaller models that retain most of their accuracy. As attention layers are the most resource-intensive parts of LLMs, pruning them is a promising compression strategy. Prior work shows that up to 33% of attention layers can be pruned with minimal accuracy loss. Nevertheless, the impact of attention pruning on model interpretability, specifically faithfulness and confidence calibration, remains unstudied. To address this gap, we study how pruning attention layers affects explanation faithfulness and confidence calibration across five LLMs and eight datasets. While the pruned models often maintain high accuracy, we find that their faithfulness and calibration often degrade. Notably, faithfulness and calibration can fluctuate significantly, even when accuracy remains stable, highlighting a misalignment between model confidence, interpretability, and accuracy. Our findings suggest that layer pruning can affect LLMs' interpretability and reliability in ways not captured by accuracy and efficiency measures alone. We recommend including explainability and calibration metrics when evaluating pruned models.

</details>


### [92] [Quantifying Explainable AI-introduced signal noise on ECG data with Spectral Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24974)
*David A. Kelly,Nathan Blake*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用谱熵作为衡量可解释性AI(XAI)输出中噪声的指标，并在心律失常分类的ECG数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医疗领域等需要高可信度的应用场景中，深度学习模型需要可解释性。然而，现有的可解释性技术(XAI)使用的启发式方法往往会在解释核心中引入信号噪声，导致难以区分哪些信号来自模型本身，哪些是XAI工具引入的噪声。

Method: 提出使用谱熵(spectral entropy)作为量化XAI输出中噪声的度量指标。在心律失常分类的ECG数据集上，使用不同的后置可解释性技术进行实验验证。

Result: 证明了谱熵能够有效地衡量XAI输出中的噪声水平，为区分模型真实信号和XAI工具引入的噪声提供了可靠的方法。

Conclusion: 谱熵作为一种客观的噪声度量指标，可以帮助评估和比较不同可解释性技术的质量，从而为医疗等关键领域的模型解释提供更可靠的依据。

Abstract: Explainability techniques are used to assess the output of various deep learning models. This is especially true in healthcare, where models need to be trusted and decisions justified. Explainability (XAI) tools use heuristics which often add signal noise to the explanation "core". It is not always obvious what is signal from the model and what is noise from the XAI. We propose the use of spectral entropy as a measure of noise in XAI output. We demonstrate its usefulness in the context of classifying arrhythmias in an ECG dataset with different post hoc explainability techniques.

</details>


### [93] [Why Do Accumulated Transformations Extrapolate?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24975)
*Mahesh Godavarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现RoPE中基于位置的旋转替换为累积的token依赖变换（如PaTH Attention）能改善长度外推，但在极长上下文时性能下降是累积正交变换的普遍性质，而非特定方法的问题。理论证明累积旋转会形成有限混合窗口，抑制远距离token的注意力；同时存在下界限制，必须在远距离质量控制和近距离信号保留间权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PaTH Attention使用累积的Householder反射替代RoPE的位置索引旋转，展现出良好的长度外推能力，但在极端上下文长度时性能下降。作者探究这一现象是Householder结构特有的，还是累积变换沿源到查询路径的一般性质，旨在理解长度外推的理论机制。

Method: 提出更简单的变体，保持RoPE的块对角SO(2)旋转结构，但用累积的token依赖角度替代位置索引角度。理论证明累积正交变换在满足正则性条件下，其乘积在有限步后变得不相关；推导出高维集中性产生分数差距抑制远距离token，同时近距离传输保留目标信号；建立下界证明累积旋转必须在远距离集合增长时退化。

Result: 实验验证：随机累积旋转显著改善RoPE的外推能力；学习的token依赖旋转在远超训练上下文的长度上维持近训练长度的困惑度；旋转values比仅旋转queries和keys效果更好。旋转模型在极端长度仍退化，而ALiBi保持长度稳定，与远距离质量控制需求一致。

Conclusion: 累积旋转改善外推但最终退化是累积正交变换的一般性质：有限的混合窗口独立于上下文长度，训练中学到的per-token抑制在任意评估长度上不变传递。必须显式控制远距离质量才能在极长上下文中保持性能，这解释了为何ALiBi等方法在长度稳定性上更优。

Abstract: PaTH Attention showed that replacing RoPE's position-indexed rotations with accumulated data-dependent Householder reflections yields strong length extrapolation, though performance degrades at extreme context lengths. We ask whether this depends on Householder-specific structure or reflects a general property of accumulated transformations along source-to-query paths. We study a simpler variant keeping RoPE's block-diagonal SO(2) rotations but replacing position-indexed angles with accumulated token-dependent ones. It shows the same pattern: improved extrapolation then degradation at long contexts. We prove the result extends to accumulated orthogonal transformations satisfying certain regularity conditions: their products become incoherent after finitely many steps, suppressing attention to distant tokens. Accumulated rotations of queries and keys create a finite mixing window independent of context length; per-token suppression learned in training transfers unchanged to any evaluation length, and high-dimensional concentration produces a score gap suppressing far tokens while near-route transport preserves the target signal. Conversely, a lower bound shows accumulated rotations must eventually degrade: as the far set grows, no rotations preserve the near signal without explicit far-mass control. For SO(2) rotations, rotating values too makes residual far contributions combine incoherently, extending the range. Controlled experiments support these predictions: random accumulated rotations substantially improve extrapolation over RoPE, learned token-dependent rotations maintain near-training-length perplexity far beyond the training context, and rotating values helps over queries and keys alone. Rotation-only models still degrade at extreme lengths, while ALiBi stays length-stable, consistent with the need for far-mass control.

</details>


### [94] [Auto-Configured Explainable Graph Neural Networks for Multi-Site Pollution Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24978)
*Abdelkader Dairi,Fouzi Harrou,Ying Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于混淆矩阵的动态图构建方法和混合损失函数，GraphSage在PM浓度预测中表现最优，优于传统机器学习和深度学习模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的颗粒物(PM)预测对缓解空气污染至关重要。现有图神经网络(GNNs)在建模时空依赖性方面有效，但预定义图限制了适应性，且某些数据集增加了学习难度。

Method: 提出基于监督学习过程中混淆矩阵的图构建方法，动态捕获类间关系；采用结合能量距离和Huber损失的混合损失函数，解决梯度消失问题并提高学习稳定性。使用犹他大学AirU污染监测网络数据，评估了五种GNN模型（GCNs、SGConv、GINs、GATs、GraphSage），并应用GNNExplainer和PGExplainer进行模型可解释性分析。

Result: GraphSage在PM₁、PM₁₀和PM₂.₅浓度的单步和多步预测中均达到最高精度。GNN模型性能优于传统机器学习模型和深度学习模型（Prophet、LSTM、GRU）。模型可解释性分析确保了透明度。

Conclusion: 所提出的动态图构建方法和混合损失函数有效提升了PM预测精度，GraphSage是空气污染预测任务的最优选择，结合可解释性方法确保了模型的透明度和可靠性。

Abstract: Accurate particulate matter (PM) prediction is crucial for mitigating air pollution. Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) effectively model spatiotemporal dependencies, but predefined graphs limit adaptability, and some datasets complicate learning. This study introduces a graph construction method based on a confusion matrix from a supervised learning process to dynamically capture inter-class relationships. Additionally, a hybrid loss function that combines energy distance and Huber loss is applied to address the vanishing gradient problem and improve learning stability. The approach is evaluated using air pollution data from the University of Utah AirU Pollution Monitoring Network in Salt Lake City, UT, with five GNN models: Graph Convolutional Networks (GCNs), Simple Graph Convolutional Networks (SGConv), Graph Isomorphism Networks (GINs), Graph Attention Networks (GATs), and GraphSage. The experimental results of single- and multistep predictions confirm that GraphSage achieves the highest accuracy in predicting the concentrations of PM${1}$, PM${10}$, and PM$_{2.5}$ over different time horizons. Furthermore, {\color{black} GNNExplainer (Graph Neural Network Explainer) and PGExplainer (Probabilistic Graph Explainer)} are applied to interpret feature importance and graph structure, ensuring model transparency. Results show improved prediction accuracy, with GNN models outperforming traditional machine learning \textcolor{black}{and deep learning models (i.e., Prophet, Long short-term memory, Gated recurrent units} in air pollution forecasting.

</details>


### [95] [CKM-Driven Communication-Aware UAV Intelligent Trajectory Optimization for Urban Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24979)
*Yang Xiaomeng,Jia Ziye,Zhu Qiuming,Wu Qihui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于信道知识图(CKM)驱动的多无人机轨迹规划框架，通过扩散模型构建信道质量分布图，结合图注意力网络和软演员-评论家算法实现通信感知的路径规划，有效提升了城市巡检任务中的通信可靠性和轨迹效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在城市巡检任务中应用日益广泛，但城市环境中严重的空间信道异质性导致可靠通信面临巨大挑战。传统的路径规划方法往往忽略了通信质量的空间分布特征，无法有效避开通信衰减区域，亟需开发通信感知的路径规划方法。

Method: 提出了CKM驱动的轨迹规划框架，包含两个核心模块：(1)应用扩散模型构建时间累积的信道知识图，利用稀疏观测数据重建高保真全局信道质量分布，实现低飞行开销下的精确感知；(2)提出全局到局部的图注意力网络软演员-评论家算法，其中图注意力网络优化复杂组合节点排序问题生成最优巡检目标序列，软演员-评论家算法执行连续动作控制确保飞行路径平滑并动态规避通信衰减区域。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提方法能够有效引导无人机经过高质量信道区域，无需依赖实时信道反馈，显著提升了轨迹效率和通信可靠性，成功实现了通信感知的路径规划目标。

Conclusion: 该研究成功将信道建模与轨迹决策相结合，为城市环境中多无人机巡检任务的通信保障问题提供了有效解决方案。扩散模型与深度强化学习的结合为信道感知的路径规划提供了新思路，具有较好的实际应用前景。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) are increasingly employed in urban inspection tasks, where reliable communication is critical but challenging due to the severe spatial channel heterogeneity. To address the issue, in this paper, we focus on the communication-aware path planning for multi-UAV tasks, and propose a channel knowledge map (CKM)-driven trajectory planning framework which integrates the channel modeling and trajectory decision-making. Specifically, we apply the diffusion model to construct a time-accumulated CKM and achieve the accurate perception with low flight overhead, which leverages the sparse observation data to reconstruct the high-fidelity global channel quality distribution. Based on the CKM, we propose a global-to-local graph attention network soft actor-critic algorithm. The graph attention network optimizes the complex combinatorial node ordering problem, generating an optimal and communication-aware sequence for the inspection targets. Subsequently, the soft actor-critic algorithm performs continuous action control to ensure the smoothness of the flight path and dynamically avoid communication attenuation areas. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed method effectively guides UAVs through high-quality channel regions without dependence on real-time channel feedback, significantly improving both the trajectory efficiency and communication reliability.

</details>


### [96] [Closed-Loop Graph Algorithm Execution with Small Language Models: Step Accuracy and Rollout Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24980)
*Michal Podstawski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过闭环预测框架评估小语言模型执行图算法的能力，发现模型在遍历和着色等结构化过程上表现可靠，但在加权算法中对误差累积敏感，强调了完整闭环评估的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型作为大规模系统的有效替代方案，其在多依赖决策的结构化算法执行能力方面仍缺乏深入理解。现有研究未能充分评估模型在连续决策任务中的表现。

Method: 将图算法执行建模为闭环预测问题，模型从当前图和算法状态中重复选择下一个动作。构建了涵盖多种经典图过程、多个合成图族的评估框架，使用不相交的训练、验证和测试分区。通过步骤准确率、精确展开准确率、约束有效性、部分解质量、前缀存活率和干预诊断等指标评估局部决策质量和全局执行行为。

Result: 适应训练可以为遍历和着色等结构化过程产生可靠的策略，但加权算法对误差累积仍然相当敏感。更重要的是，研究发现强大的下一步预测能力并不一定转化为可靠的自主执行能力。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，仅评估孤立决策是不够的，应该通过完整的闭环展开来评估算法语言模型，而非仅仅关注单步预测的准确性。这为未来算法语言模型的评估方法提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Small language models offer an efficient alternative to large-scale systems, but their ability to execute structured algorithms over multiple dependent decisions remains poorly understood. We study graph algorithm execution as a closed-loop prediction problem in which a model repeatedly selects the next action from the current graph and algorithmic state. Our evaluation framework covers several classical graph procedures, multiple synthetic graph families, and disjoint training, validation, and test partitions. It assesses both local decision quality and global execution behaviour using step accuracy, exact rollout accuracy, constraint validity, partial solution quality, prefix survival, and intervention-based diagnostics. The results show that adaptation can produce reliable policies for structural procedures such as traversal and coloring, while weighted algorithms remain substantially more sensitive to error accumulation. More broadly, the findings demonstrate that strong next-step prediction does not necessarily translate into reliable autonomous execution and motivate evaluating algorithmic language models through complete closed-loop rollouts rather than isolated decisions.

</details>


### [97] [A Single Stepsize Suffices for Unprojected Linear TD(0): Simultaneous Robust and Fast Rates via Polyak--Ruppert Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24981)
*Wei-Cheng Lee,Francesco Orabona*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在马尔可夫采样下研究了带Polyak-Ruppert平均的无投影线性TD(0)算法，提出了一个仅需混合时间信息而无需曲率参数知识的步长方案，并建立了同时实现鲁棒曲率无关速率和快速曲率依赖速率的高概率收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TD(0)算法的理论分析通常需要投影操作或依赖于曲率参数ω的稳定性论证，且步长选择需要先验知识。本文旨在为无投影的朴素TD(0)算法提供不依赖曲率参数先验知识的步长方案和高概率收敛保证。

Method: 提出一个依赖混合时间但无需曲率参数ω先验知识的步长方案η_t ∝ 1/(τ_mix·log(t)·√t)；引入针对几何混合马尔可夫链的泊松方程工具包，将马尔可夫噪声分解为鞅项和可控余项；开发了新的自界定归纳论证方法来证明路径稳定性。

Result: 证明了所提步长能保证TD(0)迭代值以高概率自动一致有界（无需投影和基于ω的稳定性论证）；建立了PR平均的同时高概率收敛保证，同一步长同时实现鲁棒的曲率无关速率Õ(τ_mix/√T)和快速的曲率依赖速率Õ(τ_mix²/(ωT))，边界取两者最小值。

Conclusion: 本文成功地为无投影线性TD(0)算法提供了首个同时实现曲率无关和曲率依赖收敛速率的理论保证，提出的步长方案实用性强（仅需混合时间信息），泊松方程工具包和自界定论证技术为马尔可夫噪声下的算法分析提供了新的技术手段。

Abstract: We study linear TD(0) under Markovian sampling, where data are generated along a single trajectory. We provide high-probability guarantees for a plain unprojected TD(0) algorithm with Polyak-Ruppert (PR) averaging, using a single stepsize schedule $η_t \propto \frac{1}{τ_{\mathrm{mix}}\log(t)\sqrt{t}}$ that depends on the mixing time but requires no prior knowledge of the curvature parameter $ω$. Our first result shows that such a choice of the stepsize guarantees that the TD(0) iterates are automatically and uniformly bounded with high probability, without projections and without any stability argument based on $ω$.
