*Sajib Debnath,Remon Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种混合CNN-LSTM入侵检测系统(IDS)，用于保护可再生能源智能电网免受复杂网络攻击。该方法结合CNN的空间特征提取和LSTM的时序建模能力，在两个基准数据集上分别达到96.1%和98.2%的精度，显著优于现有方法，并通过INT8量化实现了3.1倍加速，适合部署于资源受限的智能电子设备(IED)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着物联网传感器、高级量测体系(AMI)和SCADA系统加速可再生能源智能电网数字化，攻击面显著扩大，面临着隐蔽扭曲状态估计的FDI攻击和淹没通信信道的DoS/DDoS攻击等复杂网络威胁。然而，现有入侵检测系统(IDS)存在三大固有局限性：(1)对多步骤攻击时序演进建模不足；(2)在标准基准数据集极端不平衡的类别分布下可扩展性退化；(3)在异构网络环境中泛化能力受限。

Method: 本研究提出混合CNN-LSTM入侵检测系统，联合利用CNN进行空间特征提取和LSTM进行时序序列建模，能够实时检测瞬时容量异常和逐渐演进的"低速慢速"攻击活动。模型训练采用七步预处理工作流：缺失值插补、最小-最大归一化、独热编码、SMOTE类别平衡、互信息特征选择、因果时序序列构建(T=10)和分层划分。研究还进行了消融分析以识别关键设计选择，并采用INT8量化优化部署效率。

Result: 在基准数据集测试中，所提模型精度达到96.1%，优于LSTM(96.1%)、随机森林(93.5%)、SVM(91.2%)和KNN(89.7%)。在NSL-KDD数据集上，达到98.2%的精度，相比LSTM(96.4%)、CNN(95.2%)、随机森林(92.7%)和SVM(90.8%)具有2-9个百分点的优势。消融分析显示SMOTE平衡为最具影响力的设计选择（去除后F1下降3.7个百分点）。模型在GPU上实现27,800流/秒的实时推理吞吐量，CPU延迟为0.082毫秒/样本，INT8量化额外提供3.1倍加速且精度损失仅0.3%。

Conclusion: 本研究成功开发了一个可部署的深度学习框架，用于保护下一代可再生能源智能电网基础设施。混合CNN-LSTM架构有效解决了现有IDS在时序建模、类别不平衡和泛化能力方面的局限，同时通过INT8量化实现了在资源受限IED设备（内存<128MB）上的可行部署，为智能电网网络安全提供了有效的技术解决方案。

Abstract: The accelerated digitalization of renewable energy smart grids through IoT sensors, AMI, and SCADA systems has significantly expanded the attack surface for sophisticated cyberattacks, FDI attacks that stealthily distort state estimation and DoS/DDoS attacks that flood communication channels. Current IDS, however, exhibit three inherent limitations: inadequate modeling of the temporal progression of multi-step attacks, degraded scalability under extremely skewed class distributions of standard benchmark datasets, and restricted generalization across heterogeneous network environments. In this study, we present a Hybrid CNN-LSTM IDS that jointly exploits CNN-based spatial feature extraction and LSTM-based temporal sequence modeling, enabling the detection of instantaneous volumetric anomalies and gradually evolving low and slow-attack campaigns in real time. The model was trained using a seven-step preprocessing workflow comprising missing-value imputation, min-max normalization, one-hot encoding, SMOTE class balancing, mutual-information feature selection, causal temporal sequence construction (T=10), and stratified partitioning. LSTM (96.1%), Random Forest (93.5%), SVM (91.2%) and KNN (89.7%); in NSL-KDD, it reaches 98.2% precision versus 96.4% (LSTM), 95.2% (CNN), 92.7% (Random Forest) and 90.8% (SVM), with margins of 2-9 percentage points in all measures. An ablation analysis identified SMOTE balancing as the most influential design choice (-3.7~pp F1 without it). The model achieves a real-time inference throughput of 27,800 flows/s on GPU and 0.082 ms/sample CPU latency in FP32,, with INT8 quantization providing an additional 3.1 x speedup at 0.3% accuracy loss, confirming deployment feasibility on resource-constrained IEDs with <128MB memory and establishing a deployable deep-learning framework for securing next-generation renewable energy smart grid infrastructure.

</details>


### [132] [FDN: Interpretable Spatiotemporal Forecasting with Future Decomposition Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25201)
*Nicholas Majeske,Ariful Azad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了未来分解网络（FDN），这是一种新颖的预测模型，能够通过分类提供可解释的预测，揭示目标时间序列中的潜在活动模式，并以极低的内存和运行时间成本实现与最先进方法相媲美的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来，尽管已有高度复杂的方法在时空系统预测中实现了最先进的性能，但这些方法普遍缺乏可解释性。时空系统由空间分布但相互依赖的实体组成，每个实体产生独特的动态信号，因此需要一种既能保持预测精度又能提供解释能力的模型。

Method: 提出了未来分解网络（FDN），这是一种新颖的预测模型。该模型通过分类机制实现可解释的预测，能够揭示目标时间序列中的潜在活动模式，同时在设计上注重计算效率，显著降低了内存和运行时间成本。

Result: 在水文、交通和能源系统的多个数据集上进行了全面分析，结果表明FDN在预测准确性和可解释性方面均有提升，且以远低于最先进方法的内存和运行时间成本实现了具有竞争力的预测性能。

Conclusion: FDN成功地将高精度的预测能力与可解释性相结合，填补了时空系统预测领域在可解释性方面的空白，同时保持了与最先进方法相当的预测性能，且计算成本大幅降低，证明了该模型的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Spatiotemporal systems comprise a collection of spatially distributed yet interdependent entities each generating unique dynamic signals. Highly sophisticated methods have been proposed in recent years delivering state-of-the-art (SOTA) forecasts but few have focused on interpretability. To address this, we propose the Future Decomposition Network (FDN), a novel forecast model capable of (a) providing interpretable predictions through classification (b) revealing latent activity patterns in the target time-series and (c) delivering forecasts competitive with SOTA methods at a fraction of their memory and runtime cost. We conduct comprehensive analyses on FDN for multiple datasets from hydrologic, traffic, and energy systems, demonstrating its improved accuracy and interpretability.

</details>


### [133] [ASAP: Agent-System Co-Design for Wall-Clock-Centered Auto HPO Research for ML Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25207)
*Taicheng Guo,Haomin Zhuang,Kehan Guo,Yujun Zhou,Nitesh V. Chawla,Olaf Wiest,Xiangliang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ASAP框架，通过LLM整合多种优化器并优化系统架构，解决了现有LLM-HPO方法在多样性和端到端时间效率上的局限，显著提升了超参数优化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM用于超参数优化(HPO)的方法存在两个关键局限：(1)将LLM作为单一工具替代品，受限于其预训练目标的归纳偏置，无法处理问题的多样性；(2)按迭代次数评估忽略了LLM推理和工具执行的实际时间开销，导致实际端到端时间并未改善。

Method: 提出ASAP框架进行代理-系统协同设计：(1)代理层面：使用LLM整合多样化的归纳偏置优化器池，每轮选择最佳提案；(2)系统层面：通过前缀稳定提示词最大化KV缓存复用；推测并行化隐藏LLM和工具延迟；引入Self-Tuner自适应调整推测阈值。

Result: 在多样化现代HPO任务上的大量实验表明，ASAP持续优于所有基线方法，验证了工具整合和代理-系统协同设计的价值。

Conclusion: 证明了将LLM作为协调多种优化器的智能体，并结合系统级优化，能有效解决现有LLM-HPO方法的核心局限，实现更好的优化性能和实际时间效率。

Abstract: Hyperparameter Optimization (HPO) is essential for maximizing machine learning model performance, and its core challenge is sample efficiency: finding strong configurations within a limited budget. Because every HPO tool relies on a surrogate prior that imparts its own inductive bias, individual tools struggle once problems become sufficiently diverse and drift from these priors. Motivated by the reasoning and generalization capabilities of LLMs, recent work has explored using LLMs for HPO and reports improved per-iteration performance. Yet these methods share two limitations with a common origin: they use the LLM as a single-tool replacement evaluated by iteration count. (i) Deployed in place of prior tools, the LLM is itself constrained by its pretraining objective to one family of inductive-biased proposals; this single-source setup still fails to handle the full diversity of problems. (ii) Per-iteration evaluation ignores that, in real runs, LLM inference or tool execution is paid serially on top of model evaluation every round, so iteration-count gains do not translate into end-to-end wall-clock gains. We present ASAP, an agent-system co-design that addresses both limitations. On the agent side, ASAP uses the LLM to integrate a diverse pool of inductive-biased optimizers and to select among their proposals each round. On the system side, ASAP re-architects the loop to reduce end-to-end wall-clock while preserving regret quality: a prefix-stable prompt maximizes KV-cache reuse across rounds; speculation parallelism hides the remaining LLM and tool latency under model evaluation via a relative-error accept test; and a Self-Tuner adapts the speculation threshold from execution logs off the critical path. Extensive experiments on diverse modern HPO tasks show that ASAP consistently outperforms baselines, underscoring the value of tool integration and agent-system co-design.

</details>


### [134] [UC-Search: Risk-Aware Test-Time Search for Delayed Constrained Time-Series Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25274)
*Xibai Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了UC-Search，一种模型无关的测试时包装器，用于在不确定性和硬约束条件下进行时间序列的延迟决策，在多个基准测试中优于CEM、MPPI和风险感知随机方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列模型通常作为预测器进行评估，但实际部署的系统往往需要在不确定性和硬可行性约束下做出延迟决策，现有方法难以满足这些需求。

Method: UC-Search采用三阶段框架：骨干网络生成预测或动作分数，可行性自动机向前滚动候选路径，有界搜索返回风险调整可行轨迹的第一个动作。实现了UC-Beam和UCT风格的UC-MCTS诊断变体，利用认知不确定性、随机不确定性和传播不确定性作为路径风险项。

Result: 在预声明的9族33系列延迟控制套件上，UC-Pareto相对于CEM、MPPI和风险感知随机方法在标准化阈值下分别提升+3.1675/+2.3328/+2.5038，计算匹配审计中仍保持正向。在48系列M4库存审计中，相比经典基准库存控制提升+13556.7547。ETT/LTSF延迟库存验证支持相同的计算前沿声明。

Conclusion: 论文提出了短视崩溃/分离定理，说明了搜索何时退化为单步风险贪婪、延迟可行集耦合何时能创造非短视价值。边界和机制证据支持该方法的有效性，但未声称广泛优势。

Abstract: Time-series models are usually scored as forecasters, yet deployed systems often require delayed decisions under uncertainty and hard feasibility constraints. UC-Search is a model-agnostic test-time wrapper: a backbone emits forecasts or action scores, a feasibility automaton rolls candidate paths forward, and bounded search returns the first action of a risk-adjusted feasible trajectory. We instantiate UC-Beam and a UCT-style UC-MCTS diagnostic, using epistemic, aleatoric, and propagated uncertainty mainly as path-risk terms. A myopic-collapse/separation theorem states when search reduces to one-step risk-greedy and when delayed feasible-set coupling can create non-myopic value. Primary evidence comes from a predeclared public $9$-family, $33$-series delayed-control suite with six held-out starts per series: UC-Pareto is positive versus validation-selected CEM, MPPI, and risk-aware random at the normalized threshold ($+3.1675/+2.3328/+2.5038$), and remains positive in a compute-matched audit ($+2.8466/+2.7418/+2.7429$). ETT/LTSF delayed-inventory validation supports the same compute-frontier claim. A 48-series raw M4 standard periodic-review lost-sales inventory audit is positive versus the strongest classic base-stock control ($+13556.7547$), CEM ($+64900.2207$), and risk-random ($+52881.6042$), while MPPI remains family-mixed. FI-2010, official-forecast adapters, SB3/FQI controls, direction/capacity/intervention checks, and synthetic mechanism tests are reported as boundary or mechanism evidence rather than broad dominance claims.

</details>


### [135] [EPTS: Elastic Post-Training Sparsity for Efficient Large Language Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25285)
*Ke Xu,Jiaqi Wan,Wenhao Hu,Han Pu,Xiaoyun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出弹性后训练稀疏化框架(EPTS)，通过一次性优化生成单一弹性模型，支持多种稀疏度配置，在LLaMA和OPT模型上达到与SparseGPT、Wanda等SOTA方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练稀疏化(PTS)方法局限于单一稀疏度优化，针对不同稀疏度级别需要独立、耗时的优化过程，无法灵活适应多样化的硬件部署场景。

Method: 提出EPTS统一多稀疏度框架：1) 设计多稀疏度层级LoRA机制(MS-HiLoRA)，促进从低稀疏度到高稀疏度组的知识继承，缓解参数重建竞争；2) 引入多稀疏度特征混合器(MSFM)，通过动态融合不同稀疏粒度的特征表示增强模型对剪枝扰动的适应能力。

Result: 在LLaMA和OPT模型家族上的大量实验表明，EPTS相比SparseGPT、Wanda等最先进方法取得了具有竞争力的性能，同时通过单次优化实现多场景部署，显著提升了效率。

Conclusion: EPTS框架成功突破了传统PTS方法的单一稀疏度限制，通过一次性优化生成能够适应多种稀疏配置的弹性模型，为大语言模型在资源受限设备上的灵活部署提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Post-Training Sparsity (PTS) has emerged as a crucial paradigm for compressing Large Language Models to facilitate efficient deployment on resource-constrained devices. However, existing PTS methodologies are typically confined to Single-Sparsity optimization, necessitating a separate, time-consuming optimization session for each specific sparsity level. This rigid paradigm significantly hinders flexible deployment across diverse hardware scenarios, as adapting to a new sparsity requirement mandates a complete re-optimization process. To address these limitations, we propose Elastic Post-Training Sparsity (EPTS), a unified Multi-Sparsity framework that produces a single elastic model capable of maintaining robust performance across diverse sparsity configurations through a one-shot optimization process. Specifically, we design a Multi-Sparsity Hierarchy LoRA (MS-HiLoRA) mechanism that facilitates knowledge inheritance from low- to high-sparsity groups, effectively mitigating the competition for parameter reconstruction. Furthermore, we introduce a Multi-Sparsity Feature Mixer (MSFM), which significantly enhances the model's adaptability to pruning perturbations by dynamically fusing feature representations of varying sparsity granularities. Extensive experiments on LLaMA and OPT families demonstrate that EPTS achieves competitive performance compared to state-of-the-art methods like SparseGPT and Wanda, while offering significant efficiency gains by enabling multi-scenario deployment from a single optimization. our source code is available at https://github.com/xuke225/EPTS.

</details>


### [136] [The Gentle Collapse: Distributional Metrics for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25165)
*Ahmed Anwar,Andreas Wagner,Federico Raue,Tobias Nauen,Andreas Dengel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出六种基于softmax的连续遗忘度量指标，能够捕捉准确率无法反映的遗忘内部结构，并应用于损失加权和样本采样策略，显著降低灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灾难性遗忘评估指标（准确率下降）存在局限：仅能记录是否发生遗忘，在极端值处饱和，在任务边界离散跳变，无法揭示遗忘的内部结构特征。

Method: 提出六种softmax衍生指标（涵盖真实标签排名TLR、预测置信度和分布散度），在[0,1]区间归一化且无需修改训练过程。将指标用于：(1)作为损失权重进行样本加权；(2)计算小窗口内指标斜率作为稳定采样准则。

Result: 在CIFAR-100上，指标在准确率为0%时仍能区分不同类别（混淆边际IQR为[0.32,0.50]）；指标加权损失使遗忘降低1.3个百分点；小窗口（3个epoch）下log-TLR趋势采样达到41.07%（std=0.57），优于准确率趋势的34.79%（std=2.32）；在TinyImageNet上log-TLR趋势采样降低遗忘7.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 连续指标提供了比离散准确率更丰富的遗忘信号，能够有效指导遗忘缓解策略；小窗口趋势估计需要连续信号的结构性支持，这些指标在持续学习任务中具有实用价值。

Abstract: Accuracy degradation is the standard metric for Catastrophic Forgetting (CF), however, it records only whether forgetting occurred or not. It saturates at the extremes and collapses discretely at task boundaries, hiding the internal structure of what is being forgotten. We introduce six softmax-derived metrics spanning true-label rank (TLR), predictive confidence, and distributional divergence that characterize forgetting continuously, each normalized to [0, 1] with no modification to training. On CIFAR-100, these metrics carry information where accuracy does not: at 0% accuracy, the Confusion Margin spans an IQR of [0.32, 0.50] across classes that accuracy treats identically. We demonstrate that this richer signal is actionable in mitigating catastrophic forgetting. Per-sample metric scores used as loss weights reduce forgetting by 1.3 percentage points over uniform experience replay (ER) on CIFAR-100. Furthermore, the slope of a metric over a small window provides a stable sampling criterion: at a small-window size (e.g. 3 epochs), accuracy-trend degrades to 34.79% (std. = 2.32) while log-TLR achieves 41.07% (std. = 0.57). This gap is structural since reliable small-window trend estimation requires a continuous signal. On TinyImageNet, log-TLR trend sampling reduces forgetting by 7.7 percentage points over the ER baseline.

</details>


### [137] [Communicability-Inspired Positional Encoding (CIPE)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25293)
*Yipeng Zhang,Zhongtian Sun,Pietro Liò,Kelin Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CIPE（基于可通信性的位置编码），将图的多路径全局连通性转化为注意力兼容的几何结构，在七个基准测试上平均提升35.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图Transformer的位置编码难以在非欧几里得图上有效工作。理想的位置编码应诱导"注意力兼容几何"，不仅要描述图结构，还要定义一个几何空间，其内积反映有意义的结构相关性，使自注意力机制能够有效利用图的结构信息。

Method: 提出CIPE（Communicability-Inspired Positional Encoding）：基于节点间的可通信性（communicability）构建位置编码，该度量聚合所有长度路径的贡献。CIPE的内积恢复可通信性，将全局多路径连通性转化为注意力可用的相似性几何。引入维度对齐技术，将依赖图大小的CIPE表示映射到规定维度，同时忠实保持诱导的几何结构。

Result: CIPE在七个基准测试上使结构不可知Transformer平均提升35.5%，超越代表性位置编码方法；对结构偏置图Transformer也有持续改进，而竞争方法通常只产生边际效益。

Conclusion: CIPE作为注意力兼容图位置编码的原则性框架，有效解决了非欧几里得图上位置编码的设计挑战，为图Transformer提供了一种理论基础扎实且性能优越的位置编码方案。

Abstract: Positional encodings (PEs) are essential for Transformers. Yet designing effective PEs for non-Euclidean graphs remains challenging. Such encodings should ideally induce an Attention-Compatible Geometry for self-attention: not merely describing graph structure, but defining a geometry whose inner products reflect meaningful structural relatedness. To realize this geometry, we propose Communicability-Inspired Positional Encoding (CIPE), built from communicability, a measure between pairs of nodes that aggregates contributions from paths of all lengths. By construction, CIPE inner products recover communicability, converting global multi-path connectivity into an attention-ready similarity geometry. For practical Transformer training, we introduce dimensionality alignment, mapping graph-size-dependent CIPE representations to prescribed dimensions while faithfully preserving the induced geometry. Empirically, CIPE improves structure-agnostic Transformers by 35.5% on average across seven benchmarks, outperforming representative PEs; it also consistently improves structure-biased graph Transformers, where competing PEs often yield only marginal benefits. These results position CIPE as a principled framework for attention-compatible graph positional encodings.

</details>


### [138] [An iterative energy-based multimodal transformer for joint retrieval of wheat soil moisture, leaf area index, and plant height from Sentinel-1 and Sentinel-2 time series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25174)
*Shubham Kumar Singh,Peilei Fan,Suraj A. Yadav,Rajendra Prasad,Prashant K Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了迭代能量变换器(iEBT)方法，利用Sentinel-1 SAR和Sentinel-2多光谱数据联合反演土壤湿度、叶面积指数和株高，在印度Varanasi地区取得了平均R²=0.854的精度，并通过能量函数实现了反演质量的自动诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 田间尺度土壤湿度、叶面积指数和株高的联合反演对精准农业至关重要，但土壤湿度与冠层密度的同步变化在雷达后向散射和光谱响应中产生显著模糊性，传统前馈回归模型在异质小农种植系统中效果受限，这是一个典型的病态反演问题。

Method: 提出迭代能量变换器(iEBT)方法，将多模态预测因子嵌入共享序列中，生成初始状态估计，通过归一化梯度下降迭代更新目标向量[SM, LAI, PH]以最小化学习到的标量兼容性能量函数，采用能量引导的多模态融合策略，避免了传统的直接回归方法。

Result: 基于印度Varanasi地区700个质量控制后的田间实测数据，iEBT在随机测试集上取得四种子平均R²=0.854±0.012（SM: R²=0.841，LAI: R²=0.905，PH: R²=0.821）；模态消融实验表明Sentinel-1主导SM反演，Sentinel-2主导LAI反演，PH依赖结构-物候特征的组合；模型终端能量函数可作为未校准的反演后质量诊断工具，筛选10%最高能量样本显著降低了目标层均方根误差。

Conclusion: 虽然留一活动验证揭示了因局地管理变化导致的持续性跨季节域迁移挑战，但兼容性引导的多模态融合为可靠的生物物理参数估算提供了一条结构化的自诊断路径，该方法在精确农业应用中具有重要价值。

Abstract: Field-scale retrieval of surface soil moisture (SM), leaf area index (LAI), and plant height (PH) is essential for precision agriculture, yet it remains an ill-posed inverse problem. Concurrent variations in soil moisture and canopy density generate substantial ambiguities in radar backscatter and spectral responses, which reduces the effectiveness of traditional feedforward regression models in heterogeneous smallholder cropping systems. This study presents the Iterative Energy-Based Transformer (iEBT) for the joint retrieval of coupled soil-canopy states from Sentinel-1 C-band SAR and Sentinel-2 multispectral time series. Instead of direct regression, iEBT embeds multi-modal predictors within a shared sequence, produces an initial state estimate, and iteratively updates the target [SM, LAI, PH] vector through normalized gradient descent to minimize a learned scalar compatibility energy function. Using 700 quality-controlled field measurements from Varanasi, India, iEBT achieved the highest learned-model performance on the random test split, with a four-seed mean R^2 of 0.854 \pm 0.012 (R_SM^2 = 0.841, R_LAI^2 = 0.905, R_PH^2 = 0.821). WCM and PROSAIL were retained as physically interpretable SAR and optical reference models for comparison. Modality ablations confirmed that Sentinel-1 drives SM retrieval, while Sentinel-2 dominates LAI, whereas PH relies on combined structural-phenological signatures. Crucially, the model's terminal energy functions as an uncalibrated post-retrieval quality diagnostic; screening the 10% highest-energy samples markedly reduced target level root-mean-square errors. While leave-one-campaign-out validation highlights persistent cross-season domain shift challenges due to localized management variations, compatibility-guided multimodal fusion offers a structured self-diagnostic path toward reliable biophysical parameter estimation

</details>


### [139] [Compositional Behavioral Semantics for State Abstraction in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25357)
*Yivan Zhang,Ziyan Luo,Manuel Baltieri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一框架，用于定义和分析强化学习中的行为结构，为状态抽象下的行为保留提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 状态抽象在强化学习中至关重要，但目前缺乏一个通用原则来确定哪些行为结构在状态抽象下可被证明保留。现有的行为结构（值函数、不变量、互模拟关系、行为度量等）研究分散，需要一个统一的框架。

Method: 提出了一个基于局部、一步系统动力学描述的组合式框架，用于指定行为语义。该框架支持从逻辑行为语义构建定量度量，并提供可靠性的保证。

Result: 建立了行为结构在抽象系统和具体系统之间安全转移的理论结果，证明了如何从逻辑行为语义构建具有可靠性保证的定量度量。

Conclusion: 该框架为强化学习中状态抽象下的行为推理提供了原则性基础，并为广泛类别的行为结构提供了可重用的定义和证明原则。

Abstract: State abstraction plays a key role in scaling reinforcement learning to complex but structured systems. In studying such systems, a wide range of behavioral structures have been studied in reinforcement learning, including value functions, invariants, bisimulation relations, and behavioral metrics. However, a general principle for determining what structures are provably preserved under state abstraction is still lacking. In this paper, we present a unified framework for defining and analyzing behavioral structures in reinforcement learning. Our framework provides a compositional way to specify behavioral semantics based on local, one-step descriptions of system dynamics. Using this framework, we establish results showing how behavioral structures can be safely transferred between abstract and concrete systems. We further show how to construct quantitative metrics from logical behavioral semantics with soundness guarantees. Together, these results provide a principled foundation for reasoning about behaviors under state abstraction in reinforcement learning and offer reusable definition and proof principles for a broad class of behavioral structures in reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [140] [EveLoad: Cognitive Workload Recognition from Event-Based Eye Movements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25177)
*Guorui Lu,Shaohua Guan,Zhen Xu,Qinyu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次构建了基于事件相机的眼动认知负荷数据集EveLoad，并提出相应的学习框架，在六级认知负荷识别任务上实现了超过96%的准确率，为未来康复应用提供了新的感知方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知负荷监测对自适应康复和辅助界面至关重要，但现有基于帧的眼动追踪方法存在时间分辨率有限、快速眼动下鲁棒性差等问题。事件相机具有微秒级时间分辨率、高动态范围和低延迟优势，适合捕捉精细眼动动态。此外，以往研究多采用自由观看范式，模型可能学习注视位置分布而非眼动特征本身与认知负荷的关联。

Method: 引入EveLoad数据集——首个具有分级认知负荷标注的事件眼动数据集，采集自20名健康参与者，采用受控N-back引导注视范式以确保空间约束和任务驱动条件。建立六级认知负荷识别基准，并提出一种编码时空事件表示的学习框架。

Result: 所提方法在特定受试者评估中达到96.36%的平均准确率，在混合随机划分评估中达到96.13%的准确率，证明了事件相机眼动信号在认知负荷识别中的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于事件相机的眼动信号可作为未来负荷感知康复应用的有效感知途径，为扩展现实和机器人辅助康复环境中的认知状态监测提供了新的技术方案。

Abstract: Cognitive workload monitoring is important for adaptive rehabilitation and assistive interfaces, where task difficulty, pacing, and feedback should be adjusted according to the user's cognitive state to avoid overload and under-challenge. Emerging extended reality and robot-assisted rehabilitation environments provide controllable training tasks, but they require unobtrusive sensing methods that can capture rapid ocular dynamics during interaction. Existing eye-movement-based cognitive workload recognition methods mainly rely on frame-based eye trackers, which often suffer from limited temporal resolution and degraded robustness under rapid eye movements. In contrast, event cameras provide microsecond-level temporal resolution, high dynamic range and low latency, making them suitable for capturing fine-grained ocular dynamics. Many previous studies rely on free-viewing or similar paradigms, where gaze locations can vary across tasks. As a result, models may learn associations between gaze-location distributions and cognitive workload, rather than workload-related eye movement characteristics themselves. In this work, we introduce EveLoad, which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first event-based eye-movement dataset with graded cognitive workload annotations, collected from 20 healthy participants under spatially constrained and task-driven conditions using a controlled N-back-guided fixation paradigm. Based on this dataset, we establish a benchmark for cognitive workload recognition with six workload levels and propose a learning framework that encodes spatiotemporal event representations. Experimental results show that our approach achieves an average subject-specific accuracy of 96.36% and 96.13% under mixed random split evaluation. These results suggest that event-based eye movements may provide a useful sensing pathway for future workload-aware rehabilitation.

</details>


### [141] [FactorLibrary: From Polynomials to Circuits via Recursive Subgoals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25394)
*Rohan Pandey,Michael Ruofan Zeng,Weikun K. Zhang,Kaijie Jin,Naomi Morato,Archit Ganapule,Bhaumik Mehta,Jarod Alper*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将有限域上多项式最小算术电路发现问题建模为强化学习问题，通过引入FactorLibrary机制和结合蒙特卡洛树搜索的深度强化学习算法，成功找到了复杂度高达8的认证最优电路，成功率达91.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在代数复杂度理论中，寻找有限域上多项式的最小算术电路是一个组合难度极高的核心问题。该问题具有重要的理论意义和实际应用价值，但传统的组合搜索方法面临着搜索空间快速增长的根本性挑战。

Method: 提出了两种强化学习建模方向：自底向上和自顶向下。核心创新是引入FactorLibrary机制，用于存储可分解的子表达式，作为跨训练 episode 的可重用子目标，有效缓解组合搜索空间爆炸问题。算法方面，训练了使用 Gumbel-PPO-MCTS 的自底向上代理，以及使用 PPO+MCTS 和 SAC 的两种自顶向下代理。

Result: PPO+MCTS 自顶向下代理表现最稳定，能够找到复杂度高达8的认证最优电路，成功率达到91.8%。实验表明该方法在解决该组合优化问题上具有显著效果。

Conclusion: 本研究成功将强化学习应用于代数复杂度理论中的核心难题，通过创新的 FactorLibrary 机制和算法组合，有效应对了组合搜索空间爆炸的挑战，为该领域提供了一种新的有效解决方案。

Abstract: Finding minimal arithmetic circuits for polynomials over finite fields is a combinatorially hard problem central to algebraic complexity theory. We formulate it as a reinforcement learning problem in two directions, bottom-up and top-down. To address the challenge of a fast-growing combinatorial search space, we introduce FactorLibrary, which stores factorizable subexpressions that serve as reusable subgoals across training episodes. We trained a bottom-up agent with Gumbel-PPO-MCTS and two top-down agents with PPO+MCTS and SAC. The PPO+MCTS top-down agent exhibited the most stable performance, finding certified optimal circuits up to complexity $8$ with a success rate of $91.8\%$.

</details>


### [142] [Neural operator-based digital twins for modeling amyloid-$β$ and tau propagation and treatment optimization in Alzheimer's disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25185)
*Xiaofeng Xu,Tingting Dan,Zifan Zhou,Bin Li,Guorong Wu,Wenrui Hao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个数据驱动的数字孪生框架，能够从稀疏的临床PET成像数据中学习阿尔茨海默病中淀粉样蛋白-β和tau蛋白的时空演化动力学，实现了87%和81%的预测准确率，并进一步设计了个性化的治疗优化策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确预测个体水平的淀粉样蛋白-β和tau蛋白的时空演化对于改善阿尔茨海默病的诊断和治疗至关重要。然而，构建患者特异性数字孪生面临重大挑战：潜在的非线性聚集机制未知，且需要从稀疏、噪声大且异质的纵向PET成像数据中推断这些机制。

Method: 开发了一个数据驱动框架，将算子学习（operator learning）与降阶表示相结合，直接从临床观测数据中推断疾病进展的控制方程。该方法能够在反应-扩散动力学的基础上，从数据中学习生物标志物的传播动力学。进一步基于学习到的动力学，构建了PDE约束的最优控制问题来设计个性化治疗策略。

Result: 在淀粉样蛋白-β的预测准确率达到87%，tau蛋白的预测准确率达到81%。成功实现了从数据中学习疾病进展的控制方程，并能够基于学习到的动力学设计调节病理蛋白传播的个性化治疗策略。

Conclusion: 通过整合数据驱动的动力学建模与治疗优化，所提出的数字孪生框架为理解神经退行性疾病的进展和实现精准干预提供了一个可解释且具有预测能力的平台，为阿尔茨海默病的个性化诊疗提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurately predicting the spatiotemporal evolution of amyloid-$β$ and tau proteins at the individual level is critical for improving the diagnosis and treatment of Alzheimer's disease. We consider the problem of constructing patient-specific digital twins that model the propagation of these biomarkers on the cortical surface using reaction--diffusion dynamics. A major challenge is that the underlying nonlinear aggregation mechanisms are unknown and must be inferred from sparse, noisy, and heterogeneous longitudinal PET imaging data. To address this, we develop a data-driven framework that learns biomarker dynamics directly from clinical observations. The approach combines operator learning with reduced-order representations to infer governing equations of disease progression from data. Using this framework, we achieve predictive accuracies of 87\% for amyloid-$β$ and 81\% for tau. Building on the learned dynamics, we further formulate a PDE-constrained optimal control problem to design personalized therapeutic strategies that regulate pathological protein propagation. By integrating data-driven dynamical modeling with treatment optimization, the proposed digital twin framework provides an interpretable and predictive platform for understanding disease progression and enabling precision interventions in neurodegenerative disorders.

</details>


### [143] [Efficient Analytic Uncertainty Quantification for Multi-Modal Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25188)
*Kun Jin,James Harrison,Jiawei Li,Sihan Liu,Jiayi Liu,Randolph Linderman,Yuening Li,Arnab Bhadury,Sourabh Prakash Bansod,Liang Liu,Jasper Snoek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将变分贝叶斯推断（VBI）扩展到两种半参数回归模型（分位数回归QR和分类恢复CR），提出了一个统一的、分布无关的不确定性量化框架，能够高效估计多模态条件分布并提供可靠的不确定性量化，在多模态回归任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有回归任务的不确定性量化方法主要基于单峰参数模型假设（如高斯分布），这在多模态分布场景下失效；而能够处理多模态分布的半参数方法又缺乏对预测方差的高效量化。因此需要开发一种既能准确估计复杂条件分布，又能高效量化不确定性的方法。

Method: 基于变分贝叶斯推断（VBI）框架，重新构建了分位数回归（QR）和分类恢复（CR）两种半参数回归模型的公式，推导出解析形式的证据下界（ELBO）以简化训练过程，并获得闭式或解析近似的预测密度以实现高效推断。

Result: 在三个具有多模态标签分布的大规模回归基准测试中，该方法超越了最先进的多模态回归基线模型，在预测性能上甚至可以匹配计算成本高昂的集成模型。此外，通过认知不确定性估计，该方法能够实现高度数据高效的主动学习策略。

Conclusion: 本文提出的统一框架成功解决了多模态回归任务中的不确定性量化难题，实现了复杂条件分布的准确估计和高效不确定性量化的双重目标，为大规模可信学习提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Efficient uncertainty quantification (UQ) is essential for trustworthy large-scale learning. Existing UQ methods for regression tasks mainly operate under the assumption that the conditional label marginal satisfies single-peak parametric models, e.g., Gaussians, where the negative log-likelihood function simplifies to the mean square error. However, such single-peak assumptions fail in regression tasks featuring multi-modal distributions. On the other hand, semi-parametric methods which achieve strong regression performance for multi-modal distributions often lack efficient quantification on their prediction variances. In this work, we extend UQ techniques based on Variational Bayesian Inference (VBI) to two widely used semi-parametric regression models that yield histogram-like reconstructions of the conditional label densities: Quantile Regression (QR) and Classification Restoration (CR). Our approach introduces a unified, distribution-agnostic framework that simultaneously achieves accurate estimation of complex conditional distributions and highly efficient UQ. Theoretically, our method is grounded in novel formulations of QR and CR within the VBI framework, yielding analytic Evidence Lower Bounds (ELBO) to streamline training and a closed-form or analytically approximated predictive density for efficient inference. Empirically, we evaluate our methods on three large-scale regression benchmarks with multi-modal label distributions. Our framework outperforms state-of-the-art multi-modal regression baselines, and even matches predictive performance of computationally expensive ensemble models. Furthermore, by leveraging epistemic uncertainty estimation, our approach enables highly data-efficient active learning strategies.

</details>


### [144] [Brevity is the Soul of Inference Efficiency: Inducing Concision in VLMs via Data Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25432)
*DatologyAI,:,Matthew L. Leavitt,Siddharth Joshi,Haoli Yin,Rishabh Adiga,Haakon Mongstad,Alvin Deng,David Schwab,Bogdan Gaza,Ari Morcos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过数据筛选训练简洁模型来提升推理效率，在视觉语言模型上实现35倍成本降低且不损失准确性，证明简洁性是被忽视的关键效率杠杆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理效率优化方法（蒸馏、剪枝、量化、稀疏路由）均聚焦于降低单token成本，而将输出长度视为固定因素。随着模型输出长度不断膨胀，标准工具包却未触及这一效率组件，因此本文主张简洁性是缺失的推理效率杠杆。

Method: 提出通过预训练数据筛选来实现简洁性：在简洁且正确的数据上训练的模型能学会用更少token回答问题。将VLM筛选流程应用于MAmmoTH-VL单图像子集，在1B-4B参数规模的14个VLM上进行对照实验，通过每模型回归分离简洁性与质量，并以每个正确答案的FLOPs定价模型。

Result: 数据筛选相比最冗长的4B对比模型(Qwen3.5-4B)带来35倍成本优势(每个正确答案0.41 vs 14.58 TFLOPs)，准确率仅差约1个百分点(0.691 vs 0.704)。相比未筛选基线，在匹配长度下准确率提升+17.55个百分点，且随模型规模增长(1B时+16.7pp到4B时+21.2pp)。简洁模型甚至能找到冗长推理模型遗漏的正确答案。

Conclusion: 推理效率本质上是一个每正确答案的token数量问题，简洁性是直接针对该目标的杠杆。通用冗余在任何能力或规模下都不带来准确性提升，推理结构化冗余的有效窗口随规模扩大而缩小。简洁性不以牺牲质量为代价，推理应作为独立的筛选目标而非简洁性需要放弃的内容。

Abstract: Inference efficiency is typically pursued by shrinking the model: distillation, pruning, quantization, and sparse routing each lower per-token cost while treating token count as fixed. But output length has been inflating, and it is precisely the component the standard toolkit leaves untouched. Here, we argue that brevity is the missing inference-efficiency lever, and that pretraining data curation is a practical way to pull it: a model trained on concise, correct data learns to answer in fewer tokens; i.e. it has a lower Cost-of-Pass. We apply our VLM curation pipeline to the MAmmoTH-VL single-image subset, and compare models trained on our curated data, the standard MAmmoTH-VL data, and external open-weight frontier VLMs. On a controlled 20-evaluation set and 14 VLMs at 1B-4B activated parameters, we hold output length fixed with a per-model regression, separating brevity from quality, and price models in FLOPs per correct answer. Curation buys a 35x Cost-of-Pass advantage over the most verbose 4B comparator (Qwen3.5-4B) within $\sim$1 pp of accuracy (0.41 vs 14.58 TFLOPs per correct answer; 0.691 vs 0.704 mean accuracy). Curation also buys a +17.55-percentage-point matched-length accuracy gain over the uncurated baseline that grows with model scale (from +16.7 pp at 1B to +21.2 pp at 4B). This brevity improvement concedes no quality: generic verbosity buys no accuracy at any capability or scale, and the window where reasoning-structured verbosity still earns its tokens shrinks from 4 of 8 capability groups at 2B to 1 of 8 at 4B. Per example, the concise model even reaches correct answers the verbose reasoning model misses, marking reasoning as a distinct curation target rather than something brevity gives up. Inference efficiency in this regime is a tokens-per-correct problem, and brevity is the lever that targets it directly.

</details>


### [145] [Efficient Adaptive Data Acquisition via Pretrained Belief Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25197)
*Daolang Huang,Zhuoyue Huang,Conor Hassan,Luigi Acerbi,Samuel Kaski,Tom Rainforth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: POLAR提出了一种基于信念表示的策略学习框架，通过解耦表示学习与策略学习，利用预训练基础模型作为信念状态编码器，在贝叶斯实验设计、贝叶斯优化和主动学习中显著提升了数据采集的效率和效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自适应数据采集策略学习方法存在两大局限：基于后验的方法依赖替代模型和后验近似，可能导致模型误设或偏差；直接策略学习方法仅从历史观测映射，未能充分利用可用的模型表示，增加了学习难度。

Method: POLAR的核心思想是最优数据采集仅通过充分的信念状态依赖于观测历史。具体方法包括：1) 将表示学习与策略学习解耦；2) 利用预训练的预测性基础模型作为信念状态编码器；3) 在这些表示之上训练策略头；4) 形成统一的分摊策略学习框架，通过任务特定的效用函数来训练策略，适用于贝叶斯实验设计、贝叶斯优化和主动学习三种任务。

Result: 实验结果表明，POLAR在多种任务上超越了最先进的分摊方法，同时需要更少的训练样本，证明了其在分摊数据采集的可扩展性和效率方面取得了显著进步。

Conclusion: POLAR成功地将信念表示与策略学习相结合，通过利用预训练基础模型的强大表示能力，为自适应数据采集提供了一个简单、统一且高效的框架，在减少样本需求的同时提升了性能表现。

Abstract: Learning effective policies for adaptive data acquisition remains challenging: posterior-based methods rely on surrogate models and posterior approximations that can be misspecified or biased, while direct policy-learning methods map from historical observations and fail to exploit available model representations, making learning harder. We introduce policy learning with belief representations (POLAR), based on the insight that optimal data acquisition depends on the observation history only through a sufficient belief state. Specifically, POLAR decouples representation learning from policy learning by leveraging pretrained predictive foundation models as belief-state encoders, training a policy head on top of their representations. This yields a simple, unified amortised policy learning framework for Bayesian experimental design, Bayesian optimisation, and active learning, differing only in the task-specific utility used to train the policy. Empirically, we find that POLAR outperforms state-of-the-art amortised methods across diverse tasks while requiring far fewer training samples, demonstrating a significant step in the scalability and efficiency of amortised data acquisition.

</details>


### [146] [Interpretable Concept-Guided Polynomial Tabular Kolmogorov-Arnold Network for EEG-Based Mild Cognitive Impairment Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25434)
*Yosef Bernardus Wirian,Qiang Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出CPTabKAN框架，通过概念引导的多项式变换和Kolmogorov-Arnold网络，在睡眠EEG数据上实现轻度认知障碍检测，加权F1分数达0.9038，显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻度认知障碍（MCI）的早期可扩展检测仍是未解决的临床挑战。现有基于EEG的筛查方法受限于手工特征管道（丢弃神经生理学有意义的领域结构）和深度学习分类器（为性能牺牲可解释性），尚无工作在睡眠EEG的MCI检测框架中统一生理学组织的概念编码器、跨概念交互建模和非线性表格分类。

Method: 提出概念引导的多项式变换表格学习方法CPTKAN，将异构EEG特征映射到领域知识指导的概念表示，通过二阶多项式变换揭示一阶和二阶交互作用，并应用傅里叶参数化的TabKAN分类器学习非线性决策边界。在372名受试者的骨质疏松性骨折研究队列上进行评估，使用1,379个特征组织为十个生理学概念组。

Result: 在10折交叉验证下，CPTabKAN二阶模型获得加权F1分数0.9038（标准差0.034），比梯度提升方法高出5.65个百分点（t(9)=1.934, p=0.043），在SMOTE平衡下优势持续存在。消融分析确认各组件独立贡献。概念重要性分析显示：功率谱密度、多尺度熵和Hjorth参数主导一阶权重，而涉及LZW复杂度、统计量、人口统计学和慢振荡的跨概念交互超过所有一阶分数。

Conclusion: 研究证明概念结构化、交互感知的表格学习能够呈现生理学连贯的推理过程，支持临床信任。该方法在MCI检测任务中实现了性能和可解释性的良好平衡。

Abstract: Early and scalable detection of mild cognitive impairment (MCI) remains an unresolved clinical challenge. Existing EEG-based screening approaches are constrained by handcrafted feature pipelines that discard neurophysiologically meaningful domain structure and deep learning classifiers that sacrifice interpretability for performance. No existing work unifies physiologically organized concept encoders, cross-concept interaction modeling, and nonlinear tabular classification in a sleep EEG-based MCI detection framework. This study proposes Concept-guided Polynomial-transformed Tabular learning using Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (CPTabKAN), which maps heterogeneous EEG-derived features into domain-informed concept representations, expands them via degree-2 polynomial transformation to expose first- and second-order interactions, and applies a Fourier-parameterized TabKAN classifier to learn nonlinear decision boundaries. CPTabKAN was evaluated on the Study of Osteoporotic Fractures cohort (372 subjects, overnight polysomnography), using 1,379 features organized into ten physiologically motivated concept groups. Under 10-fold cross-validation, CPTabKAN-Second Order achieved a weighted F1-score of 0.9038 (SD 0.034), outperforming GradientBoosting by 5.65 percentage points (t(9)=1.934,p=0.043, one-sided paired test), with advantages persisting under SMOTE-based balancing. Ablation analysis confirmed independent contributions from each component. Concept importance analysis revealed that power spectral density, multi-scale entropy, and Hjorth parameters dominated first-order weights, while cross-concept interactions involving Lempel-Ziv-Welch complexity, statistics, demographics, and slow oscillations exceeded all first-order scores. These results demonstrate that concept-structured, interaction-aware tabular learning surfaces physiologically coherent reasoning, supporting clinical trust.

</details>


### [147] [TopoCast: A Topological Fidelity Framework for Evaluating Transformer-Based Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25439)
*Sandeepa Weerasekara,Sandareka Wickramanayake*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TopoCast框架，利用拓扑学方法评估时间序列预测的结构保真度，弥补传统MSE等点误差指标忽视信号结构特性的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习时间序列预测评估主要依赖MSE等点误差指标，仅关注数值精度而忽略预测信号的结构特性（如周期动力学、振荡行为、相位对齐），导致过度平滑、相位偏移或频率失真的预测可能获得较低误差分数，掩盖了实质性的结构退化问题。

Method: 提出TopoCast框架：1）使用Takens延迟嵌入重构预测序列与真实序列的相空间表示；2）应用持久同调刻画内在动力学特征；3）从持久图推导四个互补的拓扑保真度量并聚合为拓扑保真分数(TFS)；4）引入主导周期重叠指标，将持久拓扑特征映射到时间域；5）结合TFS与主导周期重叠得到局部拓扑保真分数(LTFS)，实现相位感知评估。

Result: 在三个真实世界基准数据集上对五种Transformer架构进行实验，发现具有相似预测误差的模型表现出显著不同的结构保真度特征，揭示了传统评估方法忽视的失效模式。

Conclusion: 拓扑感知的预测评估方法能够有效识别点误差指标无法捕捉的结构退化问题，为时间序列预测模型评估提供了新的视角和工具，具有重要的理论和实践价值。

Abstract: Deep learning-based models have achieved state-of-the-art performance in Time Series Forecasting (TSF), yet their evaluation remains dominated by pointwise error metrics such as Mean Squared Error (MSE), which quantify numerical accuracy but overlook structural properties of the forecast signal, including recurrent dynamics, oscillatory behavior, and phase alignment. As a result, forecasts exhibiting over-smoothing, phase shifts, or frequency distortions may achieve favorable error scores despite substantial structural degradation. To address this limitation, we propose TopoCast, a topology-driven framework for evaluating structural fidelity in TSF. TopoCast reconstructs phase-space representations of forecast and ground-truth sequences using Takens delay embedding and applies persistent homology to characterize their intrinsic dynamics. We derive four complementary topological fidelity measures from persistence diagrams and aggregate them into a Topological Fidelity Score (TFS). We further introduce dominant cycle overlap, a novel metric that maps persistent topological features to the temporal domain to assess whether dominant oscillatory patterns occur at the correct time points. Combined with TFS, this yields the Localized Topological Fidelity Score (LTFS), a phase-aware measure that captures temporal localization errors invisible to existing evaluation metrics. Experiments on five Transformer architectures across three real-world benchmark datasets demonstrate that models with similar forecasting errors can exhibit markedly different structural fidelity profiles, revealing failure modes overlooked by conventional evaluation and highlighting the value of topology-aware forecast assessment.

</details>


### [148] [Learning with a Single Rollout via Monte Carlo Pass@k Critic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25451)
*Fengdi Che,Yang Liu,Lei Yu,Meng Cao,Tong Che,Rupam Mahmood,Dale Schuurmans*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了单次展开近端策略优化(SR-PPO)方法，通过训练校准的token级信用评论器来预测Pass@k成功概率，避免了重复采样的计算成本，同时提供了比Pass@1更具选择性的学习信号，在数学推理基准上取得了稳定的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型强化学习中的token级优势估计面临挑战：扩大情节经验收集规模成本高昂；基线优势估计方法中，重复采样导致轨迹发散到不同的推理前缀；GRPO等算法中结果奖励过于稀疏，难以归因到特定动作，且采样轨迹异构导致比较困难。

Method: 提出单次展开近端策略优化(SR-PPO)方法：不通过候选组内情节回报归一化来估计优势，而是使用每个提示单次展开的蒙特卡洛结果训练校准的token级信用评论器；该评论器预测提示前缀处的Pass@k成功概率（源自Pass@1尝试）；证明了当k增加时，Pass@k收敛到可达性指标，在显式状态图中可在O(|V|+|E|)时间内计算极限值(k→∞)。

Result: SR-PPO展现出稳定的学习动态；在HMMT26和AIME24等数学推理基准上，Pass@128成功率获得一致提升；提供了比Pass@1更具选择性的学习信号，能够折扣容易解决的前缀，优先处理成功率仍处于边缘的困难前缀。

Conclusion: SR-PPO成功缓解了重复采样的计算成本和信用分配困难；通过Pass@k可达性指标实现了无需采样对比轨迹的直接信用分配；在数学推理任务上验证了方法的有效性，为语言模型强化学习中的token级信用分配提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Estimating token-level advantages in reinforcement learning (RL) for language models remains challenging because scaling up episodic experience collection is expensive. The difficulty intensifies for baseline advantage estimation methods, where repeated sampling causes trajectories to diverge into substantially different reasoning prefixes. In this context, RL algorithms such as GRPO prove limited: an outcome reward is too sparse to be attributed to specific actions like intermediate steps, and comparisons across sampled traces are non-trivial because they are heterogeneous. To mitigate both the computational cost of repeated sampling and the difficulty of credit assignment, we study single-rollout proximal policy optimization (SR-PPO) featuring token-level credit assignment in RL for language models. Instead of estimating advantages by normalizing episodic returns within the candidate group, we train a calibrated token-level credit critic using Monte Carlo outcomes from one rollout per prompt. Specifically, we use the critic to predict the Pass@k success probability at the prompt prefix, which is derived from a Pass@1 attempt. This choice yields a more selective learning signal than Pass@1: it discounts easily solved prefixes while prioritizing hard ones whose success probability remains marginal. We show that as $k$ increases, Pass@k converges to a reachability indicator, reflecting whether a prefix can lead to at least one successful continuation. In an explicit state graph, the limit ($k \rightarrow \infty$) can be computed in $O(|V|+|E|)$ time, offering a promising surrogate for direct credit assignment without the need to sample contrastive traces. As an initial validation, SR-PPO exhibits stable learning dynamics, along with consistent gains in Pass@128 success rates on mathematical reasoning benchmarks such as HMMT26 and AIME24.

</details>


### [149] [Semantic Allocation in Ordered Bottlenecks: Predictive Residual Inference for Visual Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25232)
*Erik Ayari,Manuel Traub,Martin V. Butz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了PRIOR框架，通过分层预测器替代传统的掩码排序压力，解决了有序表示学习中后期token效用弱和离散优化敏感的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的有序表示学习方法（如尾丢弃）采用基于掩码的排序压力（MBOP）来强制排序，但存在固有缺陷：（1）缺乏显式细化目标导致后期token效用较弱；（2）仅依赖梯度暴露作为重要性的代理；（3）在离散或量化token表示等优化敏感场景下表示变得脆弱。

Method: 提出有序表示预测残差推理（PRIOR）框架：用log2缩放的层级替代激活率控制，引入层级预测器。预测器将已解释信息与未解释信息分离，使每个层级专注于残差误差。这种方法替代了传统的掩码排序压力机制。

Result: 在对比学习和图像重建任务中与MBOP-TD和MBOP-ITD基线对比：PRIOR能够学习到良好排序的表示（低预算提供粗粒度描述，高预算添加细化）；除一个设置外，满预算性能均更高；在离散和量化设置中，MBOP基线性能严重受限，而PRIOR接近连续对应方法的性能。

Conclusion: PRIOR是有序表示学习的有效框架，成功解决了MBOP方法的固有缺陷，在保持满预算性能的同时实现了更好的有序表示学习，特别是在离散和量化设置中展现出显著优势。

Abstract: Ordered bottlenecks aim to provide utility at flexible budgets by assigning coarse information to early tokens and task-relevant detail to later ones. Prior work, including tail dropping (TD), typically enforces ordering by means of a masking-based ordering pressure (MBOP): Late tokens are masked more frequently than early tokens and are therefore encouraged to store less essential fine details. We introduce predictive residual inference for ordered representations (PRIOR), a framework designed to address inherent weaknesses of MBOP. MBOP is prone to weak late-token utility because it lacks an explicit refinement objective and uses gradient exposure as a proxy for importance. Furthermore, representations may become particularly brittle in optimization-sensitive settings, such as when using discrete or quantized token representations. PRIOR replaces activation-rate control with log2-scaled levels and level-wise predictors. These predictors separate already explained from unexplained information, focusing each level on residual error. We compare PRIOR against MBOP-TD and independent tail-biased dropout (MBOP-ITD) in contrastive learning and image reconstruction tasks. Unlike the baselines, PRIOR learns well-ordered representations across experiments: low budgets provide coarse descriptors, while high budgets add refinements. Simultaneously, full-budget performance with PRIOR is higher in all but one experimental setting, where performance remains comparable. MBOP baselines are severely limited in discrete and quantized settings, while PRIOR approaches the performance of continuous counterparts. Taken together, these findings establish PRIOR as an effective framework for ordered representation learning.

</details>


### [150] [Low-Complexity Policy Tessellations in Structured Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25593)
*Fredy Pokou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究结构化马尔可夫决策过程中的最优策略几何特性，提出基于边界的策略近似方法，通过直接学习策略区域来近似最优策略，在库存控制和队列准入实验中表现出优于强化学习基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近似动态规划和强化学习通常需要近似高维的价值函数，计算复杂度高。作者观察到最优策略实际上诱导出更简单的决策细分，因此希望利用这种几何特性来简化策略学习过程。

Method: 提出基于边界的策略近似方法，直接学习策略区域的边界，而不是近似高维价值函数。通过策略损失分解，将性能下降与动作边界联系起来，解释了误差为何集中在无差异边界附近。

Result: 在库存控制和队列准入实验中，相比强化学习基线方法，所提方法实现了更低的策略误差、更小的价值差距、更快的误差衰减速度，以及更好的稳定性。

Conclusion: 最优策略具有简单的几何结构，可以利用决策细分的特性来设计更高效的策略学习方法。基于边界的策略近似方法通过直接学习策略区域，避免了高维价值函数近似的困难，是一种有效的替代方案。

Abstract: We study optimal-policy geometry in structured Markov decision processes. While approximate dynamic programming and reinforcement learning typically approximate high-dimensional value functions, we show that optimal policies induce simpler decision tessellations. We propose boundary-based policy approximations that learn policy regions directly. A policy-loss decomposition links performance degradation to action margins and explains why errors concentrate near indifference boundaries. Inventory control and queue admission experiments show lower policy error, smaller value gaps, faster error decay, and stability than reinforcement learning baselines.

</details>


### [151] [Variational Inference via Entropic Transport Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25265)
*Vincent Pacelli,Akash Ratheesh,Evangelos Theodorou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了基于熵正则化最优传输的粒子变分推断方法ETD，通过全局传输结构解决了现有核方法（如SVGD）在高维和多模态目标上的方差坍缩和模态坍缩问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于核排斥的粒子变分推断方法（如SVGD）在高维情况下存在方差坍缩，在多模态目标上存在模态坍缩，这些病态行为源于缺乏全局传输结构。

Method: 将每次粒子更新构建为熵正则化最优传输问题，通过将JKO近端格式提升到耦合空间并使用KL链式法则松弛，每次迭代归结为Sinkhorn计算；无需计算分数函数，仅需未归一化目标密度的逐点评估。

Result: 在方差坍缩诊断、贝叶斯逻辑回归、神经网络和分子玻尔兹曼分布实验中，ETD性能匹配或优于SVGD、AGF-SVGD和SGLD，在高维和多模态设置中优势最为显著。

Conclusion: ETD通过最优传输提供的全局协调机制，能够自然保持多模态结构，为粒子变分推断提供了一种有效的无分数方法。

Abstract: Particle-based variational inference (ParVI) methods approximate an intractable target distribution by evolving an ensemble of interacting samples. Existing approaches rely predominantly on kernel-based repulsion (e.g., SVGD), which suffers from variance collapse in high dimensions and mode collapse on multimodal targets -- pathologies caused by the absence of global transport structure. We introduce entropic transport descent (ETD), a ParVI family that frames each particle update as an entropy-regularized optimal transport problem. Derived from the JKO proximal scheme by lifting to the space of couplings and relaxing via the KL chain rule, each ETD iteration reduces to a Sinkhorn computation. The resulting transport plan provides global coordination, guiding each particle to nearby high-density proposals and naturally preserving multimodal structure. ETD can operate entirely score-free, requiring only pointwise evaluations of the unnormalized target density. Experiments on variance-collapse diagnostics, Bayesian logistic regression, neural networks, and molecular Boltzmann distributions show that ETD matches or outperforms SVGD, AGF-SVGD, and SGLD, with the largest gains in high-dimensional and multimodal settings.

</details>


### [152] [Inverse Reinforcement Learning for Interpretable Keystroke Biomarkers in Parkinson's Disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25270)
*Navin Bondade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首次将逆强化学习(IRL)应用于击键动力学分析PD，恢复的可解释奖励函数中速度偏好权重与UPDRS-III严重程度显著相关(r=-0.607)，并修复了两个实现漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有击键动力学PD检测方法多采用统计特征+分类器范式，存在准确率差异大、可解释性差的问题。本文旨在通过IRL建模击键行为的决策过程，获得可解释的奖励函数来揭示PD患者的运动控制特征。

Method: 将每次击键建模为对打字速度的离散选择，应用IRL恢复每个受试者的奖励函数。初始四参数模型(速度、努力、平滑性、手交替成本)存在严重共线性(r=1.000)，诊断后修正为可识别的三参数模型(速度、一致性、手交替)。在MIT-CSXPD数据集(85人，42名PD患者)上验证，并进行了对抗性代码审查。

Result: 恢复的速度偏好权重与UPDRS-III严重程度相关(r=-0.607, p<0.001, n=42)，在两个独立子队列中复现，在9种敏感性配置下稳定，且比原始打字速度提供显著增量贡献(R²从0.194增至0.338, p=0.006)。一致性和手交替权重未通过混淆因素检验。修复会话边界污染和滚动窗口数据泄露两个漏洞后，主要结果实质不变。

Conclusion: IRL方法为击键动力学PD检测提供了新的可解释视角，验证过程本身(包括发现并修复实现漏洞)也是贡献的一部分。研究强调了在准确率差异显著的文献背景下，严格的验证流程对结果可信度的重要性。

Abstract: Keystroke dynamics have been explored extensively as a passive digital biomarker for Parkinson's disease (PD), typically by extracting summary statistics from typing timing and training a classifier to discriminate PD from healthy controls. We instead apply inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) to keystroke data, modeling each keystroke as a discrete choice over typing speed and recovering, per subject, an interpretable reward function that explains their observed timing behavior. To our knowledge this is the first application of IRL to keystroke dynamics. On the public neuroQWERTY MIT-CSXPD dataset (85 subjects, 42 with PD), an initial four-parameter reward decomposition (speed, effort, smoothness, hand-alternation cost) was found to suffer severe feature collinearity between two terms ($r=1.000$ in typical contexts); we diagnose and correct this, yielding an identifiable three-parameter model. The recovered speed-preference weight correlates with UPDRS-III severity at $r=-0.607$ ($p<0.001$, $n=42$), replicates independently across two sub-cohorts, is stable across nine sensitivity configurations, and retains a statistically significant contribution beyond raw typing speed alone (incremental $R^2$ from 0.194 to 0.338, $p=0.006$). Two other recovered weights (consistency, hand-alternation) did not survive confound checks and are reported as negative results. We document two implementation bugs found during adversarial code review (session-boundary contamination, a rolling-window data leakage) and show the headline result is materially unchanged after fixing both. We discuss this result in the context of a literature where reported accuracies vary widely between studies (pooled AUC 0.85, I^2=94% in a 2022 meta-analysis), and argue that the validation process itself, not only the correlation coefficient, is part of the contribution.

</details>


### [153] [Gradient-based inverse lithography for EUV masks via the waveguide method and a physics-informed neural operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25753)
*Vasiliy A. Es'kin,Egor V. Ivanov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于梯度的逆向光刻技术(ILT)，通过将可微分波导方法和波导神经算子(WGNO)作为端到端物理引擎，实现了对EUV掩模吸收层介电常数的自动微分反演，成功设计出满足晶圆目标场分布的掩模结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在极紫外(EUV)光刻技术中，掩模的精确设计与优化对实现目标晶圆图案至关重要。传统正向光刻模拟计算成本高昂，而逆向设计方法需要高效、精确地反演掩模吸收层的物理参数，这要求发展一种基于自动微分的高效梯度优化框架。

Method: 提出了一种新的梯度逆向光刻技术框架：将可微分波导方法和波导神经算子(WGNO)作为端到端的物理引擎，通过完整的正向衍射模型的自动微分，反演掩模吸收层的介电常数。该框架支持自动求导，实现了从目标场到掩模结构的反向梯度优化。

Result: 在λ=11.2nm波长下，对真实的2D和3D掩模吸收层材料(TaBN、La、U)进行了数值实验。结果表明，所提出的ILT方法能够成功获得满足晶圆目标场分布需求的掩模结构。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了基于自动微分的梯度逆向光刻技术在EUV掩模设计中的有效性，为高效、精确的掩模逆向设计提供了新的技术途径，具有重要的工业应用价值。

Abstract: Gradient-based inverse lithography technology~(ILT) for extreme ultraviolet~(EUV) masks is presented. A novel framework treats the differentiable waveguide method and the recently proposed waveguide neural operator~(WGNO) as end-to-end physics engines, recovering the permittivity of the absorber of the mask through automatic differentiation of the full forward diffraction model. Numerical experiments on realistic 2D and 3D absorbers of the mask (TaBN, La, U) at $λ{=}11.2$~nm show that the considered ILT methods make it possible to obtain a mask structure that achieves the desired field on the wafer.

</details>


### [154] [Uncertainty Quantification for Computer-Use Agents: A Benchmark across Vision-Language Models and GUI Grounding Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25760)
*Divake Kumar,Sina Tayebati,Devashri Naik,Amanda Sofie Rios,Nilesh Ahuja,Omesh Tickoo,Ranganath Krishnan,Amit Ranjan Trivedi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Argus基准测试，系统评估GUI智能体的不确定性量化方法，发现UQ排名在固定模型跨数据集时稳定，但跨模型类别和接口时会下降；闭源模型UQ需要在目标上重新排名而非外推。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用智能体将视觉语言模型预测转换为可执行的GUI点击，可靠的不确定性估计对于拒绝、校准、错误严重性排序和空间安全区域至关重要。然而现有的后验不确定性量化证据分散在孤立的模型和数据集对中，不清楚当智能体、基准测试或可观察接口变化时UQ排名是否保持稳定。

Method: 构建Argus跨机制基准测试：包含27种方法的开放权重矩阵（4个VLM智能体×4个数据集）和8种方法的闭源矩阵（3个前沿供应商）。评估方法涵盖基于logit的分数、采样和一致性度量、隐藏状态和密度估计器（Mahalanobis、SAPLMA）、注意力分数、P(True)和语言化置信度提示，以及分裂共形预测。

Result: 发现选择性迁移现象：固定模型跨数据集时UQ排名稳定（Spearman rho高达0.969），但跨模型类别和可观察接口时会下降；隐藏状态和密度方法是最稳定的开放权重家族；跨层级迁移到闭源供应商平均仅+0.08；共形点击区域显示分数级别判别不足以部署。

Conclusion: 闭源UQ应在目标上重新排名而非外推；隐藏状态和密度方法、CoCoA-1MCA、Focus、基于采样的分数和语言化自评估在特定机制下表现优异；校准的插件式UQ可将局部加权圆盘半径缩小40-60%，但在校准-测试或接口不匹配时覆盖率会下降。

Abstract: Computer-use agents turn vision-language model (VLM) predictions into executable GUI clicks, so reliable uncertainty estimates are essential for rejection, calibration, miss-severity ranking, and spatial safety regions. Yet evidence on post-hoc uncertainty quantification (UQ) for these agents is fragmented across isolated model and dataset pairs, leaving it unclear whether UQ rankings stay stable when the agent, benchmark, or observable interface changes. We present Argus, a cross-regime benchmark for post-hoc UQ in single-step executable GUI grounding: a 27-method open-weight matrix over 4 VLM agents and 4 datasets, plus an 8-method closed-source matrix across 3 frontier vendors where logits, hidden states, and attention maps are unavailable. Evaluated methods span logit-based scores, sampling and consistency measures, hidden-state and density estimators (Mahalanobis, SAPLMA), attention-based scores, P(True) and verbalised-confidence prompting, and split-conformal prediction. The main finding is selective transfer: UQ rankings are stable across datasets for a fixed model, but degrade across model classes and observable interfaces. Hidden-state and density methods are the most stable open-weight family, while CoCoA-1MCA, Focus, sampling-based scores, and verbalised self-assessment win in specific regimes. Within-model ranking transfer is strong (Spearman rho up to 0.969), but cross-tier transfer to closed-source vendors averages only +0.08, so closed-source UQ should be reranked on the target rather than extrapolated. Conformal click regions show score-level discrimination is not enough for deployment: locally weighted disks shrink radii by 40-60% when the plug-in UQ is calibrated, but coverage degrades under calibration-test or interface mismatch. We release per-item records, calibration/test splits, UQ scores, and analysis scripts for regime-aware UQ selection in GUI agents.

</details>


### [155] [OncoSynth: Synthetic data generation for treatment effect estimation in oncology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25762)
*Octavia-Andreea Ciora,Julian Welzel,Dennis Frauen,Maresa Schröder,Marie Brockschmidt,Harry Amad,Thomas Callender,Mihaela van der Schaar,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了OncoSynth，一个因果感知的生成式机器学习框架，用于生成合成癌症患者队列，在保护隐私的同时显著提升了治疗效果估计的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在肿瘤学研究中，患者层面的数据访问通常受到限制。虽然合成数据提供了分析治疗有效性的替代方案，但现有的合成数据生成方法无法保持协变量、治疗和结果之间的因果关系，导致治疗效果估计存在偏差。

Method: OncoSynth采用基于扩散模型的序列方法，建模协变量如何影响治疗分配以及治疗如何影响生存结果。这是一个生成式的、因果感知的机器学习框架。

Result: 在大型肺癌（N=37,128）和乳腺癌（N=17,046）队列上的评估表明，OncoSynth能够生成高保真度的合成患者队列，保持真实世界的患者、治疗和结果分布。与现有方法相比，群体层面治疗效果误差降低高达66%，患者层面治疗效果误差降低高达58%。

Conclusion: OncoSynth在数据共享受限的情况下支持精准肿瘤学的可靠证据生成，为解决肿瘤学研究中数据访问限制与治疗效果准确估计之间的矛盾提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: In oncology, access to patient-level data is often restricted. Synthetic data provides an alternative for analyzing treatment effectiveness, but existing methods for synthetic data generation fail to preserve the causal relationships between covariates, treatments, and outcomes, thereby leading to biased estimates of treatment effects. Here, we introduce OncoSynth, a generative, causally-aware machine learning framework designed to produce synthetic cohorts that enable accurate estimation of population- and patient-level treatment effects. OncoSynth uses a diffusion-based sequential approach to model how covariates influence treatment assignment and how treatment affects survival. We evaluate OncoSynth using large lung (N = 37,128) and breast cancer (N = 17,046) cohorts. Our results show that OncoSynth generates high-fidelity synthetic patient cohorts that preserve real-world patient, treatment, and outcome distributions. Notably, OncoSynth improves treatment effect estimation over existing approaches, by reducing population-level treatment effect error by up to 66%, and patient-level treatment effect error by up to 58%. Thereby, OncoSynth supports reliable evidence generation for precision oncology in settings where data sharing is restricted.

</details>


### [156] [MiniOpt: Reasoning to Model and Solve General Optimization Problems with Limited Resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25832)
*Ke Zhao,Zixiang Di,Hong Qian,Xiang Shu,Yaolin Wen,Qitao Shi,Bingdong Li,Xingyu Lu,Xiangfeng Wang,Jun Zhou,Ke Tang,Yang Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MiniOpt，一个基于强化学习的小型优化语言模型框架，通过“推理-建模-求解”范式和分层奖励函数，在无需大规模标注数据的情况下，实现了优异的优化问题泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的优化导向大语言模型方法依赖大规模监督数据集、昂贵的推理标注和中间步骤验证，导致训练开销巨大；同时，如何在有限训练资源下实现跨多样化优化问题的强泛化能力仍是一个挑战。

Method: 提出MiniOpt强化学习框架：1）采用“推理-建模-求解”范式，将优化推理分解为结构化优化建模与可执行求解器生成；2）引入OptReward分层奖励函数，联合评估建模和求解质量，无需专家示范即可实现有效策略学习；3）开发优化导向的策略优化方法，提升探索效率并稳定紧凑模型的强化学习训练。

Result: MiniOpt-3B在多种优化类型、问题场景和任务域上表现出强泛化能力；在参数少于10B的模型中，MiniOpt系列取得最高平均求解准确率；在参数超过10B的模型中，MiniOpt仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 优化导向的奖励设计与强化学习为开发具有强优化泛化能力的紧凑优化专用语言模型提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Achieving strong optimization generalization across diverse optimization problems while requiring limited training resources remains a challenging problem for optimization-oriented large language models (LLMs). Existing approaches typically rely on large-scale supervised datasets, costly reasoning annotations, and expensive intermediate step verification, resulting in substantial training overhead. To address these challenges, we propose MiniOpt, a reinforcement learning framework that learns to solve optimization problems through an "reasoning-to-model-and-solve" paradigm. MiniOpt decomposes optimization reasoning into structured optimization modeling and executable solver generation. Building upon this paradigm, we introduce OptReward, a reward function with hierarchical score structure that jointly evaluates formulation and solution, enabling effective policy learning without expert demonstrations. We further develop an optimization-oriented policy optimization strategy that improves exploration efficiency and stabilizes reinforcement learning for compact models. Extensive experiments show that MiniOpt-3B exhibits strong optimization generalization across various optimization types, problem scenarios, and task domains. For models with fewer than 10B parameters, MiniOpt series achieves the highest average solving accuracy (SA). For models with more than 10B parameters, MiniOpt still shows competitive performance. These results suggest that optimization-oriented reward design and reinforcement learning provide an effective pathway for developing compact optimization-specialized language models with strong optimization generalization capabilities. The code is available at https://github.com/Hsiang-1/MiniOpt.

</details>


### [157] [Stagnant Neuron: Towards Understanding the Plasticity Loss in Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning Value Factorization Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25335)
*Zhengzhu Liu,Zeming Gao,Haoyuan Qin,Jiawei Hu,Junhao Wu,Miao Zhu,Haipeng Zhang,Chennan Ma,Siqi Shen,Cheng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了KNIFE方法，通过识别并替换多智能体强化学习中的停滞神经元来解决价值分解方法在迁移到新任务时失去可塑性的问题，在多个基准测试中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体强化学习中的价值分解方法在迁移到新任务实例时会出现可塑性丧失问题，导致逐渐无法适应。作者将此问题追溯到停滞神经元——其梯度更新相对于权重变得微乎其微，从而阻碍学习。现有的可塑性注入方法对此类神经元无效。

Method: 提出知识保持神经元级可塑性聚焦注入（KNIFE）方法，直接针对停滞神经元。KNIFE将每个停滞神经元替换为由三个专门组件组成的复合单元：冻结知识神经元（保存已获取知识）、重新初始化的活跃神经元（恢复学习能力）和补偿神经元（确保组合输出与原始输出匹配，从而保持先前学习的合作知识）。

Result: 在SMACv2、捕食者-猎物和矩阵博弈上进行了大量实验，结果表明KNIFE显著优于最先进的可塑性注入方法。

Conclusion: KNIFE通过创新的神经元级复合替换策略，成功解决了MARL价值分解方法中的可塑性丧失问题，在保持已学习合作知识的同时恢复了对新任务的适应能力，为多智能体强化学习的迁移学习提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) value factorization methods can suffer from a loss of plasticity, gradually failing to adapt when transferring to new task instances. We trace this issue to stagnant neurons, units whose gradient updates become negligibly small relative to their weights, thereby hindering learning. While existing plasticity injection methods exist, they prove ineffective for such neurons. To address this, we propose Knowledge-retentive Neuron-level PlastIcity Focusing InjEction (KNIFE), a novel method that directly targets stagnant neurons. KNIFE replaces each stagnant neuron with a composite unit comprising three specialized components: a frozen knowledge neuron to preserve acquired knowledge, a re-initialized active neuron to restore learning capacity, and a compensation neuron to ensure the combined output matches the original, thus maintaining previous learned cooperation knowledge. Extensive experiments on SMACv2, predator-prey, and matrix games demonstrate that KNIFE significantly outperforms state-of-the-art plasticity injection methods.

</details>


### [158] [Semantic Consistency Policy Optimization for Reinforcement Learning of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25852)
*Peng Xu,Sijia Chen,Junzhuo Li,Xuming Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SCPO方法，通过从同一组成功轨迹中恢复步骤级信用来解决组强化学习中的语义信用不一致问题，在ALFWorld和WebShop任务上达到93.7%和74.8%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组强化学习在训练LLA智能体时存在语义信用不一致问题：语义相似的中间步骤会因轨迹最终成败而获得相反的信用分配，这导致相似动作收到冲突的梯度信号，并浪费失败轨迹中部分正确的进展。

Method: 提出语义一致性策略优化(SCPO)方法，一种无价值的奖励塑形技术。SCPO通过从同一回滚组中的成功兄弟节点恢复步骤级信用来缓解不一致性。具体地，SCPO将每个失败步骤与成功兄弟节点进行评分，并为该兄弟节点的新进展添加正向步骤级信用。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop基准上，SCPO匹配或超越强组基线方法。在1.5B参数规模下，ALFWorld成功率达93.7±4.1%，WebShop达74.8±2.0%，增益主要集中在最难的多步任务上。

Conclusion: SCPO有效解决了组强化学习中的语义信用不一致问题，通过利用成功兄弟节点恢复步骤级信用，显著提升了长视界稀疏奖励任务中LLM智能体的性能，尤其在困难的多步任务上表现突出。

Abstract: Group-based reinforcement learning effectively post-trains LLM agents for long-horizon, sparse-reward tasks by deriving step-level credit from trajectory outcomes. However, this ties a step's credit to its rollout's final outcome: semantically near-identical intermediate steps receive opposite credit depending on whether their trajectory eventually succeeded or failed. Such semantic credit inconsistency sends conflicting gradients to similar actions and wastes the partially-correct progress inside failed rollouts. Motivated by this, we propose Semantic Consistency Policy Optimization (SCPO), a value-free reward-shaping method that mitigates this inconsistency by recovering step-level credit from successful siblings in the same rollout group. Concretely, SCPO scores each failed step against a successful sibling and adds positive step-level credit for new progress along that sibling. On ALFWorld and WebShop, SCPO matches or exceeds strong group-based baselines, reaching 93.7+/-4.1 percent success on ALFWorld and 74.8+/-2.0 percent on WebShop at 1.5B parameters, with gains concentrated on the hardest multi-step tasks.

</details>


### [159] [Lifelong In-Context Learning with Transformers Requires Parametric Forms of Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25342)
*Luke McDermott,Robert W. Heath,Rahul Parhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将上下文学习扩展到终身学习场景，论证参数化注意力机制是实现固定硬件预算下Transformer终身学习的关键解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终身持续学习是实现类人智能的主要障碍。现代Transformer虽然展现出上下文学习能力，但注意力机制的二次复杂度限制了其在任意长序列上的应用。现有非参数化方法（如softmax注意力）需要不断增长的键值缓存，无法在固定内存预算下处理终身长度的上下文。

Method: 提出使用参数化注意力机制替代非参数化的softmax注意力。参数化方法（包括线性注意力、状态空间模型、快速权重编程器和测试时训练层）通过参数回归在测试时学习键值关系，用在线可训练的神经网络替代不断增长的键值缓存，从而保持恒定的内存占用。

Result: 系统分析了参数化注意力机制在终身学习中的优势与不足。发现当前参数化注意力方法由于内存容量限制或昂贵的在线更新成本，仍无法完全满足终身学习的需求，存在性能瓶颈。

Conclusion: 指出了参数化注意力在终身学习中面临的核心挑战，提出了一系列开放性问题和新见解，为构建长时域智能体的研究方向提供了指导框架，推动该领域向实用化的终身学习系统发展。

Abstract: Lifelong continual learning remains an obstacle on the path to human-like intelligence. Modern transformers show sparks of intelligence with in-context learning. The quadratic nature of attention, however, prohibits transformers from performing this process on arbitrarily long sequences. In this work, we argue that extending in-context learning to lifelong settings is a practical solution for continual learning in AI agents. In particular, we argue that \emph{parametric forms of attention} are needed to understand a lifetime of context with transformers on a fixed hardware budget. These attention mechanisms learn the relationship between keys and their associated values at test-time with parametric regression. Our generalization of parametric approaches (linear attention, state-space models, fast weight programmers, and test-time training layers) contrasts with nonparametric counterparts like softmax attention. They replace the ever-growing key-value cache with an online-trainable neural network, maintaining a constant memory footprint. We highlight how parametric attention currently fall short of lifelong learning due to limited memory capacity or costly online updates. To address these issues, we pose a set of open questions with novel insights to guide the field toward long-horizon agents.

</details>


### [160] [Geometry-Anchored Transport Framework for Exemplar-Free Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25347)
*Hongye Xu,Bartosz Krawczyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了几何锚定传输框架(GATF)，通过在训练过程中引入几何约束来解决无样本类增量学习中的特征漂移问题，相比传统的解耦后处理方法取得了更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无样本类增量学习需要在特征空间漂移的情况下保持稳定的决策边界。现有的基于类条件高斯统计的方法对各向异性表示漂移敏感，而传统方法采用解耦的后处理范式进行特征传输，在优化骨干网络时缺乏显式几何约束，导致旧类别流形失真，限制了回溯对齐的精度。

Method: 提出了几何锚定传输框架，将特征传输作为内源性训练约束而非独立的后处理步骤。具体包括：(1)通过马氏对齐回归推导解析几何锚点，以缓解宏观各向异性漂移；(2)引入拓扑感知演化目标，在根据解析先验校准残差网络的同时，正则化局部流形退化。该框架在主训练阶段耦合流形演化与传输约束。

Result: 在CIFAR-100、TinyImageNet和ImageNet-100数据集上的实验表明，所提框架在严格的无样本约束下，相比现有的后处理替代方法取得了持续的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过将特征传输约束集成到训练过程中而非作为后处理步骤，几何锚定传输框架有效缓解了评估错误，无需解耦微调即可在无样本类增量学习中实现更好的性能，为该领域提供了一种新的解决方案。

Abstract: Exemplar-free class-incremental learning (EFCIL) requires stable decision boundaries within a shifting feature space. While maintaining class-conditional Gaussian statistics provides a principled classification strategy, these parametric summaries remain sensitive to anisotropic representation drift. Existing methods often transport these statistics across tasks using a decoupled, post-hoc paradigm: optimizing a backbone without explicit geometric constraints can distort the legacy manifold, limiting the precision of retroactive alignment. In this paper, we formulate feature transport as an endogenous training constraint rather than a separate post-task step, presenting the Geometry-Anchored Transport Framework. First, we derive an Analytic Geometric Anchor via Mahalanobis-aligned regression to mitigate macroscopic anisotropic drift. Second, we introduce a Topology-Aware Evolution objective that regularizes localized manifold degradation while calibrating a residual network against the analytic prior. By coupling manifold evolution with transport constraints during the primary training phase, our framework mitigates evaluation errors without requiring decoupled fine-tuning. Experiments across CIFAR-100, TinyImageNet, and ImageNet-100 demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently improves upon existing post-hoc alternatives under strict exemplar-free constraints.

</details>


### [161] [Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning for Neural Network Compression (HiReLC): Pruning and Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26002)
*Kamar Hibatallah Baghdadi,Kawther Guoual Belhamidi,Sara Belhadj,Aissa Boulmerka,Nadir Farhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了HiReLC，一个层次化集成强化学习框架，用于深度神经网络的自动化联合量化和结构化剪枝。该方法在保持较高压缩比（5.99-6.72×）的同时，能够最小化精度损失，部分设置甚至实现精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络的压缩通常涉及量化和剪枝两个关键步骤，但如何在统一的框架中自动化地联合优化这两个过程仍是一个挑战。现有方法往往独立处理这些压缩技术，忽略了它们之间的相互作用和全局资源分配的最优化问题。因此，需要一种能够有效协调多层压缩决策、平衡计算成本与压缩质量的自动化方法。

Method: HiReLC采用双层强化学习架构：低层智能体(LLA)独立地为每个网络块选择具体的压缩配置，包括位宽、剪枝保留率、量化类型和粒度；高层智能体(HLA)通过集成投票机制协调全局预算分配，并结合Fisher信息敏感性估计进行指导。为降低策略评估的计算成本，引入迭代主动学习循环，结合代理引导的RL优化和压缩后微调。使用轻量级MLP代理模型来分摊昂贵的评估成本，并在冷启动阶段使用logit-MSE代理进行奖励塑形而非替代最终评估。控制器采用架构无关设计，通过模块化层抽象将RL环境与底层网络拓扑解耦。

Result: 在Vision Transformer和CNN基准测试上验证了方法有效性。实现了5.99-6.72×的参数存储压缩比，在某一设置中获得3.83%的精度提升，其他设置中精度下降控制在0.55-5.62%范围内。实验结果支持层次化策略分解和敏感性感知指导作为联合神经网络压缩的实用设计选择。

Conclusion: HiReLC成功实现了深度神经网络的自动化联合量化和剪枝，证明了层次化强化学习架构在压缩搜索中的有效性。该方法通过双层抽象、敏感性感知指导以及代理辅助的优化策略，在保证压缩效果的同时显著降低了计算开销，为神经网络的自动化压缩提供了一个实用的解决方案。

Abstract: We present HiReLC, a hierarchical ensemble-reinforcement learning framework for automated joint quantization and structured pruning of deep neural networks. The framework decomposes the compression search across two levels of abstraction: low-level agents (LLAs) operate independently per block, selecting per-kernel configurations over a multi-discrete action space spanning bitwidth, pruning keep-ratio, quantization type, and granularity, while high-level agents (HLAs) coordinate global budget allocation via ensemble voting guided by Fisher Information-based sensitivity estimates. To mitigate the computational cost of policy evaluation, an iterative active learning loop interleaves surrogate-guided RL optimization with post-compression fine-tuning, using a lightweight MLP surrogate to amortize expensive evaluations and a logit-MSE proxy during cold-start. The surrogate is used for reward shaping rather than as a replacement for final post-compression evaluation. The controller is architecture-agnostic by design, with a modular layer abstraction decoupling the RL environment from the underlying network topology. Experiments across Vision Transformer and CNN benchmarks demonstrate effective parameter-storage compression ratios of 5.99 - 6.72$\times$ with a 3.83 % gain in one setting and 0.55 - 5.62 % accuracy drops elsewhere, supporting hierarchical policy decomposition and sensitivity-aware guidance as practical design choices for joint neural network compression.

</details>


### [162] [Natural Ungrokking: Asymmetric Control of Which Rules Survive Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26050)
*Juliana Li,Diya Sreedhar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现语言模型在预训练过程中会出现"自然去习得"现象：模型先学会某条规则，随后又将其遗忘。规则是否保留取决于训练语料中支持该规则的频率，而非数据-参数比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者观察到语言模型在普通预训练过程中会出现一个反直觉的现象：模型在训练中期学会了某种语言学规则（如代词-性别对应规则），但在后续训练中反而将该规则遗忘，尽管训练数据中仍包含相关证据。这种"先习得后遗忘"的现象促使研究者探究其背后的机制和决定因素。

Method: 研究者通过以下方法进行研究：(1) 追踪小规模语言模型在训练过程中对代词-性别规则的学习和遗忘轨迹；(2) 分析训练语料中支持性证据频率与规则保留的关系；(3) 在公开的Pythia模型检查点上验证相同现象；(4) 通过干预实验控制训练数据中的支持性证据和反证；(5) 分析竞争性表面对模式的概率边际变化；(6) 所有确认性阈值和预测均预先注册。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 规则是否保留可由语料统计预测——训练流中规则获胜的频率决定其命运；(2) 数据-参数比例仅调节注定消亡规则的下降深度；(3) 遗忘是一种替代效应：竞争性表面对模式胜过规则，两者间对数概率边际在行为崩溃100步内跨过零点；(4) 控制能力不对称：删除支持性证据可杀死规则，但注入支持性证据即使达到自然维持水平的450倍也无法恢复规则。

Conclusion: 训练语料本身决定了模型保留哪些学习到的规则，且这种现象在损失曲线上无任何痕迹。规则的学习和遗忘呈现出"涌现-崩溃"的动态过程，这一发现在多个模型和数据集上得到验证，表明语言模型的学习过程比此前认知的更加复杂和不稳定。

Abstract: Midway through an ordinary pretraining run, a small language model learns the pronoun-gender rule: cued with a girl's name ("Sue cried because"), it resolves the next pronoun to she, generalizing to held-out probes (0.94 by step 925). By step 3,500 the same model scores near zero on the same probes, although the rule's evidence is still in the training data. We call this within-run reversal natural ungrokking: the corpus decides, with no trace in the loss curve, which learned rules a model keeps.
  Which rules survive is predictable from one corpus statistic: how often the training stream shows the rule winning. Across un-intervened runs (two corpora, three budgets, three seeds), support frequency decides a rule's fate; the data-to-parameter ratio only modulates how deeply a doomed rule falls. The same emerge-then-collapse dynamics appear in public Pythia checkpoints, collapse depth ordered by model scale as predicted. The forgetting is a displacement: a competing surface pattern out-competes the rule, and the log-probability margin between them crosses zero within 100 training steps of the behavioral collapse.
  Control over this fate is asymmetric: the same edit that destroys a rule on demand cannot restore it. Flipping support to counter-evidence in place kills the rule with monotone dose-response in two unrelated rules; but injecting support back, even to 450 times the level that naturally sustains it, buys no recovery. Every confirmatory threshold and prediction was pre-registered before the data it governed was read.

</details>


### [163] [DFMU: Data-Frugal Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25410)
*Sajith U,Prateek Keserwani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据节俭的机器遗忘方法（DFMU），仅需13%的数据样本和单次前向反向传播，即可在保持模型性能的同时实现快速遗忘，相比现有最优方法准确率提升40%，处理速度平均快88%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器遗忘是一个新兴领域，旨在从训练模型中安全移除元素（如概念、属性、实体和类别），同时最小化模型性能下降。现有方法主要依赖重新训练来移除元素，计算成本高昂，且移除预训练元素不可避免地会降低模型在剩余元素上的性能，这带来了该领域特有的挑战。

Method: 提出数据节俭机器遗忘（DFMU）方法，仅需单次前向和反向传播来计算模型各计算块的重要性分数。该重要性分数计算基于知识保留剪枝技术，能够更快收敛且相比现有方法需要更少的数据，避免了大规模重新训练预训练模型。

Result: 在多个公共数据集上的实验表明，DFMU仅使用13%的数据样本，相比现有最优方法实现40%的保留准确率提升，同时在遗忘指定类别时平均处理速度快88%。

Conclusion: DFMU方法成功降低了机器遗忘的计算需求，实现了更快的保留数据集准确率收敛，无需大规模重新训练，在数据效率和计算效率方面均显著优于现有方法，为机器遗忘领域提供了一种高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Machine unlearning is an emerging domain that ensures the safe removal of elements (includes concepts, attributes, entity and class) from the trained model along with least drop in model performance. The domain of machine unlearning brings its own indigenous challenges since the removal of pre-trained elements from model will always degrade the model performance on remaining elements. The existing methods basically rely on retraining for removal of elements from the pre-trained model, which is compute extensive. In this work, we propose a machine unlearning method which helps to reduce the computational requirement for faster retain-dataset accuracy convergence which also does not require extensive retraining of the pre-trained model. The proposed method, Data-Frugal Machine Unlearning (DFMU) requires only a single forward and backward pass for computing the importance score of various computational blocks of a model. The importance score computation is based on knowledge preserving pruning which helps to converge faster and requires far less data as compared to the existing methods. Experimentally, it achieves 40% more retain-accuracy with just 13% of data samples in comparison with SOTA method on various public datasets and also averages 88% faster processing time for forgetting a given class.

</details>


### [164] [Model Forensics: Investigating Whether Concerning Behavior Reflects Misalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26071)
*Aditya Singh,Gerson Kroiz,Senthooran Rajamanoharan,Neel Nanda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了模型取证（model forensics）的概念和基线协议，通过分析思维链和反事实实验来区分模型的有害行为是由恶意意图驱动还是良性原因（如混淆）导致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全研究主要通过检测行为来判断模型是否失调，但有害行为本身不足以证明失调——它可能源于混淆等良性原因。因此需要模型取证来调查行为的驱动因素是否为恶意意图。

Method: 提出一个迭代两步基线协议：(1)阅读思维链（CoT）生成关于驱动模型行为的假设；(2)编辑提示词或环境来测试这些假设。虽然CoT不一定完全忠实，但它是无监督洞察的丰富来源，可指导收集更严谨的证据。研究者在六个代理环境中评估了该协议。

Result: 研究发现在Kimi K2 Thinking中，模型的捷径行为源于对低努力行为的真实偏好，该假设成功预测了其行为。通过反事实实验，发现DeepSeek R1的欺骗行为源于与自身先前实例保持一致的愿望。但在测试Kimi K2 Thinking是否相信自己违反用户意图时，未发现此类信念的证据，但由于缺乏阳性对照，无法确认测试能否检测到它。

Conclusion: 这个简单的协议提供了强有力的基线，为未来工作的改进奠定了基础。该工作是发展模型取证领域的重要一步，尽管仍有显著改进空间。

Abstract: A central goal of safety research is determining whether a model is misaligned. Prior work has largely focused on detecting concerning behavior. But behavior alone does not establish misalignment: a concerning action can arise from benign causes such as confusion. This motivates model forensics: investigating whether the action was driven by malign intent. In this paper, we propose a baseline protocol for model forensics consisting of two steps, iterated as needed. First, we read the chain of thought (CoT) to generate hypotheses about what drives model behavior. Second, we make edits to the prompt or environment to test these hypotheses. While the CoT is not always faithful, it is a rich source of unsupervised insight that can guide the collection of more rigorous evidence. To evaluate our protocol, we create a suite of six agentic environments where models exhibit concerning behavior, and apply it to each. We establish that Kimi K2 Thinking takes shortcuts due to a genuine disposition towards low-effort actions, by showing this hypothesis successfully predicts its behavior. Through counterfactual experiments, we show DeepSeek R1 deceives out of a desire to be consistent with a previous instance of itself. Our methods nonetheless leave significant room for refinement. For example, when we test whether Kimi K2 Thinking believes it is violating user intent, we find no evidence of such a belief, but without positive controls we cannot confirm our tests would detect it. Overall, we find our simple protocol provides a strong baseline that we hope future work will improve upon. More broadly, our work is a concrete step in developing the growing field of model forensics.

</details>


### [165] [Neglected Free Lunch from Post-training: Progress Advantage for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26080)
*Changdae Oh,Wendi Li,Seongheon Park,Samuel Yeh,Tanwi Mallick,Sharon Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Progress Advantage方法，利用RL后训练中的策略与参考策略的对数概率比，无需额外训练即可实现高效的步骤级评估，在测试时扩展、不确定性量化和失败归因任务上均优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在智能体环境中构建过程奖励模型面临巨大挑战：长时间跨度交互、不可逆动作以及随机环境反馈使得大规模人工标注和蒙特卡洛估计不可行，需要一种无需专门训练的方法来实现步骤级评估。

Method: 基于一般随机马尔可夫决策过程推导出隐式优势函数（称为Progress Advantage），即RL训练策略与其参考策略之间的对数概率比，该比值恰好恢复最优优势函数，从而将步骤级评分信号作为标准RL后训练流程的副产品获得。

Result: 在三个应用场景（测试时扩展、不确定性量化、失败归因）和五个基准测试、四个模型家族上的实验表明，Progress Advantage始终优于基于置信度的基线方法，且在无需任务特定训练的情况下超越了专门训练的奖励模型。

Conclusion: Progress Advantage是一种无需标注、领域无关且源自标准RL后训练流程的步骤级评估信号，为智能体系统提供了实用有效的评估方案，具有广泛的应用价值。
