Abstract: Process reward models enable fine-grained, step-level evaluation of LLMs, yet building them for agentic settings remains prohibitively difficult: long-horizon interactions, irreversible actions, and stochastic environment feedback make both human annotation and Monte Carlo estimation infeasible at scale. In this work, we show that reinforcement learning (RL) post-training already provides the ingredients for effective step-level scoring, eliminating the need for dedicated reward model training altogether. Concretely, we derive an implicit advantage under a general stochastic Markov decision process, which we term progress advantage -- log-probability ratio between the RL-trained policy and its reference policy exactly recovers the optimal advantage function. This formulation makes the resulting signal annotation-free, domain-agnostic, and available as a byproduct of the standard RL post-training pipeline. We validate the effectiveness of the progress advantage across three different applications: test-time scaling, uncertainty quantification, and failure attribution on five benchmarks and four model families. Across all settings, it consistently outperforms confidence-based baselines and, despite requiring no task-specific training, surpasses dedicated trained reward models. We complement these results with deeper analyses on characteristics of progress advantage, offering practical guidance for adoption in real-world agentic systems.

</details>


### [166] [On-Policy Self-Distillation with Sampled Demonstrations Reduces Output Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26091)
*Andrei Liviu Nicolicioiu,Mohammad Pezeshki,Aaron Courville*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在策略自蒸馏虽然能获得强的pass@1准确率，但会降低rollout多样性并使pass@k曲线趋于平缓，导致在需要多样化策略的分布外场景下表现失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示在策略自蒸馏方法存在的隐藏代价：尽管能提升单次通过率，但会以牺牲多样性为代价，导致生成更多rollout无法提升准确率。

Method: 理论分析最优自蒸馏策略，证明其通过点条件互信息分数倾斜基础分布；与理想的最优在策略强化学习（保持等正确rollout的概率比）进行对比；在图寻路任务和科学问答基准上进行实验验证。

Result: 自蒸馏模型在平均性能上匹配或超过强化学习，但表现出显著较低的功能和语义多样性，在需要多样化策略的分布外设置中失败。

Conclusion: 自蒸馏通过模型自身偏置传导反馈，会放大现有概率差距，将质量集中在已占主导地位的模式上，这与理想最优在策略强化学习形成对比。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation achieves strong pass@1 accuracy by using a single model as both teacher and student, with the teacher conditioned on a correct demonstration to provide dense token-level feedback. We show that this could come at a hidden cost: rollout diversity decreases and pass@k curves flatten (i.e., generating more rollouts fails to improve accuracy). We trace this to compounding biases in the design of self-distillation with sampled demonstrations. The teacher scores each student rollout while conditioned on a sampled correct rollout, channeling its feedback through the model's own biases. We theoretically analyze the optimal self-distillation policy and show that it tilts the base distribution by a pointwise conditional mutual information score between the student's rollout and the correct rollout used as context. Unlike the ideal optimal on-policy reinforcement learning (RL), which preserves probability ratios among equally correct rollouts, self-distillation can amplify existing probability gaps, concentrating mass on already-dominant modes. On a controlled graph path-finding task and science question-answering benchmarks, self-distilled models match or exceed RL on average performance but exhibit substantially lower functional and semantic diversity, failing on out-of-distribution settings that require diverse strategies.

</details>


### [167] [The Interplay of Harness Design and Post-Training in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25447)
*Kyungmin Kim,Youngbin Choi,Seoyeon Lee,Suhyeon Jun,Dongwoo Kim,Sangdon Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将工具集成LLM代理的脚手架视为可控设计维度，提出harness-aware后训练方法，显著提升了代理在分布内和分布外场景下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具集成LLM代理的后训练算法假设环境是静态的，且将脚手架（包括工具暴露方式、描述及辅助信息）视为固定的工程细节，缺乏对脚手架设计的系统性优化，导致代理在任务和工具环境发生变化时性能下降。

Method: 扩展ALFWorld基准以支持：(i)将脚手架作为可控设计维度；(ii)支持任务和工具环境变化的评估。基于此，系统性地分析脚手架设计对分布内和分布外场景下后训练的影响。

Result: 实验表明：(1)harness-aware后训练不仅提升了分布内性能，还能使代理在OOD场景下实现鲁棒适应；(2)在最小化脚手架设计下，后训练在更强工具环境变化时性能急剧下降，凸显了harness-aware后训练的重要性。

Conclusion: 脚手架设计是后训练的关键因素，harness-aware后训练对于代理在动态环境中的鲁棒性至关重要，特别是在工具环境发生显著变化时。

Abstract: Tool-integrated LLM agents are often wrapped within a harness: the scaffolding that determines which tools are exposed, how they are described, and what auxiliary information accompanies each per-step observation. While agents are routinely post-trained, this scaffolding is typically treated as a fixed engineering detail, with design effort limited to the training-free regime. Moreover, existing post-training algorithms assume a static environment, even though tool environments and tasks often shift upon deployment. To address this gap, we extend $\texttt{ALFWorld}$ (i) to treat the harness as a controllable design dimension and (ii) to support evaluation under task and tool environment shifts. Building on this, we systematically analyze how the harness design influences post-training in both in-distribution and out-of-distribution (OOD) settings. We empirically show that harness-aware post-training not only improves in-distribution performance but also enables agents to robustly adapt to OOD settings. Under a harness with minimal design effort, post-training suffers a drastic performance drop under stronger tool environment shifts, further highlighting the importance of harness-aware post-training under such shifts.

</details>


### [168] [The Generalization Spectrum: A Chromatographic Approach to Evaluating Learning Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25450)
*Jinghan Zhang,Zerui Cheng,Shiqi Chen,Ge Zhang,Wenhao Huang,Jiashuo Liu,Junxian He,Tianle Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了“泛化谱（Generalization Spectrum）”评估框架，通过构建不同迁移距离的测试变体来揭示单样本泛化能力，发现强化学习在近迁移上优于监督学习，但局部性能提升不一定扩展泛化半径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统评估方法使用独立同分布测试集的单一聚合分数，掩盖了一个基本问题：从特定样本学习的知识能泛化到其他样本的程度如何？这种单样本泛化能力（类似人类类比学习）在标准基准测试中不可见。

Method: 提出泛化谱评估框架：为每个训练样本构建按迁移距离递增排列的测试变体套件，包括精确回忆、跨语言实现迁移、完全叙事重构下的上下文迁移、类别匹配的域内问题以及未配对基线。在竞争性编程任务上实例化该框架，使用选择-合成流程并加入近期问题以缓解数据污染。

Result: 在匹配记忆化条件下比较三种学习范式：强化学习比监督微调系列基线更有效地将记忆转化为近迁移；上下文学习表现出强但依赖对应关系的迁移能力。进一步分析发现：抽象和提示主要提升局部迁移，拒绝微调比参考SFT保留更强的远迁移尾部，自蒸馏或提示辅助的强化学习在局部迁移或优化改善时甚至可能降低远迁移能力。

Conclusion: 局部性能增益不必然扩展泛化半径；泛化谱框架能够揭示算法学习的深度和广度，为诊断学习算法的泛化特性提供了新视角。

Abstract: Traditional evaluations measure a learning algorithm's final performance on an i.i.d. test set, reducing learning to a single aggregate score. This approach obscures a fundamental question: to what extent does learning from a specific example generalize to others? Such per-sample generalization, akin to learning by analogy in human cognition, captures how far the knowledge extracted from one example can transfer, yet remains invisible to standard benchmarks. We introduce the Generalization Spectrum, an evaluation framework designed to expose this hidden dimension. For each training example, we construct a controlled suite of test variants arranged by increasing transfer distance, from exact recall to implementation transfer across languages, context transfer under complete narrative re-framing, category-matched in-domain problems, and an unpaired baseline. By tracking performance across these distances, we reveal not just whether an algorithm learns, but how far that learning extends. We instantiate this framework on competitive programming, using a selection-and-synthesis pipeline seeded with recent problems to mitigate contamination. We first compare three canonical learning paradigms under matched memorization. RL converts memorization into near-transfer more efficiently than SFT-family baselines, while ICL exhibits strong but correspondence-dependent transfer. We then use the Spectrum to diagnose within-family variants. The resulting profiles show that local gains need not expand the generalization radius: abstractions and hints mainly lift local transfer, RFT preserves a stronger far-transfer tail than reference SFT, and self-distillation or hint-assisted RL can reduce far transfer even when local transfer or optimization improves.

</details>


### [169] [Towards Robust EEG Decoding Based on Riemannian Self-Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25456)
*Shaocheng Jin,Tao Zhou,Rui Wang,Ziheng Chen,Xiaoqing Luo,Xiaojun Wu,Josef Kittler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了基于Bures-Wasserstein度量的黎曼自注意力网络(GBWAtt)，用于EEG脑机接口解码，解决了传统SPD学习方法在低信噪比和病态矩阵条件下性能受限的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于对称正定(SPD)学习的EEG解码方法存在两个主要缺陷：(1)采用基础网络架构，未显式捕获EEG信号间的局部关系，而EEG信号固有的低信噪比特性使得这一问题更加严重；(2)大多数黎曼流形方法局限于特定的仿射不变度量(AIM)，该度量对SPD矩阵具有二次依赖性，无法有效处理病态SPD矩阵，限制了网络性能。

Method: 提出基于Bures-Wasserstein度量(BWM)的黎曼自注意力网络，利用BWM对SPD矩阵的线性依赖性及其在病态条件下的优越性能。进一步引入幂变形广义Bures-Wasserstein度量，揭示了SPD矩阵与矩阵幂变形之间的非线性关系，提供了SPD流形几何结构的更细致表示，并将模型扩展为可学习版本GBWAtt。

Result: 在三个EEG基准数据集上的实验结果验证了所提方法的鲁棒性和有效性，证明了基于Bures-Wasserstein度量的方法相比传统仿射不变度量方法在处理低信噪比和病态矩阵条件下的优越性。

Conclusion: GBWAtt通过引入Bures-Wasserstein度量族和自注意力机制，成功解决了传统SPD学习方法在EEG解码中的关键限制，为脑机接口应用提供了更鲁棒和有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Brain-Computer Interface (BCI) based on electroencephalography (EEG) enables direct interaction between the brain and external environments and has significant applications in assistive technologies, medical rehabilitation, and entertainment. Recently, EEG decoding methods based on Symmetric Positive Definite (SPD) learning have demonstrated superior performance. However, these methods typically employ basic network architectures and do not explicitly capture local relationships between EEG signals. This limitation is problematic for EEG signals due to their inherently low Signal-to-Noise Ratio (SNR). Moreover, most existing Riemannian manifold-based methods are restricted to specific metrics. The most widely used is the Affine-Invariant Metric (AIM). However, it has a quadratic dependency on the SPD matrices and cannot handle ill-conditioned SPD matrices, which hinders the effectiveness of networks. In contrast, the Bures-Wasserstein Metric (BWM) exhibits linear dependence on SPD matrices and demonstrates superior performance for ill conditioning. To overcome these challenges, we propose a Riemannian self-attention network based on the BWM. Additionally, the recently introduced power-deformed generalized Bures-Wasserstein metric reveals a nonlinear relationship between SPD matrices and matrix power deformation. This metric provides a more nuanced representation of the geometric structure of the SPD manifold. Consequently, we extend our model to a learnable version. For simplicity, we refer to it as GBWAtt. Experimental results on three EEG benchmarking datasets validate the robustness and effectiveness of our proposed method. The code is available at https://github.com/jissc/GBWAtt.

</details>


### [170] [Distill on a Diet: Efficient Knowledge Distillation via Learnable Data Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25488)
*Yifan Wu,Yiqi Wang,Xichen Ye,Wenjing Yan,Xiaoqiang Li,Cheng Jin,Xiangyu Yue,Weizhong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出IF-Beta框架，通过结合影响函数和可学习的Beta分布采样策略，实现知识蒸馏过程的高效数据剪枝，使学生在更少数据和计算量下超越全量数据蒸馏性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识蒸馏被广泛用于获得紧凑模型，但蒸馏过程本身的计算开销常被忽视。现有数据剪枝方法并非为知识蒸馏设计：有的引入重训练等大量开销，有的依赖启发式规则无法捕捉蒸馏实际需求，导致子集选择次优。因此需要专为知识蒸馏设计的高效数据剪枝方法。

Method: 提出IF-Beta框架，核心包含两部分：(1)利用影响函数作为知识蒸馏中样本影响力的有效且高效的估计器；(2)采用Beta分布参数化的可学习采样策略，通过双层优化目标进行优化——内层在教师特征空间进行快速代理训练，外层更新策略参数以最大化蒸馏性能。Beta分布的双参数族使策略能适应多样化的剪枝场景。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/100和ImageNet上的广泛实验表明，IF-Beta在广泛的剪枝比例下持续优于其他基线方法。值得注意的是，使用IF-Beta在更少数据和计算量上训练的学生模型能够超越在全量数据集上蒸馏的学生性能。

Conclusion: IF-Beta成功解决了知识蒸馏中数据剪枝的关键挑战，通过影响函数与灵活的可学习采样策略的结合，实现了计算高效且与知识蒸馏目标内在对齐的剪枝框架，证明了更少数据和计算可获得更优学生模型的可行性。

Abstract: Knowledge Distillation (KD) is widely used to obtain compact models for efficient inference in resource-constrained environments. Yet the computational overhead of the distillation process itself is often overlooked, raising the question of whether a better student model can be obtained with less data and less compute via data pruning. However, existing data pruning methods are not designed for KD: some introduce substantial overhead, such as obtaining training dynamics through retraining, while others rely on heuristic selection rules that fail to capture what KD actually requires, often resulting in suboptimal subsets. To address these issues, we propose IF-Beta, an efficient data pruning framework that combines influence functions with a learnable sampling policy. Empirically, we first demonstrate that influence functions can serve as an effective and efficient estimator of sample impact in KD settings, where only a pretrained teacher is available. Building on this, our sampling policy is specifically parameterized by a Beta distribution, whose highly flexible two-parameter family allows the policy to adapt to diverse pruning regimes rather than being tied to fixed heuristic forms. Next, we formulate KD pruning as optimizing this policy through a bilevel objective, where the inner loop operates in the teacher feature space with a KD-aligned objective, enabling fast proxy training, while the outer loop updates the policy parameters to maximize distillation performance. This design ensures that IF-Beta is both computationally efficient and inherently aligned with the goals of KD. Extensive experiments on CIFAR-10/100 and ImageNet show that IF-Beta consistently outperforms other baselines across a wide range of pruning ratios. Remarkably, IF-Beta enables students trained on less data and less compute to surpass the performance of students distilled on the full dataset.

</details>


### [171] [Beyond One-Size-Fits-All: Diagnosis-Driven Online Reinforcement Learning with Offline Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25527)
*Guozheng Ma,Lu Li,Zilin Wang,Pierre-Luc Bacon,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文主张将在线强化学习中对离线先验知识的管理从基准驱动范式转变为诊断驱动的张力管理，通过部署特定证据动态指导先验的使用，以应对先验有效性在不同部署中变化的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线强化学习代理越来越依赖离线获取的知识（如离线数据集、预训练策略、多模态基础模型、生成式世界模型等），但这种依赖带来了基准驱动范式无法解决的挑战：先验的有效性在部署间变化并在训练过程中发生偏移，没有单一方法能够普遍最优，基准排名对实际部署的指导价值有限。

Method: 提出诊断驱动的张力管理框架，用部署特定证据指导学习者在训练过程中如何与其先验建立关系。该框架通过三个功能角色来刻画先验如何重塑在线优化，并通过受控实验展示先验的帮助-伤害反转现象。

Result: 展示了先验在不同条件下可能发挥帮助或阻碍作用的反转现象，提供了从基础模型后训练到具身智能等跨领域证据，验证了诊断驱动方法的必要性。

Conclusion: 该领域应从追求通用解决方案转向诊断驱动的张力管理，使基于部署特定证据的灵活自适应部署成为可能，从而更有效地管理在线强化学习中多样化的离线先验知识。

Abstract: Online reinforcement learning (RL) agents increasingly depend on knowledge acquired offline to achieve practical efficiency. Originally studied in offline-to-online RL, this paradigm now spans foundation model post-training and embodied intelligence, with prior types expanding from offline datasets and pre-trained policies to increasingly diverse knowledge sources such as multimodal foundation models and generative world models. Offline priors have become central to how deep RL is developed and deployed. However, this reliance introduces a challenge that the prevailing benchmark-driven paradigm cannot resolve: because prior validity varies across deployments and shifts during training, no single approach to managing it is universally optimal, and benchmark rankings offer limited guidance for real-world deployments. Rather than pursuing universal solutions, we argue that the field should shift to diagnosis-driven tension management, in which deployment-specific evidence guides how the learner relates to its priors throughout training, enabling both flexible and adaptive deployment. We support this position with a framework characterizing how priors reshape online optimization through three functional roles, controlled experiments demonstrating help-or-hurt reversals, cross-domain evidence from foundation model post-training to embodied intelligence, and engagement with five substantive counterarguments.

</details>


### [172] [Leaking Circuit Secrets: Gradient Leakage Attacks on Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25589)
*Rupesh Raj Karn,Johann Knechtel,Ozgur Sinanoglu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估了电路设计和硬件安全任务中图神经网络(GNNs)面临的梯度泄露攻击(GLAs)威胁，发现现有防御技术在特定场景下效果有限，强调了开发更鲁棒隐私保护方法的迫切需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着图神经网络成为电路设计和分析任务的关键工具，其安全与隐私风险亟需关注。梯度泄露攻击(GLAs)作为一种实用威胁在GNNs应用中被长期忽视，需要系统评估其风险程度和防御策略的有效性。

Method: 在标准网表基准(ISCAS'85、EPFL和TrustHub)上评估四种主流GNN架构(GraphSAGE、GCN、GIN、GAT)对GLAs的脆弱性，分析架构特征对泄露风险的影响；测试多种防御技术(差分隐私、梯度裁剪、安全聚合、量化压缩和对抗训练)的有效性及其对模型性能的影响。

Result: GLAs能够泄露敏感信息如门类型和硬件木马特征；注意力机制(GAT)加剧泄露风险，而单射聚合(GIN)具有较强鲁棒性；现有防御技术仅在特定设置下提升抗性，且可能损害模型性能。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了GNNs在电路安全应用中的重大隐私风险，强调了架构选择的重要性，并指出当前防御策略的局限性，为开发隐私保护型GNNs提供了关键洞察和方向指引。

Abstract: As graph neural networks (GNNs) become standard tools for critical tasks in circuit design and analysis, their security and privacy risks require careful attention. Here, we present the first comprehensive evaluation of gradient leakage attacks (GLAs) on GNNs in circuit-design and hardware-security tasks, a practical threat that has been largely overlooked. We assess state-of-the-art (SOTA) GNNs, including GraphSAGE, GCN, GIN, and GAT, trained on standard netlist benchmarks (ISCAS'85, EPFL, and TrustHub), for their fundamental vulnerability to GLAs. We find that GLAs can expose sensitive information, such as gate types and distinctive properties of hardware Trojans, which may assist adversaries in analyzing logic locking schemes or evading Trojan detection mechanisms. Our analysis shows that these risks are influenced by architectural features, with attention mechanisms (GAT) exacerbating leakage, while injective aggregation (GIN) provides comparatively stronger resilience. We further evaluate several SOTA defense techniques, including differential privacy, gradient clipping, secure aggregation, model compression with quantization, and adversarial training. We find that these techniques improve resilience only in specific settings and can also compromise model performance. Overall, our work provides key insights toward privacy-preserving GNNs and highlights the need for more robust and efficient defenses. We release our full methodology and artifacts.

</details>


### [173] [Learning Subset-Shared Invariances for Domain Generalization with Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25665)
*Tien-Hung Nguyen,Tien-Dat Tran,M. -Duong Nguyen,Kok-Seng Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出跨所有源域强制不变性会限制表示空间并丢弃可迁移的预测因子，提出子集共享不变性原则，通过混合专家架构实现，每个专家对齐其服务的特定域子集，路由机制组合这些子集不变组件进行预测，在DomainBed基准上表现出更好的泛化性能和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有域泛化方法通常强制所有源域间表示不变，假设预测结构全局共享。然而研究发现，跨更多域强制不变性会逐步限制可行的表示空间，丢弃那些并非普遍共享但可迁移的预测因子，导致泛化能力下降。

Method: 提出子集共享不变性原则，假设预测结构仅在域子集内稳定。采用混合专家架构实现：每个专家负责对齐其服务的特定域子集，路由机制组合这些子集不变组件进行预测，形成路由条件不变性。开发了选择性对齐、置信且平衡的路由、专家专业化多样性等训练目标。

Result: 在DomainBed基准测试中，该方法展现出改进的域外泛化性能，并且在域异质性增加时具有更强的鲁棒性。实验表明，通过域子集间部分共享结构建模不变性优于强制单一全局不变性。

Conclusion: 域泛化研究应超越强制单一全局不变性的范式，转而通过域子集间部分共享结构来建模不变性。这种细粒度的不变性建模方式能够保留更多可迁移的预测因子，提升模型泛化能力。

Abstract: Domain generalization (DG) aims to learn a model from one or more source domains that generalizes to an unseen target domain without accessing target data during training. A common approach enforces invariance of representations across all source domains, assuming predictive structure is globally shared. However, we demonstrate that enforcing invariance across more domains gradually restricts the feasible representation space, discarding transferable predictive factors that are not universally shared. To address this limitation, we propose subset-shared invariance, where predictive structure is assumed stable only within domain subsets. We implement this principle with a mixture-of-experts architecture, where each expert aligns the specific domains it serves and a routing mechanism composes subset-invariant components for prediction. This creates a routing-conditioned invariance, jointly learned with the representation. To facilitate effective decomposition, we develop training objectives that encourage selective alignment, confident and balanced routing, and diverse expert specialization. Experiments on DomainBed benchmarks demonstrate improved out-of-domain generalization and greater robustness under increasing domain heterogeneity. Our results suggest that DG should move beyond enforcing a single global invariance and instead model invariance through partially shared structure across domain subsets.

</details>


### [174] [Memory-Efficient Policy Libraries with Low-Rank Adaptation in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25700)
*Samuel Valland Lyngset,Tor Viljen Raanaas,Gard Sveipe,Eirik Møller Nilsen,Jim Torresen,Kai Olav Ellefsen,Tobias Lømo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将LoRA参数高效微调方法成功迁移到机器人多任务强化学习场景，在保持任务成功率不变的前提下，实现了20-160倍的内存压缩和90-95%的存储节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多任务机器人学习中，需要存储大量专业策略库，内存效率成为关键瓶颈。作者探索将大语言模型中成功的参数高效微调方法（LoRA）迁移至机器人强化学习领域，以降低内存占用并提升计算性能。

Method: 采用近端策略优化(PPO)算法作为基础，使用低秩适应(LoRA)技术对基线模型进行微调，针对不同任务创建专业策略库，并与全参数微调方法进行对比实验。

Result: 实验表明：根据超参数配置不同，LoRA相比全参数微调可减少20-160倍的内存占用；在部署10-50个专业策略库时实现90-95%的存储节省；同时，在选定任务上，LoRA微调与全参数微调的成功率无显著差异。

Conclusion: LoRA技术可有效应用于机器人强化学习的多任务场景，在保持性能的同时大幅降低内存需求，使得在应用机器人环境中将整个策略库存储在内存而非交换内存成为可能，这对实际部署具有决定性意义。

Abstract: When fine-tuning Large Language Models (LLMs), there has been success in minimizing both memory usage and computation with Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT), like Low Rank Adaptation (LoRA). In this article, we have explored whether this approach is transferable to the world of robotics and Reinforcement Learning (RL), allowing learning with reduced memory usage and improved computational performance. Specifically, we focused on a version of multi-task robotics, where a library of specialist policies are created. In such a library memory efficiency is especially important. We used a Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) algorithm and fine-tuned a baseline model to different tasks using LoRA. Our results demonstrate that, depending on the hyperparameters, LoRA can minimize memory usage by a factor of 20-160 compared to full fine-tuning of all layers. This implies a 90-95% storage saving when deploying a library of many (10-50) specialized policies, which can be the differentiating factor between being able to store the entire library in memory or having to use swap-memory in an applied robotics setting. At the same time, our results indicate that there is no significant difference in the success-rate between full fine-tuning and LoRA fine-tuning for the selected tasks.

</details>


### [175] [Black-Box Assisted Regression: Phase Transitions and Minimax Optimality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25743)
*Yan Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究黑盒辅助非参数回归问题，发现存在临界半径δ_c(n)控制性能相变。提出的安全残差估计器通过零初始化残差头和留出验证选择机制避免负迁移，在理论与实验中均验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型常作为固定黑盒预测器用于下游任务，但其预测可能存在偏差且盲目信任不安全。在有限标注数据场景下，需要在利用预训练模型优势的同时保证安全性（即避免性能比单独使用黑盒预测器更差）。

Method: 提出安全残差估计器（Safe Residual Estimator），核心包括：(1)学习围绕固定预测器f_0的修正残差；(2)残差头零初始化，使初始预测器等于f_0；(3)使用留出验证选择机制，当学习到的修正不被验证数据支持时回退到f_0，从而避免负迁移。

Result: 给出了有限样本极小极大特征，发现在临界半径δ_c(n)≍n^{-β/(2β+d)}处存在相变，主导风险为min{δ², n^{-2β/(2β+d)}}。安全残差估计器在主导极小极大项上达到最优，仅需额外付出验证选择成本。合成回归实验验证了预测的相变现象，CIFAR-100（CLIP）和AG News（Qwen3-8B）实验表明该残差修正权衡在形式化平方损失回归设置之外同样有效。

Conclusion: 本研究为黑盒预测器辅助学习提供了理论保证，揭示了性能相变的关键阈值，并提出的安全残差估计器在实践中具有广泛应用价值。该工作为如何在有限标注数据下安全地利用预训练模型提供了理论框架和实用方法。

Abstract: Foundation models are often used as fixed black-box predictors for downstream tasks with limited labeled data, but their predictions may be biased and unsafe to trust blindly. We study this setting through black-box assisted nonparametric regression: a learner observes labeled samples and can query a fixed predictor $f_0$, while the target $f^*$ is close to $f_0$ in $L_2(P_X)$ up to an unknown radius $δ$. We give a finite-sample minimax characterization showing a phase transition at $δ_c(n) \asymp n^{-β/(2β+d)}$, with leading risk $\min\{δ^2, n^{-2β/(2β+d)}\}$. We then analyze a Safe Residual Estimator: it learns a correction around $f_0$, initializes the residual head at zero so the initial predictor equals $f_0$, and uses holdout selection to revert to $f_0$ when the learned correction is not supported by validation data. Here, "safe" means avoiding negative transfer, i.e., performing worse than the black-box predictor alone. The estimator matches the leading minimax term up to an additive validation-selection cost. Synthetic regression experiments verify the predicted phase transition, while CIFAR-100 with CLIP and AG News with Qwen3-8B provide practice-facing evidence that the same residual-correction tradeoff is useful beyond the formal squared-loss regression setting.

</details>


### [176] [Bridging Spherical Black-Box Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25761)
*Johannes Ackermann,Stefano Peluchetti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将多种黑盒优化方法（ES、CBO、OVI）统一到一个理论框架下，提出了混合优化器，在连续控制和语言模型合并任务上取得了优于原始方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的黑盒优化方法（如进化策略、基于共识的优化、通过积分优化等）各自独立研究，缺乏统一的理论框架和相互联系的深入理解。

Method: 提出统一理论框架，指出这些方法在两个设计选择上的差异：适应度聚合（控制锐度偏好）和共识范围（控制模态）。基于此设计混合优化器：ES-OVI混合方法控制平坦极小值偏好；CBO-OVI混合方法结合参数方法的高维效率和基于粒子方法的多模态能力。

Result: 在标准黑盒优化基准测试和高层维运动任务上验证：ES-OVI混合方法在连续控制任务中实现了性能与鲁棒性的权衡；CBO-OVI混合方法在有限评估预算下的语言模型合并任务中取得了具有竞争力的结果；混合方法优于其组成算法。

Conclusion: 该统一框架揭示了黑盒优化方法之间的本质联系，提出的混合优化器成功结合了不同方法的优势，为黑盒优化领域提供了新的理论视角和实用工具。

Abstract: When gradient information is unavailable, black-box optimization (BBO) methods provide a practical alternative. While Evolution Strategies (ES), Consensus-Based Optimization (CBO), Optimization via Integration (OVI), and related methods have each been studied independently, their connections remain underexplored. We unify these approaches within a common theoretical framework, revealing that they differ primarily in two design choices: fitness aggregation (controlling sharpness preference) and consensus scope (controlling modality). Leveraging these insights, we introduce hybrid optimizers that interpolate between existing methods. Our ES-OVI hybrid allows explicit control over the preference for flat minima, enabling a trade-off between performance and robustness in continuous control tasks. Our CBO-OVI hybrids combine the higher-dimensional efficiency of parametric methods with the multimodal capabilities of particle-based approaches, achieving competitive results on language model merging under limited evaluation budgets. We validate our methods on standard BBO benchmarks and higher-dimensional locomotion tasks, demonstrating that the hybrid methods can outperform their constituent algorithms.

</details>


### [177] [Deep Neural Networks with Ordinal Loss for Medical Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25769)
*Tal Dvora,Rotem Haba,Gonen Singer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了序数交叉熵（OCE）框架，一种用于序数回归的通用且架构无关的方法，通过在标准交叉熵中引入序数成本矩阵来考虑误分类严重程度，在基准数据集上实现了更低的预测误差成本和更好的校准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医学应用等预测问题中，目标标签具有内在的序数结构，类别顺序反映临床上有意义的严重程度级别。误分类成本通常是非均匀且不对称的，远距离序数类别之间的错误比相邻类别之间的错误后果更严重，高估疾病严重程度与低估可能具有不同的临床含义。传统的多类交叉熵等损失函数将所有误分类视为等同，未能整合这种顺序信息。

Method: 提出序数交叉熵（OCE）框架，这是一种通用且架构无关的方法。该方法扩展了标准交叉熵公式，通过序数成本矩阵考虑误分类严重程度，同时保留了传统损失的概率解释和优化优势。作者还提供了OCE梯度行为的理论分析。

Result: 理论分析表明OCE产生更平滑的优化动态和改进的序数一致性。在基准数据集上的实验表明，与现有的最先进序数方法相比，该方法实现了更低的预测误差成本和更好的校准性能。

Conclusion: OCE框架为深度神经网络中的序数回归问题提供了一个简单而有效的解决方案，成功地将序数信息整合到损失函数中，同时保持了概率解释和优化优势。

Abstract: In many prediction problems in medical applications, target labels exhibit an inherent ordinal structure, where class ordering reflects clinically meaningful severity levels. The cost associated with misclassification is often non-uniform and asymmetric, as errors between distant ordinal categories may have substantially more severe consequences than errors between adjacent ones, and overestimating disease severity may have different clinical implications than underestimating it. Traditional loss functions such as multi-class cross-entropy treat all misclassifications equally and fail to incorporate this ordering information. Recent advances in ordinal regression aim to address this limitation by integrating rank-based structures into deep learning models. In this work, we introduce the \textbf{Ordinal Cross-Entropy (OCE)} framework, a general and architecture-independent approach for learning from ordinal data. The proposed method extends the standard cross-entropy formulation to account for misclassification severity through an ordinal cost matrix while preserving the probabilistic interpretation and optimization benefits of the conventional loss. We provide a theoretical analysis of the OCE gradient behavior and show that it yields smoother optimization dynamics and improved ordinal consistency. Experiments on benchmark datasets show that our method achieves lower prediction error costs and better calibration compared to existing state-of-the-art ordinal approaches, establishing OCE as a simple yet effective solution for ordinal regression in deep neural networks.

</details>


### [178] [Re-mixing Embeddings for Patient Augmentation in Data Scarce Multiple Instance Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25770)
*Muhammed Furkan Dasdelen,Fatih Ozlugedik,Anastasia Litinetskaya,Nassir Navab,Carsten Marr,Ario Sadafi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于统计的病人增强方法，通过学习疾病特异性的“配方”（实例在聚类中的分布），在嵌入空间生成逼真的病人数据。该方法解决了医学MIL任务中数据稀缺问题，特别是在罕见病、缺失类别和小样本场景下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学多实例学习(MIL)中数据稀缺是主要瓶颈，特别是对于罕见疾病或昂贵模态的数据获取困难。现有数据增强方法存在局限性：需要所有类别的样本，且无法有效生成新病人数据。需要一种能够利用已有统计数据生成逼真病人样本的方法，以缓解数据稀缺问题。

Method: 1. 使用高斯混合模型(GMM)对池化的实例嵌入进行概率聚类；
2. 学习疾病特异性的"配方"——即实例在无监督聚类中的统计分布；
3. 基于学习的配方，通过从聚类中采样嵌入生成新病人；
4. 与现有方法不同，该方法可通过重新混合池化嵌入离线生成病人，无需所有类别样本；
5. 基于不确定性量化进一步筛选生成的病人以提升MIL性能。

Result: 在三种临床相关的稀缺场景下进行评估：(i)跨数据集迁移（缺失"健康"类别时使用外部队列统计生成）；(ii)低数据机制（类别样本极少）；(iii)小队列非图像任务（单细胞RNA测序和流式细胞术）。实验表明该方法在所有场景下均优于基线，通常优于其他包混合策略。值得注意的是，在缺失类别场景中达到了与完整数据集训练相当的性能。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了医学MIL中的数据稀缺问题，特别是在缺失类别、罕见病诊断和隐私保护的病人增强方面展现出巨大潜力。通过统计驱动的嵌入空间增强，实现了无需所有类别样本即可生成逼真病人数据，为罕见疾病诊断和隐私保护的数据增强提供了新思路。

Abstract: Data scarcity is a major bottleneck in medical Multiple Instance Learning (MIL), especially for rare diseases or expensive modalities. We introduce a statistically grounded patient augmentation approach that generates realistic patients directly in embedding space. Using Gaussian Mixture Models as a probabilistic clustering approach on pooled instance embeddings from all patients, our method learns disease-specific "recipes"-statistical distributions of instances across unsupervised clusters. New patients are then generated by sampling embeddings from clusters based on learned recipes. Unlike existing methods that require examples from all categories, our method can generate patients offline by re-mixing pooled embeddings. Generated patients are further selected based on uncertainty quantification to improve MIL performance. We evaluate our method across three clinically relevant scarcity scenarios: (i) cross-dataset transfer, where an entirely missing "healthy" class is generated using statistics from an external cohort; (ii) low-data regimes, where class sizes are extremely limited; and (iii) small-cohort non-image tasks, including single-cell RNA-seq and flow cytometry. Across all experiments, our method improves performance over baseline, often outperforming other bag-mixing strategies. Notably, in the missing-class scenario, a performance comparable to full-dataset training is achieved, demonstrating its potential for rare disease diagnostic and privacy-preserving patient augmentation. The code is available at https://github.com/marrlab/RECIPE

</details>


### [179] [ROAD-VLA: Robust Online Adaptation via Self-Distillation for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25800)
*Kejing Wang,Toan Nguyen,Minh Hoang Nguyen,Simon Khan,Flora D. Salim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ROAD-VLA是一种基于优势引导的自蒸馏框架，通过在动作空间构建近端教师，将稀疏奖励转化为密集的token级监督信号，实现了视觉-语言-动作模型的在线适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作(VLA)模型的在线适应面临挑战：稀疏奖励为高维自回归动作策略提供弱监督；基于文本的特权教师（如演示、检索经验或高层计划）存在模态差距，无法有效指导低层机器人动作。

Method: 提出ROAD-VLA框架：(1)在动作空间中通过对动作token的logits施加校准的优势估计扰动来构建近端教师；(2)将稀疏奖励转化为密集的token级监督，同时保持教师策略接近当前策略；(3)推导出在校准优势和精确教师匹配条件下的策略改进下界。

Result: 在7个机器人操作环境（包含分布内和分布外偏移）中，ROAD-VLA在几乎所有设置下均优于PPO算法，展示了稳健的在线VLA适应能力。

Conclusion: ROAD-VLA成功解决了VLA模型在线适应中稀疏监督的难题，证明了在动作空间进行自蒸馏比基于文本的教师更有效，为机器人学习提供了一种可靠的在线适应方法。

Abstract: Effective online adaptation of vision-language-action (VLA) models remains challenging, as sparse rewards provide weak supervision for high-dimensional autoregressive action policies. Although self-distillation can in principle provide denser training signals, we find that text-based privileged teachers conditioned on demonstrations, retrieved experiences, or high-level plans are ineffective for VLA adaptation, exposing a modality gap between symbolic guidance and low-level robot actions. We propose ROAD-VLA, an advantage-guided self-distillation framework that constructs a proximal teacher directly in action space by perturbing action-token logits with calibrated advantage estimates. This converts sparse rewards into dense token-level supervision while keeping the teacher close to the current policy. We further derive a policy-improvement lower bound under calibrated advantages and accurate teacher matching. Across seven robotic manipulation environments with in-distribution and out-of-distribution shifts, ROADVLA outperforms PPO in nearly all settings, demonstrating robust online VLA adaptation.

</details>


### [180] [An Analysis of Posterior Collapse, Parameterization and Initialization in Variational Deep Gaussian Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25882)
*Francisco Javier Sáez-Maldonado,Juan Maroñas,Daniel Hernández-Lobato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了深度高斯过程(DGPs)中的后验坍塌问题，发现线性先验均值函数的作用机制是改善优化问题的初始化条件而非避免非单射问题，提出了零先验均值的替代初始化方法来防止后验坍塌，并解释了白化参数化为何能提供更稳定的收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度高斯过程(DGPs)的变分推断(VI)存在后验坍塌问题，即VI收敛到与先验匹配的变分后验，导致将数据解释为噪声。虽然线性先验均值函数被广泛使用来缓解此问题，但其真正作用机制尚不清楚，且该约束限制了基于建模假设选择先验的自由度。

Method: (1) 分析后验坍塌与DSVI算法及线性先验均值函数的关联；(2) 研究三种常见DGP参数化方法；(3) 提出模仿线性先验均值初始化效果的零先验均值替代初始化策略；(4) 理论分析白化参数化的稳定性优势。

Result: (1) 线性先验均值的益处源于改善初始化时的优化问题条件数，而非之前认为的避免深度DGP中的非单射问题；(2) 白化参数化通过改善条件数提供更稳定收敛并有助于避免后验坍塌；(3) 并非所有参数化方法都能从线性先验均值中获益。

Conclusion: 提出的替代初始化方法成功防止了后验坍塌、提高了训练稳定性，实现了与线性先验均值DGP相当甚至更优的性能，同时避免了强加优化驱动的先验约束，允许基于建模假设而非优化便利性来选择先验。

Abstract: DGPs are probabilistic models with remarkable prediction performance that concatenate GPs across several layers. Exact inference in DGPs is intractable, and variational inference is often used to approximate the posterior with a parametric distribution tuned by minimizing the Kullback-Leibler divergence. Moreover, finding a good VI approximation is challenging. In particular, a problem of VI is posterior collapse, where VI converges to a variational posterior that matches the prior. In variational DGPs, this implies explaining the data as noise. This work studies posterior collapse in DGPs and identifies its connection to the DSVI algorithm and the widely used linear prior mean function employed in all but the last layer. We show that the benefit of the linear prior mean does not arise from avoiding the non-injective pathology in very deep DGPs, as previously believed, but from improving the conditioning of the optimization problem at initialization. Thus, we propose an alternative initialization of a zero prior mean DGP that mimics a DGP with a linear prior mean at initialization. This enables successful training of DGPs without imposing optimization-driven constraints on the prior, allowing to choose the prior based on modeling assumptions rather than optimization convenience. Our analysis considers three common parameterizations of DGPs and shows that not all of them benefit from a linear prior mean. We also explain why a whitened parameterization of the \DGP provides more stable convergence, something often assumed from experience, but lacking a rigorous analysis. Furthermore, we show that this stability is also beneficial to avoid the posterior collapse problem. Extensive experiments validate our findings: the proposed initialization prevents posterior collapse, improves stability, and achieves performance comparable to (and sometimes better than) DGPs with a linear prior mean.

</details>


### [181] [Variational Autoencoder Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25900)
*Gananath R*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将变分自编码器（VAE）作为一种神经网络层进行集成，并提出了配套的新训练策略，系统分析了该方法的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管变分自编码器（VAE）已被广泛研究和应用超过十年，但现有工作通常将其作为独立模型使用。本研究旨在突破这一局限，探索将VAE作为神经网络层进行集成的方法，从而扩展其在深度学习架构中的应用灵活性。

Method: 提出了一种将变分自编码器（VAE）集成为神经网络层的新颖方法，并针对包含此类层的模型设计了专门的训练策略，以保证模型的有效训练与收敛。

Result: 对所提出的集成方法和训练策略进行了全面、系统的性能分析，验证了将VAE作为层嵌入神经网络架构中的可行性与效果。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了将VAE作为神经网络层集成的可行性，为深度学习模型的设计提供了新的模块化思路和方法论支持。

Abstract: Variational Autoencoders (VAEs) belong to a family of autoencoders with probabilistic properties, making them well suited for generating data by producing a smooth and continuous latent space. Despite being introduced over a decade ago, the method continues to be widely adopted in both research and industry for diverse applications. While VAEs are typically used as standalone models, this paper introduces a novel approach to integrate them as a neural network layer. Furthermore, a new training strategy is proposed for models incorporating these layers, and their performance is thoroughly analyzed.

</details>


### [182] [$\text{DT}^2$: Decision-Targeted Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25923)
*Harry Amad,Mihaela van der Schaar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DT²方法，通过决策导向的训练范式解决传统数字孪生模型在策略排序任务上的局限性，显著提升策略选择的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于机器学习的数字孪生(DT)模型通常通过最小化单步转移误差进行训练，但本文证明在模型容量受限时，这种方法会产生次优模型，无法有效根据奖励函数对策略集合进行正确排序。即使在使用高表达能力模型类别的情况下，这一问题在实证中也同样存在。

Method: 提出DT²（决策导向的数字孪生训练范式），包含两个核心步骤：(1)使用拟合Q评估从离线数据中估计候选策略的价值，作为代理真值；(2)训练DT生成轨迹，通过架构无关的损失函数保持从这些代理真值得出的成对策略排序关系。

Result: 在多种设置和架构上的实验表明，DT²相比传统DT训练方法能够持续改进策略排序性能，在策略选择过程中降低决策遗憾。该方法对训练过程中出现的策略和未见过的策略均有效，同时保持良好的原始仿真保真度。

Conclusion: DT²成功解决了传统数字孪生训练方法在决策支持场景下的局限性，证明了针对特定用例优化训练目标的重要性。该方法为数字孪生技术在策略评估和选择等决策任务中的应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: A digital twin (DT) is a virtual model of a real-world system that can assist decision-making by simulating scenarios induced by different policies. However, typical machine learning-based DTs do not optimise for this use case. We prove that, when model capacity is limited, training DTs to minimise one-step transition errors can produce suboptimal models for ranking sets of policies according to a reward function. We further show that this holds empirically, even with expressive model classes. To address this, we introduce $\text{DT}^2$, a decision-targeted DT training paradigm. Firstly, $\text{DT}^2$ uses fitted Q-evaluation to estimate values of candidate policies from offline data. A DT is then trained to generate rollouts that preserve pairwise policy rankings derived from these proxy ground-truth values with an architecture-agnostic loss function. We empirically demonstrate the efficacy of our method across a range of settings and architectures. $\text{DT}^2$ consistently improves policy ranking and reduces decision regret during policy selection relative to conventional DT training, both for policies used during training and for unseen policies, while maintaining a good level of raw simulation fidelity.

</details>


### [183] [Improving Neural Network Training by Decoupling the Magnitude and Direction of Weight Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25971)
*Alexander Hägele,Alejandro Hernández-Cano,Atli Kosson,Martin Jaggi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为幅度-方向(MD)解耦的优化器改进方法，将权重矩阵分解为固定范数的方向和可学习的幅度，通过独立的学习率分别更新，消除了对权重衰减和预热的需求，并在Adam和Muon优化器上均取得了性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代神经网络训练中，优化器（如Adam和Muon）将权重矩阵作为单一对象处理。然而，每个权重矩阵实际上包含两个不同的量——幅度和方向。现有优化器将两者的动态耦合在一起：方向更新依赖于当前幅度，而幅度变化是方向学习的副产品，这导致两者都无法直接被学习率控制。虽然权重衰减和预热等技巧可以间接调节这种耦合，但效果有限；其他方法（如固定范数约束）则放弃了可学习的幅度，将尺度控制完全交给归一化层。

Method: 提出幅度-方向(MD)解耦方法：将每个权重因子化为超球面上的固定范数方向和可学习的逐行/逐列幅度增益，使用独立的学习率分别更新，同时模型仍然看到单个融合的权重张量。该方法与基础优化器无关，可直接应用于Adam和Muon等优化器。

Result: MD解耦方法在Adam和Muon优化器上均优于精心调参的基线模型；最优学习率可以在不同模型宽度间迁移而无需重新调参；在大规模混合专家(MoE)模型上仍然有效；消除了对权重衰减和预热的需求。

Conclusion: 将幅度和方向作为独立控制的量，可以产生更可预测的训练动态，为现代优化器提供了一个简单、广泛适用的改进方案，使训练更加稳定和高效。

Abstract: Modern neural network training relies on optimizers such as Adam and Muon which act on each weight matrix as a single object. Yet every weight matrix carries two distinct quantities -- a \emph{magnitude} and a \emph{direction} -- and all optimizers stepping in the matrix as a whole couple their dynamics: the directional change from an update depends on the current magnitude, while the magnitude drifts as a byproduct of learning the direction, so neither is governed directly by the learning rate. Typical training therefore leans on surrounding recipes such as weight decay and warmup to keep learning stable at scale, though these regulate the coupling only indirectly; other recent methods instead constrain the weight to a fixed-norm sphere, but add no learnable magnitude, leaving scale control to normalization layers alone. We propose \emph{Magnitude--Direction (MD) Decoupling}, an optimizer modification that factorizes each weight into a fixed-norm direction on a hypersphere and learnable per-row and per-column magnitude gains, updated at separate learning rates, all while the model still sees a single fused weight tensor. The method is agnostic to the base optimizer and removes the need for weight decay and warmup. Across both Adam and Muon, MD Decoupling improves on well-tuned baselines, transfers the optimal LR across model width without retuning, and continues to help at scale on large Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models. Treating magnitude and direction as separately controlled quantities thus yields more predictable training dynamics and a simple, broadly applicable improvement to modern optimizers.

</details>


### [184] [Tensorion: A Tensor-Aware Generalization of the Muon Optimizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25975)
*Vladimir Bogachev,Vladimir Aletov,Alexander Molozhavenko,Sergei Kudriashov,Maxim Rakhuba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Tensorion优化器，将Muon的约束优化视角从矩阵扩展到高阶张量，通过在张量范数球上进行线性最小化，在张量相关的计算机视觉任务中表现出比Adam等基线更优的收敛性和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常见的一阶优化器（如Adam）将参数块视为非结构化向量，忽略了现代机器学习模型中存在的多线性权重结构。虽然Muon等工作表明利用矩阵结构可以改善优化动态，但缺乏针对高阶张量结构的优化方法。

Method: 基于约束优化视角，构建张量感知优化器Tensorion。核心是设计一个在张量范数球上的线性最小化预言机（LMO），范数的选择需平衡两个目标：紧致界定张量谱范数，同时保持LMO的可计算性。该方法通过对自适应选择的展开矩阵进行操作来实现计算，且在限制为二阶张量（即矩阵）时精确恢复Muon方法。

Result: 在基于张量的计算机视觉问题上的实验表明，与Adam和现有张量感知基线相比，Tensorion在评估设置中能够提供改进的收敛行为和更稳定的梯度更新。

Conclusion: Tensorion成功将矩阵感知优化扩展到高阶张量，通过合理的范数选择和LMO设计实现了理论可解性和实践有效性的平衡，为利用张量结构信息提供了新的优化范式。

Abstract: Common first-order optimizers, such as Adam, implicitly treat each parameter block as an unstructured vector, which disregards the multilinear weight structure present in many modern machine learning models. Recent work has shown that exploiting matrix structure can improve optimization dynamics. A notable example is Muon, which performs steepest descent under the spectral norm constraint. We take the next step and introduce Tensorion, a tensor-aware optimizer that extends Muon's constrained optimization perspective from matrices to higher-order tensors. Tensorion is built around a linear minimization oracle (LMO) over a tensor norm ball. The norm is carefully chosen to balance two objectives: tightly bounding the tensor spectral norm, while still keeping the LMO tractable. This LMO becomes computable because it reduces to operations on adaptively selected unfolding matrices. Notably, when restricted to order-2 tensors (i.e., matrices), Tensorion recovers Muon exactly. Experiments on tensor-based computer vision problems suggest that Tensorion can offer improved convergence behavior and more stable gradient updates compared with Adam-based and existing tensor-aware baselines in the evaluated settings.

</details>


### [185] [The Inference-Compute Frontier and a Latency-Efficient Architecture for Limit Order Book Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25986)
*C. Evans Hedges*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现在限价订单簿预测中存在类似缩放定律的计算-预测性能前沿，并提出了低延迟高性能模型FastBiNLOB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索限价订单簿预测任务中是否存在类似缩放定律的计算与预测性能之间的关系，并针对延迟与计算效率的差距设计更优的模型架构。

Method: 在FI-2010数据集上使用从决策树到神经LOB架构的多种模型，分析预测损失与结构前向工作的幂律关系，并基于延迟分析设计FastBiNLOB模型。

Result: 预测损失与计算量呈幂律关系，MLPLOB架构在高计算区域外推拟合达R²=0.941；FastBiNLOB在五种子实验中以更低延迟达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 限价订单簿预测存在计算-性能缩放定律，延迟不等于噪声计算，FastBiNLOB验证了低延迟高性能的可行性。

Abstract: We study whether a scaling-law-style inference-compute frontier appears in limit order book prediction. Using FI-2010 and a suite of models ranging from small decision trees to neural LOB architectures, we find that the realized empirical frontier of predictive loss versus structural forward work is well summarized by a power law. In particular, with MLPLOB held out as an architecture family, a power-law fit to the low- and mid-compute non-MLPLOB frontier extrapolates across multiple orders of magnitude and attains $R^2=0.941$ on the excluded high-compute MLPLOB target frontier.
  A similar exercise in latency space gives substantially weaker results, showing that latency is not merely noisy compute. We use this gap to motivate FastBiNLOB, a dense axis-separable LOB mixer built from hardware-friendly temporal and feature mixing operations. In a five-seed experiment, FastBiNLOB exceeds the published $y_{10}$ and $y_{100}$ macro-F1 targets at notably lower latency than existing published SOTA architectures.

</details>


### [186] [Is Variational Monte Carlo Robust? Sharp Moment Thresholds and Heavy-tailed Stochastic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26009)
*Philipp Grohs,Davide Nobile*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了VMC算法中随机优化问题的内在特性由波函数的节点几何决定，证明了关键估计量具有重尾性质，并提出了更鲁棒的PS-Clip-VMC变体算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分蒙特卡罗(VMC)是电子结构理论中的核心算法，在现代神经网络方法如FermiNet中获得了新的重要性。然而，VMC通过随机优化最小化Rayleigh商时，其背后的数学性质尚未被充分理解，特别是波函数节点几何对优化过程的影响。理解这些内在性质对于改进算法的鲁棒性和收敛性至关重要。

Method: 本文从理论上分析了VMC中随机优化问题的数学性质，建立了波函数节点集性质与局部能量、梯度估计量可积性之间的联系。针对Slater-Jastrow波函数等实际相关的参数化函数类，证明了估计量的重尾性质。对于一般解析参数化，推导了弱矩界限并确定了不同节点结构下的可积性阈值。在此基础上，提出了PS-Clip-VMC算法，通过对局部能量和梯度随机变量进行裁剪来提高鲁棒性。

Result: 证明了在实际相关的参数化函数类中，VMC的估计量通常具有重尾分布且不存在高阶矩；对于一般解析参数化，获得了弱矩界限并识别了精确的低矩区域；理论证明了PS-Clip-VMC在VMC的弱矩区域中能够以期望和高概率收敛；在训练FermiNet处理多达18个电子的原子系统的初步实验中，PS-Clip-VMC表现出比标准方法显著更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文建立了VMC算法中节点几何与估计量统计性质之间的深刻联系，揭示了标准VMC方法面临的重尾挑战，并提出了一种理论上可靠、实践中有效的解决方案。这为理解和改进量子蒙特卡罗方法提供了新的理论基础和实践工具。

Abstract: Variational Monte Carlo (VMC) is a central algorithm in electronic structure theory and has gained renewed importance through modern neural-network ansätze such as FermiNet. At its core, VMC seeks ground states by minimizing the Rayleigh quotient by stochastic optimization. In this work, we show that the resulting stochastic optimization problem is intrinsically governed by the nodal geometry of the underlying wave function. More precisely, we establish that properties of the nodal set determine the integrability of the local energy and gradient estimators that drive VMC. For broad and practically relevant ansatz classes, including Slater-Jastrow wave functions with variable-exponent Slater-type orbitals, we prove that these estimators are generically heavy-tailed and fail to admit higher moments. At the same time, for general analytic ansätze, we prove weak moment bounds for the relevant estimators and identify precise low-moment regimes, showing how generic and degenerate nodal structures lead to different integrability thresholds. Building on this analysis, we introduce a new robust variant of VMC $\unicode{x2013}$ coined PS-Clip-VMC $\unicode{x2013}$ which is based on clipping both the local energy and the gradient random variable. We prove that PS-Clip-VMC converges both in expectation and with high probability in the weak moment regime of VMC. Preliminary experiments for training FermiNet on Atoms with up to 18 electrons suggest that PS-Clip-VMC is significantly more robust than standard methods.

</details>


### [187] [RevengeBench: Reverse Engineering Code-Space Policies from Behavioral Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26094)
*Babak Rahmani,Sebastian Dziadzio,Joschka Strüber,Sergio Hernández-Gutiérrez,Matthias Bethge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了RevengeBench基准，用于研究如何通过行为轨迹重构智能体的决策程序代码。实验表明，12个前沿大语言模型在恢复质量上差异显著（初始距离缩短34%-72%），重构的策略能带来竞争优势，验证了通过代码空间进行行为恢复的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在科学研究历史中，研究者通常只能从外部行为推断隐藏机制，这是一个逆问题。本文提出了一个计算类比问题：给定智能体在游戏环境中的行为轨迹，学习者能否重构底层的决策程序为可执行代码？这种重构在能够设计对照实验时会改善多少？这涉及到对手建模、策略可解释性等核心问题。

Method: 1. 构建RevengeBench基准，包含75个LLM生成的、Elo校准的策略，覆盖五个游戏环境，数据来源于CodeClash锦标赛轨迹
2. 学习者观察隐藏目标策略与采样对手的对战，设计行为探针（自定义对手策略）来诱发信息丰富的行为
3. 提交可执行的假设程序，使用连续动作距离指标进行评估
4. 在下游玩家对战锦标赛中验证恢复代码的信息价值

Result: 1. 在12个前沿LLM中，恢复质量差异显著，初始距离缩短34%至72%
2. 重构策略产生可测量的竞争优势，尤其对较弱的模型效果更明显
3. 较弱的模型通常难以设计有效的反策略，但重构策略能弥补这一劣势
4. 恢复的代码在下游锦标赛中携带信息信号

Conclusion: 该基准将程序化策略的行为恢复定位为代码空间中可处理的逆问题，为对手建模、策略可解释性以及从观察中推断潜在机制等更广泛的问题开辟了道路。这验证了通过设计对照实验可以显著提升从行为轨迹重构决策程序的能力。

Abstract: For most of scientific history, researchers studying behavior could only infer hidden mechanisms from outward actions: an inverse problem that becomes more tractable when observation is augmented by targeted intervention. We pose a computational analogue: given only behavioral traces of an agent in a game environment, can a learner reconstruct the underlying decision program as executable code, and how much does this reconstruction improve with the ability to design controlled experiments? We introduce RevengeBench, a benchmark of 75 LLM generated, Elo-calibrated policies across five game environments, drawn from CodeClash tournament trajectories. The learner observes the hidden target policy play against sampled opponents and designs behavioral probes in the form of custom opponent policies that elicit informative behavior. It then submits an executable hypothesis, which is evaluated using continuous action-distance metrics. We further validate that recovered code carries informative signal in downstream player-versus-player tournaments. Across twelve frontier LLMs, recovery quality varies substantially (34 to 72% of initial distance closed), with reconstructed policies yielding measurable competitive advantage, particularly for weaker models that otherwise struggle to design effective counter-strategies. Our benchmark positions behavioral recovery of programmatic policies as a tractable inverse problem in code-space, opening a path to opponent modeling, policy interpretability, and the broader question of inferring latent mechanisms from observations.

</details>
